# Decisions to review

The user asked (2026-11-24) to keep building without stopping for questions. Every important decision made on the user's behalf is logged here, with the option picked and the alternatives, so it can be reviewed later. When a decision is reviewed, mark it **Kept** or **Changed: …** and update the spec it links.

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Open | Decided by the agents, not yet reviewed by the user |
| Kept | The user reviewed it and agreed |
| Changed | The user picked something else (the change still needs building) |

| M3-1 | Only the host changes the payer | Host only | The payer can hand off | Open |
| M3-2 | Changing the payer clears "I paid" marks, after a confirm (no confirm if there are no marks) | Clear + confirm | Keep marks | Open |
| M3-3 | "I paid" works even when the payer has no Venmo | Allowed | Block until they add Venmo | Open |
| M3-4 | The host can pick the payer before every item is claimed, once 2+ people have joined | Allowed | Only once every item is claimed | Kept (user, 2026-11-25): the host can set the payer anytime. The only limit is that someone else must be on the bill to pick |
| M3-5 | Venmo link format | ADR 0007's format; the backup format is used if the $0.01 phone check fails | n/a | Open (V1 pay and request links confirmed on iPhone 2026-11-25; Android and desktop pending) |

## 2026-11-24 · Several "Total" lines (spec: docs/specs/2026-11-24-first-total-as-subtotal.md)
- Decided by the user: guard (no Subtotal line, no tax before the first Total); 3+ distinct Total amounts → rule off; a discount between the Totals counts as a discount on the total; "VAT included" lines between the Totals are not added to tax.

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FT-A3-1 | Bill form with no printed subtotal | Leave Subtotal blank and treat the items as the subtotal for the checks; never show "Enter the subtotal…"; the pill can say "Adds up" without a subtotal (Amendment A3, Task 2) | (A) Auto-fill Subtotal from the items; (C) keep today's hint and "Not checked yet" | The host can't fix a subtotal the receipt doesn't print. The total check already runs on items + extras. An auto-filled value goes stale when an item is edited and causes false alarms | Open |
| FT-A3-2 | Mismatch wording when the subtotal is blank | "The items, tax, fees and tip don't add up to the total." | Keep "The subtotal, tax, fees and tip…" | The host never entered a subtotal, so the old wording points at a field they didn't touch | Open |
| FT-A3-3 | "VAT included" shown on the receipt but Tax left blank | No change | Show a note such as "VAT is already in the total" | Rare; if the host types it in, the "Off by" banner explains the problem. Revisit if a real receipt shows it | Open |
| FT-A3-4 | M1a criterion 28 (empty form shows the missing-subtotal hint) | Changed to "not shown" | Keep the old criterion | Follows FT-A3-1 | Open |
- Implementer decisions (Task 1), open for review:
  - "Included" tax is marked when the row is classified: a tax row whose label matches the existing `INCLUDES_RE` ("VAT included", "incl. tax") carries `included: true`. Only the several-Total rule looks at it; tax rows before the first Total are counted exactly as before, and an included-tax row before the first Total still turns the rule off (R3).
  - First Total larger than the last with no discount between them (e.g. Total 55.00 / Total 50.00): the rule still fires. Total, tax and tip are the same as today; the host sees "subtotal doesn't add up" instead of "missing subtotal". Alternative, per A2's "if unsure, don't fire": only fire when first < last or a discount sits between them. Not done, to keep the rule to R1–R3.
  - With two or more tip lines, the tip choice (`chooseTip`) reconciles against the subtotal once the rule sets it, so in a firing case with several tip lines it can pick a different tip than today. No test or eval receipt shows a difference; noted because A2 says tip must never get worse.

## 2026-11-24 · Several "Total" lines, follow-up (spec Amendment A4)
- Decided by the main session (reason: A2, "if unsure, don't fire; tip must never get worse"): the rule also needs the first Total below the last, or above it with a discount between them; the tip is chosen as if the rule hadn't fired; an included-tax row before the first Total stays as is (counted as tax, rule off).

## 2026-11-24 · Blank subtotal in the bill form, implementer decisions (spec: docs/specs/2026-11-24-first-total-as-subtotal.md, Amendment A3, Task 2)
- The copy is chosen by a pure helper, `reasonCopy(ev, reason)` in `lib/billDraft.ts`, plus the constant `NO_SUBTOTAL_MISMATCH_COPY`; `BillForm` calls `reasonCopy` for each banner line. Picked over a new field on `DraftEvaluation` so the evaluation's shape stays the same for its other users (`billPayload`, `editBill`). Open.
- An invalid subtotal (e.g. `abc`) counts as blank in the check (`receipt.subtotalCents` is null), so if the banner shows alongside the field error it uses the items wording. That matches what the check actually compared. Open.
- `lib/billPayload.test.ts:311` (criterion 68) asserts `status 'incomplete'` with the comment "only the subtotal is missing". Under A3-1 that draft is now `adds_up`, so the test fails. A3-9 says that file passes without edits, and it was outside Task 2's files, so it was not edited. Proposed fix (needs the main session's OK): change the line to `toBe('adds_up')` with the comment "a blank subtotal isn't asked for (A3)". Open.

## 2026-11-24 · M3 Settle up screens (brief: docs/specs/2026-11-24-m3-settle-ux.md; spec: docs/specs/2026-11-24-m3-settle-venmo.md, Amendment A1)
- Decided by the PM after the staff engineer's review, because the user asked not to block on questions. Q1–Q10 (the user's answers) are not repeated.

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| M3-UX-1 | How "Did you pay?" appears after Venmo | Tapping Pay switches the card to "Did you pay Hana?" with "I paid" as the primary. No detection of returning to the app | (A) Detect the return (`AppState` / page visibility) and show a dismissible card; (B) the spec's original layout, with Pay and "I paid" side by side from the start | Return detection gives false prompts (iOS goes `inactive` for Control Center and Face ID), can't see a blocked web popup, and is lost if Safari reloads or the OS kills the app. Switching on the tap never misfires | Open; **amended 2026-11-27 by Sent via Venmo (SV-1, SV-ENG-13, ADR 0024):** the Pay tap now records "Sent via Venmo" itself on phones and mobile web; the "Did you send…?" card shows only as the fallback (unknown outcome, desktop web, closed mobile tab) |
| M3-UX-2 | Label of the secondary before Pay | "I already paid" (ghost) | "I paid" (the spec) | Next to "Pay", a plain "I paid" invites marking before paying | Superseded by SV-14 (2026-11-26): "Paid another way" (method `other`), next to the Venmo / Cash choice |
| M3-UX-3 | Undoing someone else's "I paid" | Inline confirm. Your own undo is one tap | No confirm (the spec); confirm every undo | Only the person can restore their own mark, so a stray tap by the host or payer is costly. Your own mis-tap is fixed in one tap | Open |
| M3-UX-4 | Payer-change confirm when there are 0 marks | Confirm when the bill is complete ("People may already be paying Hana."). No confirm while it's incomplete | Never confirm at 0 marks (M3-2 as written); always confirm | Once the bill is complete, someone may be in Venmo paying the old payer even though nobody has marked yet. Before that, nobody can see a Pay button. Narrows M3-2 | Open |
| M3-UX-5 | Telling others the payer changed | A warning banner: "Sam is now collecting for this bill." When their mark was cleared or they had just opened Venmo, it adds "If you already paid Hana, sort it out with them." No button marks a payment to the old payer | No notice (the spec) | Otherwise a person who paid Hana silently sees "You owe Sam" | Open |
| M3-UX-6 | Telling a person their mark was undone by someone else | Banner: "Your "I paid" was undone. Check Activity to see who." | Name who did it (needs Activity data on this screen); no notice | Bill data doesn't say who undid it; Activity does | Open |
| M3-UX-7 | Share changed after a mark | Copy only: "Your share is now $16.20. Sort out the $1.10 with Hana." / "Hana owes you $1.10 back." (also for $0 and negative). Undo stays | Pay the difference in Venmo and replace the mark (needs a DB change to `mark_paid` or a new function, plus an Activity sentence) | Smallest version of the user's Q3 A. The fuller flow adds a money path and a DB change; revisit if people hit it | Superseded by SE-4, SE-5, SE-10 (2026-11-25, ADR 0021): the user asked for the fuller flow. Differences now have Pay / Request / "I paid the difference" / "I paid it back" buttons through `settle_difference`; the "Sort out the $1.10" copy is gone |
| M3-UX-8 | Where "Settle up" goes on the bill screen | Sticky footer: hidden with 1 participant, a secondary button plus "N items left to claim" while incomplete, the primary when complete | A top-bar button next to Activity (the staff engineer's preference); always shown (the spec) | It's the bill's next step once everything is claimed, so it belongs by the person's share. A top-bar ghost button is easy to miss and looks as minor as Activity | Open |
| M3-UX-9 | The all-$0 blocked copy for guests | Guests: "Every item is $0. Ask Hana to fix the prices." The host keeps the Q10 copy plus "Edit items" | The same Q10 copy for everyone | Guests can't edit prices or change the split mode | Open |
| M3-UX-10 | A marked person on a bill that became incomplete | "Your "I paid" is saved." (no amount) | Show nothing; show the amount | Otherwise the mark looks lost. No amount, so blocked plans show no money | Open |
| M3-UX-11 | Payer and marked copy | "3 people owe you / $45.20 / 1 of 3 paid"; "You paid Hana / $15.10 / ✓ Marked paid" | The spec's "Sam and 2 others owe you $45.20 · 1 paid" and "You marked this paid ($15.10)" | Shorter lines, one fact each, no name-order question | Open |
| M3-UX-12 | Who is in the Everyone list | People who owe, plus a negative share, in a stable order with the viewer first. Not the payer, and not people with nothing owed (unless they have a mark) | Everyone; unpaid first | Fewer rows, and rows don't jump when someone pays | Open |
| M3-UX-13 | Task plan | Tasks 8, 9 and 11 replaced by Task 8 (pure `lib/settleView.ts` + `markDiff` in `lib/settle.ts`) and Task 9 (one screen task, one design pass, may land as two commits) | Three screen tasks (the spec) | The same files would have been reworked three times | Open |
| M3-UX-14 | The handle line on each platform | "@hana-1 · $15.10" on every platform; "On a computer? Send it from the Venmo app on your phone." on all web | Only on desktop web (Q8's wording) | It shows a mistyped handle before money moves, and it's the fallback for an undetectable blocked popup. Desktop detection isn't reliable | Open |
| M3-UX-15 | Error copy on the settle screen | Keep the engineer's existing `BILL_ERROR_COPY` strings (M3-8) for `marks_changed`, `payer_changed` and `not_complete`. Use a settle-specific "Couldn't save. Refresh and try again." for `invalid_bill`, whose shared copy talks about editing | New settle wording for all of them | No churn in `lib/bills.ts`. Only the string that reads wrong in this context changes | Open |
- Decided by the main session after code review round 1 (reason A2; spec Amendment A6): any tax, tip or fees row above the first Total turns the rule off; the rule's reading is dropped for the old one when it adds a totals mismatch the old one doesn't have; an included tax-sum line between the Totals is not added to tax.
- Implementer reading of the fallback (open for review): it compares "has any totals mismatch" for each reading, not the exact reasons. So if both readings are off (e.g. criterion 8d: the rule flags items vs subtotal, the old reading flags subtotal vs total), the rule's reading is kept. Comparing the exact reasons would fall back there and change criterion 8d's expected result.
- Decided by the main session after code review round 2 (reason A2; spec Amendment A7): the rule is also dropped when its subtotal-vs-total check is off by more than 1 cent and it added tax or discounts. Tax or discounts that don't add up aren't trusted.

Tested on both sides (`lib/settle.test.ts` K6, `settle_marks.test.sql` K6) | Add the same rule to `private.bill_is_complete` (keeps the two rules identical, as CLAUDE.md asks) | Marks are honor-system and move no money, and the app shows no Pay or "I paid" buttons on such a bill. User checks (need a person; not run by agents):
- Real Google sign-in on web with two accounts, then the criterion 62 flow (Pay opens a new tab at the C5 URL; "I paid" shows on the other window within seconds; Undo with confirm; payer change with confirm; Activity lines).
- Safari (macOS and iOS) popup behaviour: Pay must open Venmo in a new tab from the tap. If Safari blocks it, the "@hana-1 · $15.10" line is the fallback; record what happens.
- The phone's $0.01 Venmo link (criterion 64): open `venmoPayUrl({ username: <your handle>, cents: 1 })` on an iPhone with Venmo. Don't press Pay. Record which fields are filled for V1 and V2.
- VoiceOver on iOS: where focus lands after Pay swaps the card to "Did you pay Hana?" (brief §8; focus on the card's label is acceptable), and that "Marked paid" / "Marked not paid" are announced.
- Light and dark mode screenshots of the Settle screen (visually unverified by the agent).

## 2026-11-24 · People you've split with + Invite from contacts (spec: [docs/specs/2026-11-24-people-and-invites.md](specs/2026-11-24-people-and-invites.md))
- Decided by the PM under the "don't block" rule. The user's fixed decisions (derived list, ranking by bills together, host adds directly, DB-enforced "shared a bill", contacts on device, Profile + "Add people", placeholders and friend requests deferred) are not repeated.

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| PI-1 | Which shared bills count as "split with" (rule L1) | A bill counts once both people took part: hosted it, joined with the link, or claimed something on it. Being added alone doesn't count | (A) Any shared bill (the existing `shares_bill_with`); (B) the added person must tap "Accept" | With (A), being added to Hana's bill would let everyone else on it add you to their bills, though you never chose them. (B) is a friend request in disguise. Claiming is the natural "I was there" | Open |
| PI-2 | Search | On device over the loaded list: every typed word must start a word of the name, case- and accent-insensitive, ranking order kept | Substring match; server-side search | The list is small (≤ 200), so search is instant and offline. Word starts are how phone contact search works | Open |
| PI-3 | Adding | One tap per person ("Add"), no confirm, one person per call | Multi-select + "Add 3 people"; confirm each add | Fewest steps. A mistake is undone with "Remove" (PI-4) | Open |
| PI-4 | Host removing someone | Allowed only when they have no claims, no "I paid" mark and aren't the payer; no confirm | Remove anyone with a confirm (their claims go and shares change); no remove at all | Fixes a wrong "Alex" without ever changing anyone's money | Open |
| PI-5 | Leaving a bill | Anyone but the host, under the same conditions as PI-4. "Leave this bill" shows only then, with an inline confirm | Always shown, with an explanation when blocked; leaving drops your claims | The "I wasn't at this dinner" case. Never silently moves money | Open |
| PI-6 | Telling someone they were added | Nothing (no push yet). The bill shows in "Your bills" on the next Home load; Activity logs "Hana added Sam". Home keeps ordering by bill date | A "New" badge (needs per-user "seen" state); order Home by when you were added | Smallest; the host can still text the link. A bill added long after it was created can sit lower in Home | Superseded by BM-1 to BM-18 (bill markers and notices, 2026-11-24): an in-app card and "New" pill now tell you; Home is ordered by when the bill entered your list (BM-12) |
| PI-7 | Ranking ties | Bills together, then most recent shared bill, then name A–Z (case-insensitive, no name last), then user id. Computed only in the database | Name first; app-side sort | One place for the rule; stable order | Open |
| PI-8 | People list size | At most 200 (starting value) | No cap; paging | Bounded work per call; nobody is near it | Open |
| PI-9 | When the search box shows | Only with more than 5 people (starting value) | Always | Avoids a search box over a 2-person list | Open |
| PI-10 | Two people with the same name | Each row's caption shows "4 bills · last Nov 20"; when two names match, each also shows "@venmo" if they have one | Always show the handle; show nothing extra | The handle is already visible to co-participants and tells two Alexes apart; hidden otherwise to keep rows quiet | Open |
| PI-11 | Profile layout | One card, full list, between Venmo and Sign out; rows aren't tappable | A separate People screen linked from Profile; top 5 + "Show all" | The user asked for it on Profile; no new route. Rows have nothing to open yet | Open |
| PI-12 | Where adding happens | A new screen `/bills/[id]/people` opened by "Add people" in the bill's Invite card (host only), with "On this bill" and the addable list | A bottom sheet on the bill screen | A search field plus keyboard works better on its own screen; "Remove" lives next to the people it affects | Open |
| PI-13 | Where "From contacts" lives | A button in the Invite card next to Copy link / Share…, for every participant, iOS and Android only | Only on the Add people screen; host only | Any participant can share the link today; one home for "send the link" | Open |
| PI-14 | How the message is sent | Messages via `expo-sms` (in-app composer, prefilled); WhatsApp via `wa.me/<number>?text=` when the number has a `+` country code, else `wa.me/?text=` (pick the chat in WhatsApp) | An `sms:` URL; guessing the country code; WhatsApp only when the number is international | `expo-sms` avoids iOS/Android `sms:` body quirks; WhatsApp needs an international number and we don't guess one | Open |
| PI-15 | Message text | The existing share text "Claim what you had on SplitIt: <link>", now one constant | New wording for texts | Same words wherever the link is sent; no names or amounts | Open |
| PI-16 | Web | No "From contacts"; Profile list, Add people, Remove and Leave work as on phones | A `sms:` link on mobile web | Browsers have no contacts picker we'd use | Open |
| PI-17 | Activity names for people who left or were removed | "Hana removed someone", "Someone left" (the app can't read their name once they're off the bill) | Store a name snapshot in the event | Events hold ids only (audit-log rule); a snapshot keeps a name after the person left. Privacy call for the user | Open |
| PI-18 | Settle with an added person who claims nothing | No change to M3: they owe $0 and don't block; only unclaimed items block (Q1). "No items yet" shows next to them on Add people | A host "claim for them" tool | Q1 already decided no new tool | Open |
| PI-19 | Contacts permission | Ask for nothing if the installed picker works without permission (Task 8 checks the source); block `WRITE_CONTACTS` on Android; the iOS purpose string is set anyway ("SplitIt opens your contacts only when you pick someone to text a bill link to. Your contacts stay on your phone.") | Request contacts permission up front | Picking one contact shouldn't need access to all of them. Apple wants a purpose string whenever the contacts API is in the app *(unverified)* | Open (Task 8 verifies); Superseded by PI-N-1 (the picker needs contacts permission to read numbers). |

## 2026-11-24 · People and invites, implementer decisions (spec: [docs/specs/2026-11-24-people-and-invites.md](specs/2026-11-24-people-and-invites.md), Tasks 3, 4, 9)

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| PI-B-1 | Screen-reader label for someone with 1 shared bill | "Sam Lee, 1 bill together, on Nov 20" ("last on" only from 2 bills) | "…, last on Nov 20" for every count | Matches the caption, which drops "last" at 1 bill. "Last" on a single bill reads oddly | Open |
| PI-B-2 | Two people with no display name (both read "A friend") | Counted as the same name, so each shows "@handle" if they have one | Only compare real names | They look identical on screen, which is the case PI-10 is meant to tell apart | Open |
| PI-B-3 | What "cleaned name" means for the same-name check | Trimmed, inner spaces collapsed, case ignored. Accents count ("José" ≠ "Jose") | Also fold accents, like search | The handle is there to tell apart names that *look* the same. Names that differ by an accent already look different | Open |
| PI-B-4 | Name words for search | Split on whitespace only ("Mary-Jane" is one word, so `jane` doesn't match it) | Also split on `-`, `'` and `.` | Spec L4 says whitespace. A one-line change if hyphenated names come up | Open |
| PI-B-5 | Accent folding | NFD, then remove the combining-mark ranges U+0300–036F, 1AB0–1AFF, 1DC0–1DFF, 20D0–20FF and FE20–FE2F | `/\p{M}/gu` | Same result for Latin names, and it doesn't depend on the phone's JS engine supporting Unicode property escapes *(Hermes support not verified)* | Open |
| PI-B-6 | The 50-character query cut | Counted in characters (code points), so an emoji is never split | UTF-16 units (`slice(0, 50)`) | A split surrogate pair would be an invalid character in the query | Open |
| PI-B-7 | `listPeople` when the same person comes back twice | The whole result is `'network'` | Keep the first | The function returns each person once (L1). A repeat means a broken response, and it would give duplicate row keys on screen | Open |
| PI-B-8 | `personCaption` / `personA11yLabel` date formatter type | `(d: Date) => string`, so screens can pass `defaultTimeFormatters.date` from `lib/activityText.ts` | Take the ISO string | One formatter for Activity and People, which keeps dates consistent | Open |
| PI-B-9 | "someone" and "a friend" in the middle of the new Activity sentences | A new helper `targetName` gives "Hana added someone", "Hana added a friend" and "Hana removed you". `objectName` is unchanged | Change `objectName` itself | `objectName` keeps "Someone" capitalised in the older settle sentences ("changed who gets paid to Someone"), and their tests pin that wording. Changing it would change M3 copy outside this task | Open (the older sentences could switch later, for consistency) |
| PI-B-10 | Share… text in `SharePanel` | `Share.share({ message: inviteMessage(url) })`. The exported `SHARE_MESSAGE_PREFIX` is gone (nothing imported it) | Import `INVITE_MESSAGE_PREFIX` and add `url` | Same text. One function builds the message for Share… and contacts | Open |
| PI-B-11 | `isUuid` | Exported from `lib/bills.ts` and used by `lib/people.ts` | A second copy of the UUID pattern | One definition of the id check in the data layer | Open |

## 2026-11-24 · People and invites, database decisions (spec: [docs/specs/2026-11-24-people-and-invites.md](specs/2026-11-24-people-and-invites.md), Tasks 1–2; migrations 20261124091041, 20261124091042)

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| PI-D-1 | Shape of the L1 helper | `private.my_links()` returns `(user_id, bill_id, bill_created_at)` for the caller (auth.uid()), security invoker, no client EXECUTE; only the two security definer functions call it | A helper that takes any user id; a view | A no-argument helper can only ever answer about the caller, and the rule stays in one function body | Open |
| PI-D-2 | People-list limit | 200, the spec's starting value | Raise or lower it | No evidence yet to change it. Recorded in the migration header | Open |
| PI-D-3 | Lock in `add_participant` | `FOR SHARE` on the bill row | `FOR UPDATE` | Enough to serialize with remove/leave and set_payer (both `FOR UPDATE`), and doesn't block marks (`FOR SHARE`) | Open |
| PI-D-4 | Lock in `remove_participant` / `leave_bill` | `FOR UPDATE` on the bill row, then `FOR UPDATE` on the target's participant row, then the L3 check in a new statement | `FOR SHARE` on the bill | `FOR UPDATE` on the participant row conflicts with the claim FK's `FOR KEY SHARE`, so a simultaneous claim either makes the call refuse or fails with 23503 (set_claim maps it to `not_found`). `FOR UPDATE` on the bill row matches what the reset-payer trigger takes later, so there's no lock upgrade that could deadlock | Open (review check, criterion 21); Superseded: the lock reasoning missed a deadlock; corrected by PI-R-2 (migration 20261124094656). |
| PI-D-5 | Where L3 lives | One helper, `private.is_removable(bill, user)`, used by both functions | The check written twice | One definition in the DB, like L1 | Open |
| PI-D-6 | `add_participant` with a null user id | `22023 not_linked` (it falls through the "already a participant" check) | `42501` or a separate error | Same answer as any id that isn't a linked user, so nothing new to map in the app | Open |
| PI-D-7 | Criterion 1, "every row that existed before the migration is false" | Checked once by hand on the local DB (0 of 4 rows had `added = true`); the pgTAP file only checks fixture rows (host row and joined row) | A whole-table assertion in pgTAP | A whole-table check would fail once real added rows exist (lessons log: tests check only their own rows) | Open |
| PI-D-8 | Extra ranking case | Added an R3 case with "Sam" and "ali" alongside the spec's "sam" / "Ali" | Only the spec's case | The spec's names sort the same with or without `lower()`, so they didn't prove case-insensitivity | Open |

## 2026-11-24 · People and invites, native decisions (spec: [docs/specs/2026-11-24-people-and-invites.md](specs/2026-11-24-people-and-invites.md), Task 8; `expo-contacts` 57.0.9, `expo-sms` 57.0.2)
- Found in the installed sources: `Contact.presentPicker()` opens the system picker without any permission on both platforms, but it hands back only the contact's id (iOS `ios/next/form/FormDelegate.swift:164-170`; Android `android/.../next/Contact.kt:220-229`). Reading the numbers with `getPhones()` goes back to the contacts store, and that needs contacts access: on iOS `CNContactStore.unifiedContact` (`ios/next/ContactRepository.swift:32-35`, called from `ios/next/properties/ListPropertyManager.swift:35-38`), on Android a query on `ContactsContract.Data` (`android/.../next/domain/ContactRepository.kt:233-238`), which needs `READ_CONTACTS`. So PI-19's "ask for nothing" isn't possible with this API.

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| PI-N-1 | Contacts permission (changes PI-19) | Ask for contacts access when "From contacts" is tapped, before the picker opens, never at launch. Denied → `denied` (the screen's "You can allow it in Settings" copy) | (A) iOS uses the legacy `expo-contacts/legacy` `presentContactPickerAsync`, which serializes the picked contact without any permission (`ios/ContactsModule.swift:375-389`), and Android asks; (B) open the picker first and ask only if reading fails | (A) is the legacy API ("Mark legacy API deprecated", CHANGELOG 56.0.5). It also copies every field, including the photo into the app's cache folder, and reads the note field, which needs a special Apple entitlement. Android needs `READ_CONTACTS` with either API (`android/.../ContactsModule.kt:327-330`). (B) shows the picker, then a prompt, then maybe a failure: more confusing. **Changes a "contacts stay private" decision: needs your review.** Play's data-safety form must then say contacts are accessed (not collected) (Risk 3) | Open |
| PI-N-2 | Android `READ_CONTACTS` | Not blocked. Only `WRITE_CONTACTS` is in `android.blockedPermissions` | Block both | Spec check (c): the picker can't return numbers without it. With `WRITE_CONTACTS` blocked, the library asks only for read (`android/.../next/ContactsPermissionsDelegate.kt:18-25`) | Open |
| PI-N-3 | iOS 18 "limited" access when the picked contact isn't shared with the app | `denied` (so the screen points to Settings) | `error` ("Use Share… instead") | Reading fails for that one contact, and the fix is in Settings (share it, or allow full access). *Not verified on a device*: whether iOS adds a contact picked this way to the limited set | Open |
| PI-N-4 | Phone labels | Passed through as the library gives them ("mobile", "home", "iPhone", "work", "other" for unlabeled; Android may differ); iOS labels it has no name for ("_$!<Name>!$_") are unwrapped to "Name". Blank → null | Map to friendly words in the wrapper | The wrapper stays a thin copy of `number` + `label`. Task 10 can decide how labels read on screen (the keys aren't localized) | Open |
| PI-N-5 | What `sendSms` counts as success | Any composer result ('sent', 'cancelled', 'unknown') is `'done'`; only a throw is `'error'` | Treat 'cancelled' as its own result | The spec's flow goes back to idle either way, and Android always reports 'unknown' | Open |
| PI-N-6 | Testability | `createContactPicker(contacts, sms)` takes the two native APIs; the exported functions are that factory wired to the real modules. `lib/contactPicker.ts` exports the `ContactPicker` type for screens to fake | Only `jest.mock` the modules | Tests can pass plain fakes; Task 10 mocks `lib/contactPicker` | Open |
| PI-N-7 | A picker that never settles | No timeout in the wrapper | Time out after N seconds | The user may browse contacts for as long as they like; both platforms resolve on cancel (iOS delegate; Android back fixed in 2024, CHANGELOG #29202). Task 10 owns single-flight and unmount | Open |
| PI-N-8 | Criterion 51's prebuild check | Checked with `npx expo config --type introspect` (runs the same config plugins without writing `ios/`/`android/`): `NSContactsUsageDescription` is the spec text; the manifest has `READ_CONTACTS` and `WRITE_CONTACTS` with `tools:node="remove"` | `npx expo prebuild --clean` in a copy | Same plugin output; no generated folders written. A real build still confirms the merged manifest | Open |
| PI-N-9 | Criterion 54 (web bundle has no picker code) | Not verifiable yet: nothing imports `lib/contactPicker` until Task 10, so neither the web nor the iOS export contains `presentPicker`/`sendSMSAsync` today. Re-run after Task 10 | — | The lessons log says to show the check can find something first; today it can't | Closed 2026-11-24 (QA): after Task 10, `npx expo export -p ios` to a scratch folder has both strings in its `.hbc` (`/usr/bin/grep -a -c`: presentPicker 1, sendSMSAsync 1); a fresh `npm run export:web` has neither in any file under `dist/_expo/static/js/web/` (same grep, no match; it does match "Claim what you had on SplitIt" in the entry bundle). Criterion 54 passes |

Such a mark also stops them from being removed or leaving (L3, M7). | Accept (honor-system marks; ADR 0015) | Make `mark_paid` refuse when the caller's share is ≤ 0 (DB change; needs the split math in SQL) | Open |

## 2026-11-24 · People and invites, screen decisions (spec: [docs/specs/2026-11-24-people-and-invites.md](specs/2026-11-24-people-and-invites.md), Tasks 6 and 7)
Made by the product-engineer while building the Profile card, the Add people screen, "Add people" in the Invite card and "Leave this bill". Copy that the spec fixes is used as written.

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| PI-E-1 | Title of the addable list on Add people | "People you've split with" (card title), with "On this bill" as the second card | "People you've split with, not on this bill yet" as a literal title | The spec's bold line reads as a description of the section. The people already on the bill are in their own card right below, so "not on this bill yet" adds length without adding meaning | Open |
| PI-E-2 | "Only <host> can add people." when the host has no display name | "Only the host can add people." | "Only A friend can add people." | "A friend" reads oddly as the subject of a rule | Open |
| PI-E-3 | Announcement after Remove | "Sam Lee removed" (live region; spoken on iOS), like "Sam Lee added" | Announce nothing | The spec announces adds only; a screen-reader user otherwise hears nothing when the row goes away | Open |
| PI-E-4 | Add or Remove worked, but the refetch after it failed | The button is released, "Sam Lee added" still shows (the server confirmed it), plus "Couldn't refresh the bill." with "Try again" | Keep the button busy until a refetch lands; show only the error | Lesson 2026-11-24: a failed refetch must never leave buttons stuck. Tapping Add again is harmless (the server treats it as a no-op) | Open |
| PI-E-5 | Several adds in a row | Each person has their own double-tap guard, so the host can tap Add on Sam and then on Ali without waiting | One write at a time for the whole screen | Adding three friends shouldn't need three waits. Each button still sends one call per tap | Open |
| PI-E-6 | Search when the list shrinks to 5 or fewer while a query is typed (after adding people) | The field disappears and so does its filter | Keep the field until the screen is left | A hidden filter would hide people with no visible reason | Open |
| PI-E-7 | Same-name check (PI-10) on Add people | Compares against everyone in your list, including people already on the bill | Only the people still addable | If one Alex is on the bill and the other isn't, the handle still tells the host which one they're adding | Open |
| PI-E-8 | Section order on Add people | The addable list (with search) first, "On this bill" second | "On this bill" first | Adding is the screen's main action, and the spec lists it first. "On this bill" is short and sits right below | Open |
| PI-E-9 | Profile's People card and the profile load | The card loads on its own: it shows (with its own loading and error) even when the Venmo part failed to load | Show the card only after the profile loads | Two separate reads; one failing shouldn't hide the other | Open |
| PI-E-10 | Leave errors | `cannot_leave`: the confirm closes, the bill refetches, and the message stays even though "Leave this bill" then disappears. A network error keeps the confirm open with "Leave" enabled | Same handling for both | After `cannot_leave` they now hold a claim, so trying again can't work; after a network error it can | Open |
| PI-E-11 | "No items yet" on the host's own row | Shown, as the spec says (every row without claims) | Hide it on "You" | Spec as written. Hiding it on "You" would make the card a little quieter; your call | Open |
| PI-E-12 | Hints the spec didn't word | "Add": "Puts them on this bill so they can claim". "Remove": "Takes them off this bill. You can add them again." "Leave this bill": "Asks before taking you off this bill". "Leave" (in the confirm): "Takes you off this bill". Back: "Returns to the bill" | — | Criterion 49 asks for hints where the result isn't obvious | Open |
| PI-E-13 | "Add people" button | Secondary, with a "+" glyph before the label (hidden from screen readers) | Plain label | The "+" marks it as the action that adds, among three outlined buttons in the card | Open |
| PI-E-14 | Existing test fixture | `__tests__/profile.test.tsx`'s fake now answers `people_i_split_with` with an empty list. No assertion changed | Leave it; the card then shows its error and a second "Try again" | The fake's default answer (`null`) isn't a valid people response, so it made a second alert and a second "Try again" on Profile | Open |
| PI-E-15 | A refetch that never answers (neither lands nor fails) | No timeout; the button stays busy until a later refetch (live update, focus, app foreground) finishes | A timeout that releases it | Same as the Settle screen. `fetchBill` has no hang path in tests; noted for review | Open |
| PI-E-16 | Logic placement | New `lib/peopleView.ts` (screen copy, "Only … can add people.", "<name> added/removed", the "On this bill" rows) and `lib/usePeople.ts` (the list, newest answer only, nothing set after unmount, reset when the signed-in user changes) | Inline in the screens | Pure logic stays in `lib/` with its own tests | Open |

**User checks (need a person):**
- U-E-1. Look at Profile and Add people in light and dark mode on a phone and on web (not seen in a browser by the agent: the screens need a signed-in session).
- U-E-2. On Add people with a long list, scroll down and tap Add: say whether the row disappearing plus "Sam Lee added" at the top of the screen (out of view when scrolled) is enough feedback, or whether the confirmation should sit next to the list.
- U-E-3. VoiceOver on Add people (spec U6): each row reads its name, bill count and date; "Add Sam Lee" is a button; "Sam Lee added" is spoken after adding; "Remove Sam Lee from this bill" reads as one button.
- U-E-4. On the bill as a guest with nothing claimed: "Leave this bill" at the end of the page is easy to find but not in the way (spec U3 covers the leave itself).

One primary per step | Open |
