## 2026-11-25 · Settle early and differences (spec: [docs/specs/2026-11-25-settle-early-and-differences.md](specs/2026-11-25-settle-early-and-differences.md))
User requests: settle once you've claimed your items in proportional mode; buttons to pay or pay back the difference when a share changes after "I paid". Decided by the PM under the "don't stop for questions" delegation. Supersedes M3-UX-7 and VR-5; closes K6 (M3-6, M3-R-1, M3-B-12).

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SE-1 | When early settling opens (changes M3 Q1) | Proportional mode only, per person: you have a claim on at least one item row (discount-only claims don't count), the bill isn't all-$0 (rule Z), and you aren't the payer. Even mode stays blocked until complete. One rule E in `lib/settle.ts` (`maySettle`) and SQL (`private.settle_state`), same boundary table E1–E15 on both sides | Open early for everyone in proportional (even with no claims); require "all your items" (unknowable) | In proportional mode others' claims can't move your share except 1¢ (the weights' sum is fixed), so the amount you pay is stable; a person with no item claim would see $0. Even mode changes everyone's share with every new claimer. **Money: needs your look** | Open |
| SE-2 | What a mark stores after a difference | `settled_cents` = the net amount settled (marked + paid more − paid back), 0..99,999,999; 0 means "paid back in full". `settled_at` stays the first mark's time | Separate `paid_more` / `paid_back` columns; clear the mark at 0 | One number keeps the existing "mark vs share" comparison; a stored 0 tells "paid back" apart from an Undo without guessing (clearing would show "Your I paid was undone"). Activity keeps every step. **Money: needs your look** | Open. **Changed by SE-ENG-1 (2026-11-25, spec A1.1):** still the net amount, but the range stays 1..99,999,999 and a pay-back to $0 clears the mark; 0 is never stored |
| SE-3 | All-$0 bills (K6) | Closed: the DB refuses `mark_paid` / `settle_difference` with `22023 all_zero` using rule Z (proportional, Σ item rows = 0, total − Σ rows ≠ 0), the same rule the app uses | Keep K6 as an accepted difference | The user asked not to create another K6-style gap; Z is a 3-term SQL expression and a property test proves it equals the app's `unclaimedCents ≠ 0` on complete bills. Departs from the approved-exception state of K6, so **needs your look** | Open |
| SE-4 | Who acts when a share goes down | The payer pays back ("Pay $1.10 back" Venmo pay link + "I paid it back", which records it); the person gets a "Request $1.10 from Hana" link that never writes | Only the person's Request; let the person record "Hana paid me back" | Same rule as the rest of the app: whoever owes the money pays and records it. The Request is the nudge, as on the payer's rows | Open |
| SE-5 | Who may call `settle_difference` | Raising a mark: only that person. Lowering: only the current payer. Not the host | Host may do both; either party both ways | Each side can only record money they sent, so nobody can erase what someone else owes them | Open |
| SE-6 | Differences before the bill is complete | Shown as info ("Settle the difference once everything is claimed"), no buttons; the DB requires complete (`final`) too | Allow difference payments while incomplete | During claiming, shares move as people share items; settling once at the end avoids several small back-and-forth payments. Same rule both sides | Open. Display amended by SE-UX-2 and SE-ENG-5 (2026-11-25): the info line says which way and how much, from the one `difference` field; still no buttons before complete |
| SE-7 | Stale-state token | `settle_difference(p_bill_id, p_user_id, p_payer_id, p_expected_cents, p_new_cents)`; `p_expected_cents` must equal the stored mark or `22023 mark_changed` | A `mark_paid` variant that overwrites; a version counter column | Catches double taps, two devices and a pay-back landing mid-payment with no new column. It can't see the share changing again; the record stays what was actually paid, and the next difference shows after the refetch | Open. Amended by SE-ENG-3 (2026-11-25): a retry whose requested state is already stored succeeds with no event instead of `mark_changed`; `p_expected_cents` is now 1.. (SE-ENG-1) |
| SE-8 | Payer's Request buttons before complete | Not shown in early mode | Show them with the "so far" amount | Requesting while people are still claiming invites a second request later; the ower can pay early on their own | Open |
| SE-9 | Bill-screen footer in early mode | Primary "Settle up" with "You can pay now · N left to claim"; Pay stays on Settle up | Pay straight from the footer as in VR-11 | The "may change" line needs room; one caption makes early paying discoverable without a second Pay path to keep in sync | Open |
| SE-10 | Copy for differences | `You owe Hana more` / `$1.10` / `Pay Hana $1.10 more on Venmo` / `I paid the difference`; `Hana owes you` / `Request $1.10 from Hana on Venmo`; payer rows `Owes $1.10 more` + `Request $1.10`, `You owe $1.10 back` + `Pay $1.10 back` / `I paid it back` | Keep "You paid Hana $15.10" as the label with a warning line | The big number is what you act on now, same as every other card | Open |
| SE-11 | 1¢ differences | Not prompted: `SETTLE_DIFF_IGNORE_CENTS = 1` (starting value) hides differences of ±1¢ in the app; the DB has no tolerance | Prompt every cent | Rounding alone moves a share by 1¢ when others claim (W7); asking someone to send $0.01 is noise. The payer may be 1¢ off per person. **Money: needs your look** | Open |
| SE-12 | Pay-back when the share went below $0 | Pay back down to $0 only; the rest stays "Hana owes you $1.00. Settle that outside the app." (Q6) | Pay back the full amount (negative net) | Keeps marks ≥ 0 and Q6's answer unchanged; negative shares are rare. **Money: needs your look** | Open |
| SE-13 | A $0 mark and leaving/removal (rule L3) | Counts as a mark (blocks leaving until undone) | Ignore $0 marks in L3 | No change to `leave_bill`/`remove_participant`; Undo is one tap. Revisit if it bothers people | Superseded by SE-ENG-1 (2026-11-25): no $0 mark is ever stored, so the question no longer arises; L3 unchanged |
| SE-14 | `mark_paid`'s lock order | Participation read before the bill lock (R4 rule), then `FOR SHARE` as today | Leave the old order | Brings it in line with the newer functions while it's being replaced anyway | Open. See SE-ENG-4 (2026-11-25): the participant-row lock is `FOR NO KEY UPDATE` (implicit in `mark_paid`, explicit in `settle_difference`) |

## 2026-11-25 · Home settle markers (same spec, D7)
User request: "Paid" on bills I owe on; "Settled up" or "2 people left to pay" on bills I'm owed on.

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| HM-1 | Where the numbers come from | (c) Home batch-reads the listed bills' rows, participants and claims in 4 requests (`fetchSettleData`, same columns and parsers as `fetchBill`) and runs the same `settlePlan` / `settleCard` as the Settle screen | (a) `my_bills` counts in SQL; (b) `fetchBill` per bill (up to 80 requests) | "Who owes" needs `computeShares` (zero and negative shares, even mode); a SQL count would be a second split-math rule. (c) has one rule and few requests | Open. Amended by SE-ENG-9 (2026-11-25): `.range(0, 999)` truncation guard, only candidate bills read, per-bill parsing |
| HM-2 | Ower markers | `You owe $15.10` (before paying, ready or early), `✓ Paid $15.10`, `Paid · $1.10 more to pay`, `Paid · $1.10 back to you`; none when nothing is owed | Only "Paid" | "You owe" is the obvious next step; difference states say what's left in one line | Open |
| HM-3 | Priority | `New` → `Nothing claimed yet` → settle marker; one per row; the host now gets settle markers | Two markers per row | One marker keeps rows calm; the existing two come first because they need action before money does | Open. Detail fixed by SE-ENG-10 (2026-11-25): the host skips only steps 1–2 and reaches the settle markers |
| HM-4 | Payer markers before complete | None (also in early mode). Complete: `1 person left to pay` / `N people left to pay`, then `N difference(s) to settle`, then `✓ Settled up` | Show "left to pay" early | Before everyone claims, "left to pay" would count people who don't know their share yet | Open; **amended 2026-11-26 by Sent via Venmo (D7, Task 7):** the payer's early bill can show `sent` ("Sam sent you $15.10") while someone owes, so `settleCandidateIds` no longer skips it; "left to pay" still waits for completion |
| HM-5 | When settle data fails to load | Rows keep today's markers, no error | Show an error | The list itself loaded; a missing pill is harmless, a wrong one isn't | Open |
| HM-6 | Privacy | No new exposure: the same rows RLS already shows on each bill screen; paid counts are visible to everyone on the bill (Q5) | — | Nothing new is written, logged or broadcast | Open |

### 2026-11-25 · Settle early and differences, UX brief (brief: [docs/specs/2026-11-25-settle-early-and-differences-ux.md](specs/2026-11-25-settle-early-and-differences-ux.md))
Decided by the PM under the "don't stop for questions" delegation. "Changes the spec" rows need a dated amendment in the spec before Tasks 3, 6, 7 and 9 start.

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SE-UX-1 | Line on a card marked early with no change | `If your share changes, you'll settle the difference here once everything is claimed.` | The spec's `Your share may change until everything is claimed (N left).` | After paying, people need to know what happens next. A bare "may change" reads as a warning with nothing to do. Copy/layout only | Open |
| SE-UX-2 | Early mark whose share moved (`pending`) | Muted, no ⚠, says the direction and amount: `You'll pay the $1.10 difference …` / `Hana will owe you $1.10 back …` (amount capped at the mark). `pending` gains `kind` and `cents` | Spec: warning tone, one sentence for both directions | Nothing to act on yet, so a warning next to "✓ Marked paid" reads as an error. Knowing "pay more" vs "get back" and how much removes the guesswork. **Changes the spec** (D4/D6, criterion 27) | Open; folded into spec A1. Model part changed by SE-ENG-5: no `pending` field; the copy reads `difference.kind` / `difference.cents` |
| SE-UX-3 | Undo label on difference cards | `Undo "I paid"`, hint `Clears your whole I paid of $15.10` | Bare `Undo` | Next to "I paid the difference", "Undo" looks like it undoes the difference, but it clears the whole mark. Copy/layout only | Superseded by SV-UX-18 / SV-T6-15 (2026-11-26): `Undo all $15.10` |
| SE-UX-4 | Payer's Undo on a row with an open difference | Hidden for the payer while the difference is open (the difference controls take its place); back once settled. Host-not-payer keeps Undo | Show Undo as a third control | Three controls on one row is clutter, and Undo there erases what the person already paid, which the payer almost never means. Rare dispute path: settle first, then Undo. **Changes the spec** (D6 rows) | Open; folded into spec A1. **Changed by SE-ENG-13:** a payer who is also the host keeps Undo, on its own line below the difference controls |
| SE-UX-5 | Row layout for difference controls | Control line under the row: `@handle` left, controls right; `I paid it back` on its own line under `Pay $1.10 back`; ready difference sub-line `Marked $X · share now $Y` without ⚠; payer's back-due status in warning tone, more-due muted | Controls inline with name/amount; keep ⚠ | Same spot as Request/Undo (VR-P1). No two ghost buttons side by side on a phone. One emphasis point per row. Copy/layout only | Open |
| SE-UX-6 | Status on the viewer's own row | `You owe $1.10 more` / `Hana owes you $1.10 back` | The spec's third-person `Owes $1.10 more` / `Hana owes $1.10 back` | The row is named "You"; third person reads "You owes" or "Hana owes [whom?]". Copy/layout only | Open |
| SE-UX-7 | back_due when the share is below $0 | Extra line `Hana also owes you $1.00 for your share. Settle that outside the app.` | Show only "Your share is now -$1.00" | Otherwise the extra dollar is unexplained (pay-back stops at $0, SE-12). Copy/layout only | Open |
| SE-UX-8 | Explaining why even-mode bills can't be paid early | Blocked card adds `Tax and tip are split evenly on this bill, so shares aren't set until then.` (existing blocked sentence unchanged) | No explanation | With early settling, one bill lets you pay now and the next doesn't. Without a reason it looks like a bug. Copy/layout only (needs the split mode in the view input) | Open |
| SE-UX-9 | Bill-screen footer captions | Early marked: secondary + `✓ Marked paid · N left to claim`. Ready `more_due`: primary + `You owe Hana $1.10 more`. `back_due`: primary + `Hana owes you $1.10 back`. Payer with differences: primary + `N difference(s) to settle`. Still no Pay in the footer | Spec: footer unchanged apart from the early caption | A changed share is otherwise invisible until someone opens Settle up. A caption is the quietest pointer and adds no second Pay path (SE-9 kept). **Changes the spec** (D6 footer, criteria 34/43) | Open |
| SE-UX-10 | Home pill tones | `You owe` accent; `✓ Paid` / `✓ Settled up` success; `more to pay` / `to settle` warning; `back to you` / `left to pay` / `Nothing claimed yet` muted; `New` accent | Spec: H1 neutral | "You owe" is the one settle pill asking the viewer to act, like "New". Copy/layout only | Superseded by SE-VIS-1 (two tones, accent/muted), folded into spec A1 |
| SE-UX-11 | Web line when the payer's list has Pay-back links | `On a computer? Open Venmo on your phone to pay or request.`; the existing request line stays when there are only Request links | Reuse the request line; no line | The request line says "requests" only. One extra string, and the rule is simple. Copy/layout only | Open |
| SE-UX-12 | Announcements for differences | iOS: `Difference marked paid`, `Marked paid back`. Android/web: the card's existing live "✓ Marked paid"; a new polite caption slot on the Everyone card for `Marked paid back` | No announcement for list writes | The payer's write happens in the list, which has no live slot today. Copy/layout only | Open |
| SE-UX-13 | `not_complete` copy on the Settle screen | Settle-only `Something on the bill just changed. Check your share and try again.` (`BILL_ERROR_COPY` untouched) | Keep "Some items aren't claimed anymore…" | Early marks fail with it when the host switches to even or you unclaim everything, and the old copy is wrong in both cases. Same pattern as M3-UX-15. Copy/layout only | Open |
| SE-UX-14 | Home settle pills during a refresh | Keep each row's previous settle pill until the new settle data lands; drop settle pills if that read fails; the list never waits for settle data | Clear pills at every refresh; wait for both reads before showing the list | Otherwise pills blink off and on at every focus/foreground, and waiting would slow the list people came for. A failed read still shows no pill (HM-5). **Changes the spec** (D7/Task 9, criterion 44) | Open |
| SE-UX-15 | Card after a difference is recorded | Net amount ("You paid Hana $16.20 / ✓ Marked paid"), no history line | A "paid $15.10 + $1.10" breakdown | One number that matches what the payer sees. Activity has the steps. Confirms spec | Open |
| SE-UX-16 | $0 mark (paid back) and Undo | No Undo on the paid-back card (as spec). The $0 mark still blocks leaving (SE-13), and `cannot_leave` copy ("Unclaim your items first.") doesn't mention it. Not changed in this slice | Show Undo on the paid-back card; change the leave copy now | Rare, and the leave copy belongs to the people/invites flow. Flagged so it isn't forgotten. Confirms spec | Superseded by SE-ENG-1 (2026-11-25): a pay-back to $0 clears the mark, so there's no $0 mark, no paid-back card, and nothing blocks leaving |
| SE-UX-17 | "Add your Venmo" for the payer in early mode | Shown on an early plan too, once anyone has an item claim | Only on a ready plan (today) | Owers can pay early and would see "Hana hasn't added Venmo yet" while Hana gets no prompt. **Changes the spec** (D6 payer row; `addVenmoPrompt` condition) | Open; folded into spec A1 (condition: any non-payer can settle) |

### 2026-11-25 · Settle early and differences, visual design (doc: [docs/specs/2026-11-25-settle-early-and-differences-visual.md](specs/2026-11-25-settle-early-and-differences-visual.md))
Decided by the visual designer under the "don't stop for questions" delegation. No new colours or tokens. SE-VIS-1, -3 and -6 change what view models return (doc §8).

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SE-VIS-1 | Home pill tones | Two tones only. Accent (`primary`/`primarySurface`): New, You owe, Paid · more to pay, N differences to settle. Muted (`textMuted`/`surfaceMuted`): Nothing claimed yet, ✓ Paid, Paid · back to you, N left to pay, ✓ Settled up. Contrast 4.84/7.80 (accent), 6.79/7.04 (muted), light/dark | The brief's four tones (SE-UX-10): success green for done, warning amber for differences | Colour means one thing on Home: "your move". Four hues on one list read as a rainbow, and teal next to green is easy to confuse. Done bills should be the quietest rows; `✓` and the words already say done. **Changes the spec's H1–H7 tone column and criterion 35** | Open |
| SE-VIS-2 | Pill metrics | Unchanged from today: caption 13/18 600, 8×2 padding, `radius.pill`, under the label with 4-pt gap, text glyph ✓ in the pill's colour, wraps and never truncates | A lucide check icon; per-tone glyph colour | The existing pills already look right in both schemes (QA screenshots); the 16-pt icon size is too big for a 13-pt pill | Open. Glyph detail changed by SE-ENG-19: no ✓ in pill text; whether a check icon is drawn is the user's question Q-GLYPH (spec A1; recommended: drop it on pills). **Q-GLYPH answered by the user 2026-11-25: no icon on pills** (built that way, `MarkerPill` ignores `icon`) |
| SE-VIS-3 | Request button on the card (B4) | Secondary (outline, block); B4 has no primary, and the amount is the focal point | Primary (brief) | Request only nudges, records nothing and doesn't finish the bill. A filled button promises "tap and you're done". Pay and "I paid the difference" stay primary. **Needs a model change** (card must mark the button as outline) | Open. Built 2026-11-25 as `Button variant="secondary"`, which the visual pass (V6) made a tonal teal tint, not an outline (SE-T8-5); the point (not filled) holds |
| SE-VIS-4 | New card lines | "May change", early-marked, pending, diff and no-Venmo lines are all muted 13-pt regular captions in the head group; the success/warning lines stay 600. No new tone, icon or tinted block in the card | A tinted info block for the difference | Hierarchy from type and order, not boxes. The difference is already the card's display amount | Open |
| SE-VIS-5 | Ghost order on B1 | `I paid the difference` then `Undo "I paid"`, same wrapping row (`gap: space.lg`) | Stack them | Fits on one line at 375 pt; matches today's card secondary row. The forward action comes first | Open |
| SE-VIS-6 | Everyone list status column | Fixed 120-pt status column (text wraps inside, right-aligned) for every row when any row has a difference status; otherwise today's `minWidth: 64`. Status and wrapped control buttons get `marginLeft: 'auto'` so they stay flush right | Leave `minWidth: 64` (money column drifts); move statuses to a second line for all rows | Keeps the money column straight without redesigning every row; lists without differences don't change. **Needs `SettlePeopleModel.wideStatus`** | Open |
| SE-VIS-7 | Row controls | Ghost, `paddingHorizontal: 0`, 44×44 min, flush right; the second control on its own line; the `@handle` ellipsizes on one line, the button label never truncates | Shrink the button label | The handle is a visual fallback (screen readers get it from the button); the button is the action | Open |
| SE-VIS-8 | Footer captions | All muted regular captions, including the ✓; layout unchanged | Success-green ✓ / warning colour for differences | The primary button carries the emphasis; a coloured caption beside it would be a second focal point | Open |
| SE-VIS-9 | Avatar contrast (QA, dark) | Others: 1-pt `border` ring on the `surfaceMuted` fill (3.74/3.75 vs surface); you: 1-pt ring in `primary`. Replaces the 2-pt `surface` ring | A darker fill; a solid grey fill with light initials | The shape was invisible in dark (1.13:1). The ring is the outline style the app already uses for secondary buttons and fields, and `border` is the token defined as ≥ 3:1 on surface. App-wide: every `Avatar` | Open. Built by the visual pass, not by this spec's tasks (SE-ENG-20) |
| SE-VIS-10 | Long button labels | `Button` label `Text` gets `flexShrink: 1`, `textAlign: 'center'`, so long labels wrap and the button grows | Truncate; shorter labels | "Pay Hana Lee-Watanabe $1.10 more on Venmo" could run past the button on native. No change to one-line buttons. App-wide primitive | Open. Built by the visual pass, not by this spec's tasks (SE-ENG-20) |

### 2026-11-25 · Settle early and differences, engineering review (review: [docs/specs/2026-11-25-settle-early-and-differences-eng.md](specs/2026-11-25-settle-early-and-differences-eng.md); spec Amendment A1)
The user approved the build ("okay start implementing") and asked to fold in the staff engineer's review without stopping for questions. The main session listed which recommendations to adopt, and the PM wrote them into the spec as Amendment A1. SE-VIS-1, 3, 6, 9, 10 and SE-UX-2, 4, 9, 14, 17 are folded into the same amendment.

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SE-ENG-1 | What a pay-back to exactly $0 stores (F2) | It clears the mark (`settled_at`, `settled_cents` null) and logs `paid_back {from, to: 0}`. The `1..99,999,999` constraint stays. No "✓ Paid back" card or row status; Activity keeps the history | Store 0 as "paid back in full" (SE-2 as first written) | A stored $0 had no Undo on any screen, blocked leaving and removal through L3, and reached undesigned cards ("You paid Hana $0.00"). Clearing keeps the schema, `set_payer` counts and L3 as they are. Supersedes SE-13 and SE-UX-16; changes SE-2. **Money: needs your look** | Open |
| SE-ENG-2 | Notice when someone else clears your mark and your share is now ≤ $0 | No notice (the "undone" banner is suppressed). Shares > $0 keep today's banner | Show "✓ Hana paid you back."; a neutral "Your 'I paid' was cleared. Check Activity to see who." | After a real pay-back, "undone" reads as alarming. "Paid you back" would be false when the payer used Undo instead, which SE-ENG-13 still allows. Suppressing says nothing untrue; Activity has the step. Risk logged in before-production | Kept by the user (2026-11-25, option A; see "User answers"). Test: review R9 in `__tests__/settle.test.tsx` |
| SE-ENG-3 | Retrying `settle_difference` after a lost response | Idempotent: under the row lock, if the row already holds the requested state, return success with no write, event or broadcast. This runs after the auth and state checks, before the token compare | Keep `mark_changed` for any second call (SE-7 as first written) | A retry for a write that worked shouldn't show "Your 'I paid' just changed". What it gives up: two devices raising the same mark to the same value record once, so a real duplicate payment isn't flagged. Amends SE-7 | Open |
| SE-ENG-4 | Lock on the participant row | `FOR NO KEY UPDATE` (explicit in `settle_difference`; `mark_paid`'s UPDATE takes it implicitly). D10 now lists the `bill_events` FK locks (bills; the caller's profiles row) | `FOR UPDATE` (spec as first written) | `FOR UPDATE` conflicts with the `FOR KEY SHARE` every claim takes, so it would queue behind the person's own claim taps. `NO KEY UPDATE` still serializes with `mark_paid` and `unmark_paid`. Relates to SE-14 | Open |
| SE-ENG-5 | `pending` vs `difference` | One `difference` field, computed the same way in `ready` and `early` plans. The view decides whether it can be acted on (buttons only in `ready`) | A separate `pending` (spec), or `pending` with `kind`/`cents` (SE-UX-2) | Same shape and formula, so one formula fewer to keep in sync. Changes SE-UX-2's model part; its copy stays | Open |
| SE-ENG-6 | When the "Did you pay?" prompt survives a refetch (F3) | While the **payer and the mark** match (`share`: unmarked; `more`: mark equals the opened `fromCents`), whatever the share is now. Recording uses the opened amount | Today's rule: drop it when the amount changes | The person already sent that amount in Venmo, and dropping the prompt would offer a button that records the **new** amount. Early mode makes share changes frequent. Any gap left shows as a normal difference | Open |
| SE-ENG-7 | Plan statuses with and without `allowEarly` (F4) | Without: incomplete → `blocked` (as today, even if all-$0). With: incomplete all-$0 → `blocked_all_zero`, incomplete proportional → `early`, incomplete even → `blocked`. Complete is the same either way | — | The spec contradicted itself (Task 3 vs criterion 17b). Pins it so existing tests stay unchanged without the flag | Open |
| SE-ENG-8 | Payer owes pay-backs but nobody owes the payer (F5) | Pay-backs owed win: the payer card is `You owe money back` + `⚠ N difference(s) to settle`, and Home shows H6 (accent), not "Nobody owes you anything" / no pill | Today's `payer_nobody` first | Otherwise the payer is told everything's fine while they owe money. `You owe money back` is new PM copy | Open |
| SE-ENG-9 | Home batch read, truncation and scope (F1) | `.range(0, 999)` on each of the 4 requests, with ≥ 1,000 rows treated as failure (no settle pills); only read bills that can show a settle pill; parse each bill on its own; compare claims as sets in the parity test. A one-snapshot definer RPC is the recorded upgrade path | Read all 20 bills with no guard; build the RPC now | `max_rows = 1000` (`supabase/config.toml:19`) cuts results off **without an error**, which would give a wrong "You owe $X". Trimming ids costs nothing. Amends HM-1 | Open |
| SE-ENG-10 | The host and `billMarker` | The host skips only `new`/`unclaimed` and reaches the settle markers | Today's early `return null` for the host | Otherwise host-payers (the default) never get H5–H7. Fixes HM-3's detail | Open |
| SE-ENG-11 | More boundary cases | E16–E20 in both the pgTAP and Jest tables. Criterion 17c widened to random sequences of other people's actions run to completion (≤ 1¢ in total; exactly 0 when the person's item subtotal is 0) | The original E1–E15 and single-claim 17c | They cover a discount-only incomplete bill, the complete twin of E10, extras coming only from a discount, negative extras, and losing your item claim through an edit | Open |
| SE-ENG-12 | Keeping pgTAP and Jest cases in step | `[E#]`/`[D#]` tags in both, plus one Jest test (`lib/settleCaseIds.test.ts`) that reads the `.sql` files and fails when the tag sets differ | Rely on review | Otherwise a case can land on one side only and the "same rule" claim silently breaks (CLAUDE.md shared-rule convention) | Open |
| SE-ENG-13 | Payer's Undo on a row with an open difference | Hidden for a payer who isn't the host (SE-UX-4). A **host-payer keeps Undo**, on its own line below the difference controls | Hide it for every payer (SE-UX-4 as written) | The payer is the host by default. Hiding Undo for them meant nobody but the person could undo a mistaken mark while a difference was open. Changes SE-UX-4 | Open |
| SE-ENG-14 | Task order (F7) | Task 10 split: **10a** (live parity, E/D tables, random bills, truncation, drift test) after 1, 2, 3, 4, gating Tasks 8 and 9; **10b** (view, markers, Activity live) after 2, 5, 6, 7. Order: 1 → 2; 3 ‖ 4 ‖ 5; 10a; 6 (6a, 6b); 7; 8 ‖ 9; 10b; 11 | One Task 10 after 2, 4, 5 | Task 10 also depends on 3, 6 and 7, and parity problems should surface before the screens are built | Open |
| SE-ENG-15 | Planned breakage (F6) | (Stub part changed by SE-ENG-21: Task 3 uses `settlePlan` overloads instead of a stub in `lib/settleView.ts`.) Task 3 adds a stub `case 'early'` in `settleCard` so typecheck stays green; Task 6 updates the copy/state-only screen assertions that flip when `allowEarly` turns on; `settle_payer.test.sql:85-88`, `lib/bills.test.ts:552` and `lib/activityText.test.ts:681` are untouched under SE-ENG-1 | Leave typecheck red for three tasks; let Task 8 find the screen breakage | Lessons 2026-11-24: list cross-task breakage up front | Open |
| SE-ENG-16 | Quote style for "I paid" in Activity | Match the code: single quotes (`'I paid'`, `lib/activityText.ts`). Settle screen copy keeps its double quotes, as today | Switch Activity to double quotes | The spec's `Hana undid Sam's "I paid"` contradicted the shipped sentences. No user-visible change. The 0-cent `unmarked_paid` sentence is gone anyway (SE-ENG-1) | Open |
| SE-ENG-17 | How `private.settle_state` reads the bill | One `language sql` statement (CTEs) with bigint sums, called once per function | plpgsql with several selects; integer sums | Under READ COMMITTED each plpgsql select takes a new snapshot, so a claim landing between them could give an answer that matches no real state. 500 rows × 9,999,999 overflows int4 | Open |
| SE-ENG-19 | Status glyphs in strings (from the visual-pass session's V11/P3, already in `lib/settleView.ts:131`) | Strings from `lib/settleView.ts` / `lib/homeMarkers.ts` carry no `✓`/`⚠`, only a `tone`. The UI draws lucide `Check` / `TriangleAlert` from the tone, and screen readers get words only. Home pills keep accent/muted and return `icon: 'check' \| null`. Whether a pill draws it is the user's question Q-GLYPH; we build "no icon" until answered | Keep text glyphs (brief, visual doc) | One rule app-wide; no `withoutGlyph` stripping for screen readers. Changes SE-VIS-2's glyph detail | Open |
| SE-ENG-20 | Who builds the shared UI files | The visual pass (splitit-app-52) builds the Button label wrap (SE-VIS-10) and the Avatar ring (SE-VIS-9) and owns the copy in `lib/settleView.ts`. Our Tasks 6, 8 and 9 start only after it reports V6–V13 done, and re-read its files first. Tasks 1–5, 7 and 10a start now | Build them in our Task 9; race the visual pass | Two sessions editing the same components would conflict; their pass already includes our two fixes | Open |
| SE-ENG-21 | Decoupling Task 7 (Home markers) from Task 6, and Task 3 from `lib/settleView.ts` | `billMarker` reads the plan's entries from `lib/settle.ts` (role, mark, `canSettle`, `difference`), not Task 6's card states. A Task 6 test (criterion 67) checks that markers and cards agree. Task 3 adds `early` through `settlePlan` overloads (`SettlePlanWithEarly`), so it doesn't touch `lib/settleView.ts` | Task 7 after Task 6 (reading `settleCard`); a stub case in `settleView.ts` (SE-ENG-15 as first written) | As written, Task 7 depended on Task 6 and would have waited behind the visual-pass gate. Reading the plan is the same rule source, and the agreement test keeps Home and Settle from drifting. The overloads keep Task 3 off a file another session is editing | Open |
| SE-ENG-18 | "People may already be paying …" in the payer picker | Shown when the plan is `ready` **or `early`** (the caller passes `complete: ready \|\| early`) | Only on `ready` (today) | With early settling, people can already be paying while items are unclaimed | Open |

### 2026-11-25 · Settle early and differences, Tasks 4–5 (implementer; `lib/bills.ts`, `lib/activityText.ts`)
Spec: `docs/specs/2026-11-25-settle-early-and-differences.md` (Amendment A1), D4, D7, D8; criteria 21–25.

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SE-T4-1 | Columns in `fetchSettleData`'s child reads (deviates from criterion 24's "select strings equal fetchBill's") | The bills read uses `BILL_COLUMNS` exactly. The `line_items`, `bill_participants` and `claims` reads use the fetchBill constant plus `, bill_id`. The test asserts exactly that (`<fetchBill select>, bill_id`) | Add `bill_id` to the shared constants, so fetchBill selects it too; group claims through their row ids | None of `ROW_COLUMNS`, `PARTICIPANT_COLUMNS` or `CLAIM_COLUMNS` includes `bill_id`, so a four-request batch can't tell which bill a participant or row belongs to. Appending it keeps the constants shared (no drift) and leaves fetchBill and its exact-select test unchanged. `bill_id` has a table-level SELECT grant on all three tables, and the parsers drop it | Open |
| SE-T4-2 | A batched row that can't be put under a requested bill (null row, missing or non-UUID `bill_id`, or a bill that wasn't asked for) | The whole call fails (`network`) | Drop just that row | A row dropped silently could be someone's claim, which would give a wrong "You owe $X". Since it could belong to any bill, no bill's data can be trusted. PostgREST never returns such rows, so this is a guard, not a path | Open |
| SE-T4-3 | Per-bill failures in `fetchSettleData` | A bill is left out of the map when its bill row, any of its rows, participants or claims fail to parse, when RLS returns no bill row, or when its bill row comes back twice. The other bills keep their data | Fail the whole call | Spec D7 (per-bill parsing). A repeated bill row is the same "never guess" rule as `listMyBills`' repeated-id check | Open |
| SE-T4-4 | Bad ids passed to `fetchSettleData` | A non-UUID id is refused with `invalid_bill` and no request, like 21 ids. Repeated ids are sent once. `[]` returns an empty map with no request | Skip bad ids; send duplicates | Same local-refusal style as the other wrappers. Spec names only the 21-id case | Open |
| SE-T4-5 | Ordering in the batched reads | Rows are ordered by `bill_id, group_position, unit_index` and sorted again per bill after parsing (as fetchBill does). Participants are ordered by `joined_at`, as in fetchBill. Claims have no order (as in fetchBill) | Sort everything client-side | Gives the same per-bill order as fetchBill, so the parity test can compare deep-equal (claims as sets) | Open |
| SE-T4-6 | `all_zero` from `mark_paid` | Mapped to `invalid_bill`, the same as from `settle_difference` (criterion 22). `mark_changed` from `mark_paid` stays `network` (only `settle_difference` raises it) | Map it only for `settle_difference` | Task 1 makes `mark_paid` raise `22023 all_zero` (D3 step 6). Without the mapping it would show as a connection error | Open |
| SE-T5-1 | Name of the other person in the new Activity sentences | `targetName`: mid-sentence "you", "someone" or "a friend" (`Sam paid someone $1.10 more`) | `objectName`, as `marked_paid` uses ("Someone" stays capitalised) | These sentences are new, with no pinned tests. `targetName` is the newer helper, made for names mid-sentence | Open |
| SE-T5-2 | An event naming the actor as the other party (`paid_difference` by the payer, or `paid_back` to the actor) | Treated as malformed (null; the screen skips it) | Render a reflexive sentence | The database refuses both (D4 step 5: `p_user_id` can't be the payer), so such an event can only be forged or broken. Without this, it would read "You paid you back $1.10" | Open |

### 2026-11-25 · Settle early and differences, Tasks 3 and 7 (implementer; `lib/settle.ts`, `lib/homeMarkers.ts`)
Spec: `docs/specs/2026-11-25-settle-early-and-differences.md` (A1), Tasks 3 and 7.

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SE-IMP-1 | Where `difference` appears on plan entries | Present (a `SettleDifference` or null) on every **marked** non-payer in `ready` and `early` plans; absent on unmarked people and the payer, like `markDiffCents`. Read it with `differenceOf(entry)`, which gives null in every absent case | `difference: null` on every non-payer | The spec requires every existing `lib/settle.test.ts` assertion to pass unchanged, and several use `toEqual` on unmarked entries. An always-present `null` would break them. `differenceOf` gives callers the spec's "null for unmarked" answer | Open |
| SE-IMP-2 | The tolerance seam (criterion 20) | `differenceFor(owedCents, markCents, ignoreCents = SETTLE_DIFF_IGNORE_CENTS)` is exported. It throws `RangeError` for non-integer cents or a negative or non-integer tolerance | A module-level mutable setting | Pure and testable. An invalid amount never reads as "no difference" (lesson 2026-11-21, invalid money) | Open |
| SE-IMP-3 | Fields of the `early` plan | Exactly the spec's: `status, payerId, unclaimedRows, unclaimedDiscountRows, people`. Early entries carry `canSettle` and `difference`, but not `markDiffCents`/`markDiffers` | Add `owingCount`/`paidCount`/`collectCents` | Totals before completion would invite a "N of M paid" that isn't final. Task 6 counts marks from `people` for "`<p>` paid so far" | Open |
| SE-IMP-4 | Property-test finding: a complete **even-mode** bill with **no item rows** (only discount rows) | Lib answers differ: `settlePlan` says `blocked_all_zero` (the extras have no claimer), while rule Z (proportional only, as in SQL) is false and `maySettle` says `final`. The bill can't be stored: `validate_bill_rows` requires at least one item row (`20261124094657_claimable_discounts.sql`). The random generator now always makes at least one item row, and a test pins this case on purpose | Widen rule Z to "complete and `unclaimedCents ≠ 0`" in both lib and SQL | The engineering review's "equal mode always has an item claimer" holds only because of that DB check. Named here per the "can't happen must name what prevents it" lesson. If the check is ever relaxed, the pinned test fails | Open, for review |
| HM-IMP-1 | Marker type names | Kept `BillMarker = 'new' \| 'unclaimed'` and added `SettleMarker` (the 7 settle markers) and `HomeMarker = BillMarker \| SettleMarker`. `billMarker` has overloads: with two arguments it returns `BillMarker \| null` (today's type), with a plan argument it returns `HomeMarker \| null` | Widen `BillMarker` itself, as the spec's Task 7 wording says | Home (`app/(app)/index.tsx`, gated for Task 9) indexes `BILL_MARKER_COPY[marker]` with a `BillMarker`. Widening it would break the type-check in a file this task may not touch (criterion 49: typecheck green after every task). Task 9 switches Home to `HomeMarker` + `billMarkerView` | Open |
| HM-IMP-2 | `billMarkerView`'s return type | `MarkerView \| null`. It's null when `ctx.plan` doesn't give that marker for the viewer (stale or missing data) | Always return a view | D7: "never a guessed one". Home shows no pill | Open |

### 2026-11-25 · Settle early and differences, Tasks 1–2 (supabase-engineer)
Spec: `docs/specs/2026-11-25-settle-early-and-differences.md` (Amendment A1), Tasks 1 and 2. Migrations `20261125090050_settle_early.sql` and `20261125090051_settle_difference.sql`.

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SE-DB-1 | Security mode of `private.settle_state` and `private.bill_all_zero` | `security invoker`, like `private.bill_is_complete`; execute revoked from `public, anon, authenticated` | `security definer` (D10's wording: "All are security definer") | No client can call them, and their only callers (`mark_paid`, `settle_difference`) are definer functions that already run as the owner and read past RLS. Definer adds privilege without need (our rule: definer only when needed). Behavior and tests are identical either way | Open |
| SE-DB-2 | Where rule Z lives in SQL | `bill_all_zero(b)` is `settle_state(b, null) = 'all_zero'`; the rule is written once, in `settle_state`'s first branch | A second copy of the sums in `bill_all_zero` | Two copies of the same money rule could drift. `all_zero` doesn't depend on the user, so a null user answers Z alone. A test checks they agree on E1, E8–E10, E12, E17–E19 | Open |
| SE-DB-3 | `claimable_discounts.test.sql` c11 K-D1 and K-D3 | Changed: instead of expecting `mark_paid` to raise `not_complete`, each asserts `bill_is_complete = false` (C1, the original intent) **and** `settle_state(A) = 'early'`. Same assertion count; K-D2 unchanged | Leave them failing; delete them | The spec's planned-breakage list (only the K6 block of `settle_marks.test.sql`) missed them: in both states A has an item claim on a proportional bill, so rule E now accepts A's mark (K-D1 is exactly E16). The task required every file to pass | Open |
| SE-DB-4 | Unknown bill in `settle_state` | Returns null; `bill_all_zero` returns false; `mark_paid` treats null as `not_complete` and `settle_difference` as "not final" | Raise an error | Unreachable in practice (both callers reject a missing bill with 42501 before asking), but a null must never mean "may settle" | Open |
| SE-DB-5 | `settle_difference` with `p_user_id` null | `42501 not_allowed` at step 5 | `22023 mark_changed` at step 8 | Nobody may act on "no one"; step 5 is the who-may-call check | Open |
| SE-DB-6 | Criterion 14's overflow case | The table constraints allow a bill whose item rows sum above 2,147,483,647 (each item ≤ 9,999,999, no row-count limit outside `create_bill`), so the test inserts 220 rows of 9,999,999 as postgres and asserts `settle_state` answers `early` without an error. A static check also asserts no `::integer`/`::int` cast in its body | Static check only | The live check proves the bigint sums; the static one guards future edits | Open |
| SE-DB-7 | Fixture for D14/D20 ("Dee, host, not payer") | Every Task 2 bill is created by Dee, and Dee's `set_payer` makes Hana the payer before anyone marks | Hana as host and payer, Dee a plain participant | Matches the D table exactly: the host who isn't the payer is refused in both directions | Open |
| SE-DB-8 | pgTAP helpers | Small `pg_temp` functions (act as a user, build rows, create a bill, check "marked with one event") in both new test files | Inline SQL for each of the 40 cases | 20 E bills and 12 D bills would otherwise be ~600 lines of repeated setup. `pg_temp` objects vanish with the session, and the files still roll back | Open |

## 2026-11-25 · Friend-test web deploy prep (slice A1.1, `docs/before-production.md` §5; setup: [docs/setup/vercel.md](setup/vercel.md))
Implementer decisions for the public web origin and the Vercel config.

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A1.1-1 | When a bad `EXPO_PUBLIC_WEB_URL` is refused | When `lib/inviteLink.ts` loads (app start, since Expo Router loads every route), with an error naming the variable | Throw inside `inviteUrl` on each call; ignore a bad value and fall back to dev links | The bill screen catches errors from `inviteUrl` and just hides the share link, so a per-call error would be silent. Falling back would quietly ship `localhost`/`splitit://` links to friends. Failing at start matches how `lib/supabase.ts` treats bad Supabase values | Open |
| A1.1-2 | What counts as a valid origin | `https://host[:port]`: DNS-style labels (letters, digits, inner hyphens), optional port 1–65535, trailing slashes trimmed, lowercased. Refused: http, other schemes, a path, query, hash, credentials (`user@`), spaces, backslashes. IP-literal IPv6 hosts aren't accepted | Use `new URL()`; allow http for LAN testing | React Native's `URL` polyfill doesn't implement `pathname`/`search` on native, so a regex is the portable check. Http links would be blocked or warned about on phones, and the task asked for https only. IPv6 literals have no use for a Vercel address | Open |
| A1.1-3 | Where the origin applies | Every platform when set (web, iOS, Android), including the dev build on a phone | Web only | A link texted from a phone must open for a friend without the app; `splitit://` can't. Side effect: with the app installed, the https link opens the browser until universal/app links exist (§5) | Open |
| A1.1-4 | `inviteUrl` signature | Unchanged: `inviteUrl(code)`; the origin is read once from `process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_WEB_URL` (a direct member access, so Expo inlines it) | Add an `origin` parameter | Callers (bill screen → SharePanel → ContactInvite) need no change. Tests load the module fresh per env value with `jest.isolateModules` | Open |
| A1.1-5 | `vercel.json` | Kept the existing file (created 2026-11-25 by another session); added `__tests__/vercelConfig.test.ts` pinning build command, `dist`, `framework: null` and the SPA rewrite | Rewrite the file | Checked against Vercel's vercel.json docs (2026-11-25): every property and the exact `/(.*)` → `/index.html` rewrite are documented, and the filesystem is served before rewrites. The `.gz` content-type header is still unverified on the live site (noted in the setup guide) | Open |
