| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SE-T6-1 | `NoticeSnapshot.myShareCents` | Optional (`myShareCents?: number \| null`); absent reads as null. New `noticeSnapshot(input)` always fills it | Required, as the spec types it | `app/(app)/bills/[id]/settle.tsx:150` builds the snapshot by hand, and Task 6 can't touch `app/`. Required would break the type-check until Task 8. Task 8 switches the screen to `noticeSnapshot()`, then it can become required | Open |
| SE-T6-2 | `not_allowed` after a difference write (coordinator's Task 10a finding) | `actionError(error, { difference: true })`: `not_allowed` reads as `Your "I paid" just changed. Check the amount and try again.` and refetches. Without the flag it's unchanged (`You can't make that change.`, no refetch) | Map every `not_allowed` to a refetch | A stale token whose implied direction flipped is refused by the DB's direction check (step 5), before the token compare. The app always takes the direction from the mark it sends, so for the app this only happens when the mark moved under the screen: the same situation as `mark_changed`. Other writes keep today's meaning. Task 8 passes the flag from the `mark_difference` handler | Open |
| SE-T6-3 | Row pay-back control shape | `SettleRowModel.payBack: { link, record }`. `link` reuses the Request link shape (`kind: 'link'`, url, cents, handle, caption, label, a11y), so the existing open-failed and repeat-tap handling apply. `record` is a `SettleButton` with `mark_difference` | A `{ kind: 'pay_back'; url }` `SettleAction` (listed in Task 6) | Rows don't use `SettleAction` for links today. A second link shape would need its own failure tracking. So the `pay_back` action kind isn't added: nothing would produce it | Open |
| SE-T6-4 | The more-due row's Request | Reuses `SettleRowModel.request` with label `Request $1.10` and a11y `Request $1.10 more from Sam on Venmo, @sam-1` | A separate field | One Request slot per row: the unmarked request and the difference request can't both apply, and the screen's handler and `requestOpenFailedLine` already work on it | Open |
| SE-T6-5 | `payOpenFailedLine` under contract O | Still strict: shown only while the card asks for exactly that amount (unmarked share, or a "more" difference) to the same payer and username | Follow contract O (payer only) | The line tells someone how much to send, so it must be current. The "Did you pay?" prompt follows contract O; this line doesn't. After a share change the line goes away and the prompt stays (`__tests__/billScreen.test.tsx` R2 updated) | Open |
| SE-T6-6 | New card states | `more_due`, `more_opened`, `back_due`, `payer_differences`, `payer_early`, `early_not_ready`. `marked_changed` is removed (spec D6) | Reuse `blocked` for the early "no item claim" card | Criterion 67 needs to tell them apart from blocked, and the screen may want to style them differently | Open |
| SE-T6-7 | Early cards when marked but no longer able to settle (unclaimed everything after paying) | The marked card, with the early difference line (back to $0) | The "Not ready yet" card | A mark wins over the role everywhere else (3.3), and Home's H2 marker does the same, so card and pill agree (criterion 67) | Open |
| SE-T6-8 | `more_opened` / `owes_opened` when the payer's Venmo is gone after opening | The prompt and "I paid …" stay; the handle note and "Open Venmo again" are left out | Drop the prompt | Contract O keeps the prompt on payer and mark only; a missing username can't build a link but doesn't change what was sent | Open |
| SE-T6-9 | Payer footer caption with differences | Shown whenever `d > 0` on a ready plan, even while someone is unmarked (spec D6 footer row, literally) | Only when every ower is marked (the card's rule and H6) | The spec's footer table says "payer with d > 0". The card hides the line while someone is unmarked because "1 of 3 paid" already tells the story; the footer has room for only one caption, and an owed pay-back is the payer's own to-do. Flagging the difference from H6 for review | Open. **Changed by code review R2 (2026-11-25):** the payer *card* now also shows the differences line whenever `d > 0` (next to "1 of 3 paid"), so card and footer agree. Home still shows one pill, and "N left to pay" (H5) wins over "N differences to settle" (H6) while someone is unmarked (`lib/settleView.ts` `settleCard`; test "review R2" in `lib/settleView.test.ts`) |
| SE-T6-10 | SE-UX-8 even-mode line | `SettleViewInput` gains `extrasMode` (from `splitInputFor`); a blocked card on an `equal` bill adds `Tax and tip are split evenly on this bill, so shares aren't set until then.` | Leave it out (not listed in Task 6) | A1 says the brief's copy-only rows apply, and SE-UX-8 is one of them. The card is Task 6's | Open |
| SE-T6-11 | Screen tests broken by `allowEarly` and contract O | Blocked-footer and blocked-card tests set `extras_split: 'equal'` to keep their intent. Bill test 51 now expects `You can pay now · 1 discount left to claim` (E16). 50a share-changed tests use the D6 copy. Settle test 17 marks at the share. The settle `not_complete` copy is SE-UX-13. Footer R2/R3 expect the prompt to stay (contract O) | Leave them failing for Task 8 | The caller asked for a green suite. Only copy and plan-state assertions changed; no handler presses were added or changed | Open |
| SE-T6-12 | Status tone `warning` on rows | `SettleRowModel.status.tone` gains `'warning'` (the payer's `You owe $X back`) | — | Spec D6. Note for Task 8: `components/SettlePeopleList.tsx:138` draws the status icon in `colors.success` for any tone, so a warning status would show a TriangleAlert in green until Task 8 maps tone to colour | Open |

### 2026-11-25 · Settle early and differences, Task 8 (product-engineer; Settle screen, `SettleCard`, `SettlePeopleList`, bill footer)
Spec: `docs/specs/2026-11-25-settle-early-and-differences.md` (Amendment A1), Task 8; UX brief §3, §4, §7, §8; visual design §2, §3. Review item R3 (visual pass) applied in `__tests__/billScreen.test.tsx`.

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SE-T8-1 | Criterion 46 (no `fromCents`/`toCents` in `app/` or `components/`) | Two pass-through helpers added to `lib/settleView.ts`: `openedFor(action)` (Pay / Pay more → `OpenedVenmo`) and `differenceArgs(action)` (→ `settleDifference`'s arguments), with unit tests | Read the fields in the screen (not arithmetic, but the grep would match) | The criterion's grep must print nothing; two small functions keep every money field name in lib. Touches Task 6's file, so it's reported | Open |
| SE-T8-2 | Card buttons that open Venmo | One `onOpen` prop on `SettleCard` for `pay`, `pay_difference` and `request`, behind the card's 1 s guard; the list's Request and Pay back share the list's guard | One guard for every link; a guard per button | Spec: one guard for card links, one for list links. A double tap is always on the same button, and two separate guards let the payer's card and rows not block each other | Open |
| SE-T8-3 | Open-failed lines for the new links | Back-due card Request: remembered by URL, shown (`Couldn't open Venmo. Request $1.10 from @hana-1 …`) only while the card still offers that exact link. Row Pay back: remembered by URL, `Couldn't open Venmo. Send $1.10 to @sam-1 …` only while a row still offers that link. Pay more reuses `payOpenFailedLine` | Show nothing (the handle note is already under the button) | Brief §3.F lists these lines. The URL carries the handle and amount, so a changed difference or username drops the line (lesson 2026-11-25 "A 'Couldn't open Venmo' line outlived …") | Open |
| SE-T8-4 | When "Difference marked paid" / "Marked paid back" are announced | Only once the refetch that includes the write has landed (version ≥ its `clearAt`); a failed refetch announces nothing. "Marked paid back" also shows as a polite caption under the list until the next action (SE-UX-12) | Announce as soon as the rpc returns | The screen only says what the server has confirmed (its first rule); announcing before the data lands would claim a state the screen doesn't show yet | Open |
| SE-T8-5 | Card Request button style | `Button variant="secondary"` (tonal teal tint, block), per the visual pass V6 | Outline, as visual doc §2.5 describes | V6 made secondary buttons tonal app-wide and reserves outlines for inputs; the doc's point (not filled, so not "tap and you're done") still holds | Open |
| SE-T8-6 | `index.tsx` (bill footer) | No change: `SettleFooter` already renders the new `settle` captions from lib, with only an exact `Marked paid` as a live region | Add an `early` prop | The footer action carries everything; criterion 43 passes with the component as the visual pass left it | Open |
| SE-T8-7 | Early "Marked paid · N left to claim" footer caption | No check icon (plain muted text); the exact F-e `Marked paid` keeps the visual pass's check icon (P3) | Add the icon to the early caption too; remove it from F-e | Spec D6/SE-VIS-8 say footer captions have no icon; the F-e icon is the visual pass's P3 call, not this task's. Flagged for a look: the two "marked" captions now differ by an icon | Open |
| SE-T8-8 | Two existing picker tests on an incomplete bill | They now use an even-split bill (blocked), since a proportional one is early and confirms (A1.17); criterion 61 has its own tests | Change the expectation to a confirm | Keeps each test's intent ("nobody can pay yet → no confirm") and adds the new rule separately | Open |
| SE-T8-9 | Status column at large text | Status keeps `marginLeft: 'auto'` in both widths (64 min / 120 fixed), and the status text can shrink and wrap inside it | Only on the wide column | Visual doc §3.2: a status that wraps onto its own line stays flush right, which applies to short statuses too | Open |

### 2026-11-25 · Settle early and differences, Task 9 (product-engineer; `app/(app)/index.tsx`)
Spec: `docs/specs/2026-11-25-settle-early-and-differences.md` (Amendment A1), D7 and Task 9; UX brief §6; visual design §1. Q-GLYPH (no icon on pills) honoured as answered.

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SE-T9-1 | What a refresh keeps while the new settle read is in flight | The previous per-bill plans, filtered to the ids in the new `my_bills` list. A kept plan still goes through `billMarker`/`billMarkerView` with the new summary, so `New`/`Nothing claimed yet` from the new list win at once | Keep the previous pill *views* (strings) | Re-deriving from the plan means a row that became `new` never shows an old settle pill, and the a11y label and pill always come from the same data | Open |
| SE-T9-2 | A bill dropped from the candidates on refresh (e.g. its plan no longer applies) but still listed | Keeps its old pill until the new read lands, then loses it | Drop it at once | SE-UX-14 says keep each row's previous pill until the new data lands; the new read is the only thing that says the bill changed. `billMarkerView` already returns null when a kept plan doesn't give the marker | **Changed by code review R4 (2026-11-25):** a refresh keeps previous pills only for the new `settleCandidateIds`, so a bill that just became incomplete for its payer loses "Settled up" at once instead of showing it until the read lands |
| SE-T9-3 | A bill whose data arrives but can't be turned into a plan | Left out (no pill); the other bills keep theirs | Fail the whole read | Same rule as `fetchSettleData`'s per-bill parse: a missing pill is harmless, a wrong one isn't (HM-5) | Open |
| SE-T9-4 | `fetchSettleData` rejecting (it shouldn't; it never throws) | Treated like a failed read: every settle pill dropped, no error | Let it reject unhandled | An unhandled rejection would leave stale pills up with no path to clear them | Open |
| SE-T9-5 | Pill wrapping at large text | `maxWidth: '100%'` on the pill so a long pill wraps inside the row (visual §1.2 "never truncates; wraps inside the pill") | Rely on the row's `alignItems: 'flex-start'` alone | Makes the wrap explicit on web as well as native; no visual change at default sizes | Open |
| SE-T9-6 | Test seam for "after the web OAuth redirect lands on /" | Opening on `/?code=…` with the session already present (what `detectSessionInUrl` leaves), on native and web | Driving the real sign-in flow | The fake's `exchangeCodeForSession` signs in a fixed non-UUID user (`user-a`), so the settle fixtures (built for B) can't follow it. The redirect path's risk here is Home navigating or reading twice, which this test checks | Open |

## 2026-11-25 · Settle early, code-review fixes (spec A1)

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SE-R-5 | Bill-screen footer when a Pay was opened and the bill then turns early (the host adds a row while the person is in Venmo) | The footer keeps "Did you pay Hana?" with I paid recording the opened amount, following contract O like the Settle card (criterion 59). The screen's own "N left to claim" count doesn't replace it either. Decided by the user | Drop to the early "You can pay now · N left to claim" footer (SE-9's "no Pay in the footer before complete") | The person may already have sent that amount in Venmo; losing the prompt on the bill screen while Settle up still shows it would make them hunt for where to record it. This adds no Pay button to an early footer, so SE-9 still holds for starting a payment | Kept (user, 2026-11-25) |

## 2026-11-25 · Invite QR (share panel; `components/InviteQr.tsx`)
Made by the product-engineer while adding a QR code of the invite link to the "Invite friends" card. The QR encodes exactly the string `inviteUrl(code)` returns (passed in as SharePanel's `url`); nothing rebuilds the link.

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| IQ-1 | Where the toggle sits | A quiet `TextLink` with a QR icon, "Show QR code", under the Copy link / Share… / From contacts row (and its "Link copied" line), above "Make a new link". The QR folds out right under it | Directly under the link text, above the buttons; a fourth button in the row | Opening a 248-pt tile between the link and its buttons would push Copy/Share off screen. The button row is the "send it" group; scanning is the "show it" alternative, one step quieter | Open |
| IQ-2 | Shown by default? | Folded; the same link toggles to "Hide QR code" (`accessibilityState.expanded`) | Always shown; shown only while the host is alone on the bill | Opt-in like the other quiet extras ("simple and uncluttered"); most invites go by text. One control, one place | Open |
| IQ-3 | Who sees it | Every participant (same as Copy link / Share…) | Host only | Any participant can share the link today | Open |
| IQ-4 | After "Make a new link" | Stays open and redraws for the new link | Close it | The host made a new link to show it; the old QR stops working, so it must never stay on screen | Open |
| IQ-5 | Colours | Fixed `qrColors` in `lib/theme.ts`: modules `#111827` on `#FFFFFF` (17.7:1) in both light and dark mode, with a 24-pt light quiet zone (≈ 4 modules at the deploy URL's 33×33 code) and a hairline edge | Theme colours (inverted in dark mode) | Many phone cameras can't read light-on-dark codes; the spec needs a light quiet zone. A bright tile in dark mode is the trade-off | Open |
| IQ-6 | Size and error correction | 200-pt code, error correction "M" | Larger / "H" | Fits a 320-pt phone card; "M" keeps modules large (≈ 6 pt) and still survives glare | Open |
| IQ-7 | Screen reader | The tile is one image: "QR code for the invite link"; the caption "Friends can scan this with their phone camera." is read after it. The URL is not repeated (the link text right above is read and stays selectable) | Put the URL in the image label | Saying a 59-character URL twice is noise | Open |
| IQ-8 | Library | `react-native-qrcode-svg` 6.3.54 (exact pin) over the already-installed `react-native-svg` 15.15.4 (SDK 57's version). Pure JS, so web picks it up on the next Vercel deploy | A hand-written encoder; `qrcode` + our own SVG path | Maintained (published Nov 2026), RN >= 0.63 / svg >= 14 peers, no native code of its own. Its `text-encoding` dependency prints an npm "no longer maintained" warning; it's only for RN < 0.75 and isn't loaded at runtime here | Open |
| IQ-9 | Dev links on native | When `EXPO_PUBLIC_WEB_URL` is unset, the QR shows the dev link (`splitit://b/<code>`) like the text does | Hide the QR for non-https links | It only happens in dev; a friend's camera opens it only if they have the dev build. With the Vercel URL set it's always `https://…/b/<code>` | Open |

## 2026-11-25 · Settle early, QA fix F-1 (`lib/settleView.ts` `settlePeople`)

| ID | Decision | Chosen | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SE-QA-1 | The Everyone list's aside (non-payers, ready plan) when every ower has marked but a difference is still open | "All paid" only when there are no open differences (`d === 0`, the payer card's and Home pill's rule). Otherwise the aside shows the payer card's own wording, "1 difference to settle" / "N differences to settle". With someone still unmarked it stays "N of M paid" | No aside while differences are open | "All paid" above "You owe $1.10 more" contradicts itself. Reusing the payer card's line keeps one phrase for one state across the screen, and tells the guest why the bill isn't done; an empty aside would leave them guessing. No new copy | Open |

## 2026-11-26 · Universal Links / App Links prep (native-mobile; guide: [docs/setup/app-links.md](setup/app-links.md))
Prepared so `https://<host>/b/<code>` opens the installed app on `app/b/[code].tsx`. Everything is off until build-time values exist (paid Apple team, Android fingerprints).

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| AL-1 | How native config is switched | `app.config.ts` extends `app.json` (still the base) through `withAppLinks` in `lib/appLinks.ts`; adds `ios.associatedDomains` + `ios.appleTeamId` only with `APPLE_TEAM_ID`, the Android `autoVerify` filter only with `ANDROID_CERT_SHA256`, both only with `EXPO_PUBLIC_WEB_URL` for the host | Always add them in `app.json`; a separate EAS-only profile | The free Personal Team can't sign Associated Domains, so an always-on entitlement would break today's `npx expo run:ios`. Unset → output is exactly `app.json` (tested) | Open |
| AL-2 | Also set `ios.appleTeamId` from `APPLE_TEAM_ID` | Yes | Leave team choice to Xcode | The same value that turns on the entitlement picks the team that can sign it, so `expo run:ios` uses the paid team without a manual Xcode step | Open |
| AL-3 | TypeScript in `app.config.ts` imports | `import 'tsx/cjs'` (Expo's documented way; `tsx` 4.23.15 is already an exact-pinned devDependency) | Duplicate the logic in plain JS | One tested implementation shared by the native config and the well-known script. `tsx` must stay a devDependency, and EAS/Vercel installs must include devDependencies (`npm ci` does by default) | Open |
| AL-4 | Well-known script language | `scripts/write-well-known.ts` run with `tsx` from `prestart` / `preweb` / `preexport:web` | `.mjs` like the Tesseract copy | Reuses `lib/appLinks.ts` (same validation and file shapes as the app config) | Open |
| AL-5 | When a variable is unset | Its file is deleted from `public/.well-known/` (and the empty folder removed); a malformed value fails the build | Leave old files; publish placeholders | Nothing wrong or stale is ever published (task requirement) | Open |
| AL-6 | AASA format | Current `applinks.details[]` with `appIDs` + `components: [{ "/": "/b/*" }]`, no legacy `apps`/`paths` | Add the legacy `appID`/`paths` entry too | The app needs iOS 16.4+, and `components` has been the format since iOS 13 | Open |
| AL-7 | Android path match | `pathPrefix: "/b/"` | `pathPattern: "/b/.*"` | Same result for our route; `pathPrefix` is Expo's documented example | Open |
| AL-8 | Vercel rewrite | `/:path((?!\\.well-known/).*)` → `/index.html` (every other path still rewritten, so `/b/<code>` doesn't 404), plus `Content-Type: application/json` for both files | Keep `/(.*)` (real files are served first anyway) | Vercel serves real files before rewrites, so the files would work either way; excluding `/.well-known/` makes a missing file a 404 instead of `index.html` with 200, which Apple/Google would read as a broken file. Only checked with a regex test, not Vercel's matcher | Open |
| AL-9 | Fingerprint input | Comma-separated, colon or plain 64-hex, any case; normalized to upper-case colon form and de-duplicated; SHA-1 rejected | Colon form only | People paste from `keytool`, Play Console and EAS, which format differently | Open |
| AL-10 | Host with a port in `EXPO_PUBLIC_WEB_URL` | Fails the build, but only when app-link variables are set | Ignore the port | Both platforms match the host on 443; a dev URL with a port never fails a build that isn't using links | Open |

## 2026-11-26 · App banner ("Get the app" on the website; guide: [docs/setup/app-links.md](setup/app-links.md#get-the-app-banner-on-the-website-added-2026-11-26))
Code: `lib/appBanner.ts`, `components/AppBanner.tsx`, mounted in `app/_layout.tsx` (web only). Off until a store URL is set.

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| AB-1 | Where it's mounted | Root layout, web only, as a bar above every screen that pushes content down | Only on the join route `app/b/[code].tsx`; an overlay | A signed-out invitee is sent from `/b/<code>` to `/sign-in` straight away, so a join-route banner would flash and vanish. A bar above the screens never covers content. It has no router access and never navigates, so the resume effect stays the only post-sign-in navigation (tested on `/b/<code>` signed out, deep link signed in, and `/` after the web OAuth redirect) | Open |
| AB-2 | When no store URL is set (today) | No banner at all | A "coming soon" bar; a link to the website | A button that goes nowhere is clutter. Nothing to get yet | Open |
| AB-3 | Only one platform's store set | Only that platform gets the bar | Send everyone to the one store | Never send an iPhone to Google Play | Open |
| AB-4 | "Open in app" | Left out | `splitit://b/<code>`; Android `intent://` with a Play fallback | iOS Safari shows "address is invalid" without the app, with no reliable way to check first. People with the app get it through Universal/App Links before reaching the site. Android's intent link would duplicate "Get the app" for people without the app | Open |
| AB-5 | How long ✕ hides it | 14 days, stored as a timestamp only (`splitit.appBanner.dismissedAt` in localStorage); a time in the future or junk counts as not dismissed | 7 days; 30 days; forever; this visit only | Long enough not to nag someone who's chosen the website, short enough to remind a regular once the app is out. Not per-user data, so it's kept across sign-out; blocked storage still hides it for the visit | Open |
| AB-6 | Who counts as a phone | iPhone/iPad/iPod or Android in the user agent, plus a "Mac" with touch points (iPadOS asks for desktop sites). Android tablets included | Screen width | Width can't tell a small laptop window from a phone, and the store is per OS anyway | Open |
| AB-7 | Bad store URL | Web: the app stops at start with an error naming the variable (like `lib/env.ts`). Native: not read at all | Hide the banner quietly | Same fail-fast rule as the other `EXPO_PUBLIC_*` values; native never shows the bar, so a web-only typo can't break the app. Allowed: `https://apps.apple.com/…/id<digits>` and `https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=<package>` only | Open |
| AB-8 | Look | Surface-colored bar with a hairline; 32-pt teal tile with a phone glyph; one caption line; tonal (not solid) "Get the app"; ✕ as a 44-pt icon button labelled "Continue on website" | Solid primary button; a separate "Continue on website" text button; bold line | The screen below keeps the one solid primary button; one dismiss control, not two. Caption keeps the line to two lines on a 320-pt phone | Open |
| AB-9 | Icon | Stand-in phone glyph | `assets/images/icon.png` | Every icon in `assets/` is still Expo's default placeholder (the "A" on blue grid), and the PNG is 800 KB. Swap in the real icon when it exists (`docs/before-production.md` §5) | Open |
| AB-10 | Apple's Smart App Banner | Documented, not built | Add the meta tag now | Needs an App Store ID and a build step writing it into `public/index.html` (Safari reads it only at load). Note in the guide; hide our bar in iOS Safari if it's added | Open |
| AB-11 | Safe area (notch/status bar) while the bar shows | The bar pads for the top inset (and left/right, for landscape notches) inside its own background; everything below it gets the same insets with `top: 0`, through a `SafeAreaInsetsContext.Provider` around the Stack in `components/AppBanner.tsx`. Hidden: insets pass through unchanged. The tree keeps one shape, so dismissing never remounts screens | A flag screens read to skip the top inset; leave it (bar under the notch, then Home adds the inset again: double padding) | Matters once `viewport-fit=cover` makes `env(safe-area-inset-top)` non-zero (home-screen web apps, some landscape cases). Screens using `useSafeAreaInsets`/`SafeAreaView` (web) fix themselves with nothing to check. `SafeAreaFrameContext` is left as is (no screen reads it). Tested with a 47-pt inset in `__tests__/appBanner.test.tsx` | Open |

## 2026-11-26 · Home mobile refresh (spec: [docs/specs/2026-11-26-home-mobile-refresh.md](specs/2026-11-26-home-mobile-refresh.md))
PM decisions on the scope the user approved on 2026-11-26 (sticky Scan bar, clearer rows, "Waiting on you" / "Recent", installed feel on mobile web).

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| HR-1 | What the sticky bar holds | "Split a bill" as a small section heading (header role kept), the "Scan receipt" primary button, and the "Type items instead" link. The intro line "Take a photo of the receipt, then check the items it found." is removed | Keep heading + intro at the top of the scroll and only the button in the bar; heading + intro + button in the bar | Keeps the screen-reader landmark (review R2) next to the action it names, and the bar short (about 100 pt plus the safe area). The intro was first-run help; the Scan screen's choice cards already explain both paths. *Note 2026-11-26 (after the build):* the built bar is about 146 pt plus the bottom safe area, not about 100; user check U1 looks at whether that leaves enough room for the list on a small phone | Open |
| HR-2 | Where "Type items instead" goes | Straight to a blank `/bill` (clears any stored scan result first, as the Scan screen's "Enter items" does) | `/scan`, which shows the two choice cards again | The link exists to skip scanning; going through `/scan` adds a tap and repeats the choice | Open |
| HR-3 | When the amount column shows your share | Only for a guest (not the host, not the payer) on a bill whose settle plan is `ready` (complete), with a share ≥ $0. Otherwise the bill total. Every amount has a small label under it: "Your share" or "Total" | Total always (the "You owe $X" pill already carries the share); also in `early` mode; label only the share rows | Uses only data Home already loads (the settle plan), no backend. `early` shares can still move by a cent as others claim. Labelling every row stops a guest reading the bill total as what they owe | Open |
| HR-4 | The share repeats the "You owe $X" pill | Accepted | Show "Total" on rows whose pill already names an amount | One rule for the amount column is easier to read than one that depends on the pill. Cheap to change if U1 finds it noisy | Open |
| HR-5 | Where "4 people" comes from | A new client read: `bill_participants.bill_id` for the listed bills (≤ 20), counted in `lib/`. No migration | Add `participant_count` to `my_bills()` (migration; `supabase/` is owned by the deploy session now; Q2 asks the user about later); load full settle data for every bill; leave the count out | The user asked for the caption. RLS already lets participants read these rows, so it reveals nothing new | Open |
| HR-6 | People wording | `Just you` for 1, `<n> people` otherwise (host included); unknown → the date alone | `1 person`; leave out the host | "Just you" says it's not shared yet; counting everyone matches the bill screen's people list | Open |
| HR-7 | Counts during a refresh or after a failed read | Keep the previous counts for bills still listed; a failed read keeps them too, with no error | Drop them on failure (like settle pills, HM-5) | A count that's one refresh stale is harmless; a caption that flickers between "4 people" and nothing on a flaky connection is noise. Settle pills drop because a wrong amount isn't harmless | Open |
| HR-8 | What counts as "Waiting on you" | The pill shown on the row is one of: New, Nothing claimed yet, You owe $X, Paid · $X more to pay, N differences to settle | Only the accent-tone pills (leaves out "Nothing claimed yet", which is muted) | The user's examples included "items left to claim". Derived from the same view as the pill, so a row's section and its pill can't disagree. The pill's tone stays as the settle-early work set it | Superseded by GV-1 and GV-17 (2026-11-27, Home groups v2: the group comes from the balance, not the pill). Previously Open |
| HR-9 | Order inside each section | The list's order (newest joined first, from `my_bills()`) | New bills first; owes first | Same order as before the split; rows don't reshuffle when a marker changes inside a section | Open |
| HR-10 | Headings when there are bills | "Waiting on you" and "Recent" replace "Your bills"; an empty section is hidden. Loading, error and no bills keep "Your bills" and today's text | Keep "Your bills" above both sections | Three headings for one list is clutter; empty-state redesign is out of scope | Superseded by GV-13, GV-14 and GV-18 (2026-11-27: "Recent" removed; the headings are Waiting on you, Owed to you and the Settled toggle, plus the all-caught-up card). "Your bills" for loading, error and no bills still holds. Previously Open |
| HR-11 | First load before settle data lands | The list shows at once (SE-UX-14 kept); a bill whose pill comes from settle data can move from "Recent" to "Waiting on you" a moment later | Hold the list until the settle read lands or a short timeout | Reversing SE-UX-14 would slow the list people came for. Risk 1 in the spec; U1 step 3 checks whether the move is noticeable | Superseded by GV-24 and GV-28 (2026-11-27; the user chose the alternative, A1.M3: the first load waits up to 1000 ms). Previously Open |
| HR-12 | Row screen-reader label | `Hana's bill, Nov 20, 4 people, your share $14.80. You owe $14.80`: commas, the people count, the amount word; the pill's words last | Keep the old " · " separators and wording | Commas read as pauses; " · " can be read aloud as "dot". The label says everything the row shows, in order (DESIGN.md) | Open |
| HR-13 | Manifest `display` | `browser` | `standalone` (feels installed: no Safari bar); `minimal-ui` | On iOS a home-screen web app keeps its own storage, separate from Safari, so a friend signed in in Safari would be signed out there, and invite links from Messages open in Safari anyway. Google sign-in in standalone isn't tested. Revisit before launch (`docs/before-production.md`). Platform note: Android Chrome honours `display`; iOS may open home-screen sites as web apps depending on version (U3 records it) | Open |
| HR-14 | `theme-color` values | The page background (`colors.light.background` / `colors.dark.background`), the same colours `public/index.html` already paints | Paper white; Settle Teal | The browser bar should match the top of the page (Home's header sits on the background). Teal would be a second loud surface (One Voice Rule) | Open |
| HR-15 | Home-screen and manifest icons | The existing placeholder (`assets/images/icon.png`) resized to 180, 192 and 512 px | No icon; a temporary teal tile now | No real icon exists (AB-9). Swapping three files later is cheap. Q1 asks the user | Open |
| HR-16 | 16px inputs | On web only, the `subtle` `TextField` (the item note, 13 px) uses body size (16 px). Native unchanged. Every other field is already ≥ 16 px | A global CSS `input { font-size: 16px !important }` in `index.html`; 16 px on native too | The fix lives in the one component that renders inputs, so it can't drift. `!important` fights react-native-web's styles. Native has no zoom-on-focus | Open |
| HR-17 | Tap highlight | `html { -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent }` in `public/index.html` too | Rely on react-native-web's runtime rule only | Covers the page before JS loads, and makes the choice visible in our own file | Open |
| HR-18 | Counts in section headings | None ("Waiting on you", not "Waiting on you (2)") | Add a count | Calm; the rows are right there | Open |

## 2026-11-26 · Sent via Venmo and payment methods (spec: [docs/specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo.md](specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo.md))
PM decisions for the plan stage. The user reviews the plan, UX, visual and eng docs before any code. Full reasoning and alternatives are in the spec's "Decisions (SV-)" table. Rows marked **needs your look** are listed at the end of the spec.

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SV-1 | Recording the auto-send | `mark_paid` / `settle_difference` with a method argument; amount = the opened amount | New `auto_mark` function | One tested write path | Open; amended by SV-ENG-3 (A1: returns `created`) |
| SV-2 | Old signatures | Dropped, replaced with method-taking ones | 3-arg wrapper | One function per rule; nothing released | Open, needs your look; deploy rule SV-ENG-23 |
| SV-3 | Auto vs manual flag | None; method `venmo` is written only by the auto path | `auto` column; new event kind | The wording needs only the method | Open |
| SV-4 | Method after mixed history | Same method kept, a different one → `other` ("Marked paid") | First or latest method | Never claims the net went one way when it didn't | Open |
| SV-5 | Native order | Write first (4 s limit), then open Venmo | Open first | iOS suspends JS once Venmo is in front | Amended by SV-ENG-2 (A1: record → schedule → open) |
| SV-6 | Write fails before open | Refusals: don't open. Network/timeout: open, then "Did you send…?" fallback | Always / never open | No money to a stale payer; flaky network doesn't block paying | Open; amended by SV-ENG-3 (`created = false` → don't open) |
| SV-7 | Open fails after write | Undo the mark | Leave it | Don't say "Sent" when Venmo never opened | Open |
| SV-8 | "Pay more" on native | Open first, then write | Write first | Sam can't lower his own mark to reverse it | Open |
| SV-9 | Web | Auto-mark only when `window.open` reports the popup opened; else ask on return | Always auto-mark; always ask | Never say something untrue about money | Replaced by SV-ENG-13 (A1) |
| SV-10 | Payer's Undo vs Not received | Merged: Not received replaces the payer's Undo | Both on the row | One control; Sam gets told | Open, changes Q4 |
| SV-11 | Confirm on Not received | Inline confirm | None | Destructive to someone else's record | Open |
| SV-12 | Pay-back automatic | No | Yes | User named "Pay more" only | Open |
| SV-13 | Undo after an auto "more" | Clears the whole mark, with a confirm | Partial reversal | Sam can't lower his own mark (SE-5) | Moot after SV-ENG-4 (no check-in for "more"); `Undo all $X` (SV-UX-18) stays |
| SV-14 | "I already paid" | Stays as "Paid another way" (method `other`) | Becomes cash; removed | Zelle/PayPal users need a truthful option | Kept (user approved Venmo / Cash / Paid another way, 2026-11-26) |
| SV-15 | Delays | Check-in 60 s (user); inline question up to 30 min (starting value) | No limit | Avoid asking about old payments on a new device | 60 s kept (user, SV-ENG-29); 30 min open |
| SV-16 | Permission timing | First Venmo Pay tap, before Venmo opens, with our line on the card | After return; first launch | "When first needed" (user) | Open; amended by SV-ENG-20 (full authorization, `ios.status`) |
| SV-17 | Permission denied | No re-ask, no nag; inline question still works | Settings nudge | Inline covers it | Open |
| SV-18 | Remembered method | Device-local per user, venmo/cash only | `profiles` column | A column would be visible to co-participants | Open; key format SV-ENG-1 |
| SV-19 | "Undone" notice after Not received | Not shown; card warning instead | Both | One message per event | Open |
| SV-20 | When Hana's update clears | `mark_bill_seen` on open and when the bill/Settle screen loses focus | On open only | She may watch it arrive live | Replaced by SV-ENG-7 (A1) |
| SV-21 | Marker priority | new > unclaimed > differences > sent > left_to_pay/settled | Other orders | An owed pay-back is an action | Amended by SV-ENG-17 (user: `sent` only while someone owes; never replaces `settled`) |
| SV-22 | Differences on Home | Activity only | New `settled_updated_at` column | Rare; needs a column | Open |
| SV-23 | Age-off | 72 h (starting value) | 24 h; 7 days | "A few days" (user) | Dropped (user, 2026-11-26; SV-ENG-9) |
| SV-24 | Count ≥ 2 copy | "2 people sent you money" | "2 people paid you" | User's wording; not untrue | Open |
| SV-25 | Not-received line after a payer change | Shown only while `not_received_by` is the current payer | Clear in `set_payer` | No extra `set_payer` change | Reversed by SV-ENG-14 (A1) |
| SV-26 | Payer's direct `unmark_paid` | Kept, recorded | Revoke | Q4 change beyond the slice; direct-caller only | Open |
| SV-27 | Lock-screen text | Payer name + amount | Generic text | User's wording; Sam's own phone | Open, privacy: needs your look; amended by SV-UX-6 / SV-ENG-21 (hidden-preview placeholder) |
| SV-28 | Notification taps signed out / other user | Ignored; one handler, never during the sign-in resume | Pending action | Shared devices; M0 resume rule | Open; amended by SV-ENG-10 (resume gate) |
| SV-29 | Chooser when payer has no Venmo | Hidden; Cash only | Venmo disabled | Fewer elements | Open |
| SV-30 | Not-received storage | 3 columns on `bill_participants` | Derive from Activity | Store what the UI reloads | Open; `not_received_by` = the `to_user` (SV-ENG-16) |

### 2026-11-26 · Sent via Venmo, UX brief (brief: [docs/specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo-ux.md](specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo-ux.md))
Decided by the PM under the "build straight through" approval. The brief goes to the visual designer and the staff engineer next. Rows tagged **changes the spec** need a dated amendment in the spec before Tasks 1, 6, 7 and 9 start.

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SV-UX-1 | Chooser placement and label | `SegmentedControl` "Venmo \| Cash" between the head lines and the primary; no visible label (a11y radio group `How you're paying`); hidden with one method; disabled while busy; `Paid another way` under both | Visible "How you're paying" heading; chooser above the amount | "Venmo \| Cash" under "You owe Hana $15.10" explains itself; a heading adds a line to every card. Copy/layout only | Placement adopted (A1); width replaced by SV-VIS-1 / SV-ENG-26 (hug) |
| SV-UX-2 | Not-received line on Sam's card | First line under the amount, warning tone; venmo copy `Hana hasn't received your $15.10. Check Venmo before paying again.` (cash/other variants) | Above the label; the spec's "Check Venmo." | Keeps the label/amount slot fixed. "Before paying again" prevents a double payment when the money did go. Copy/layout only | Open |
| SV-UX-3 | Completion line | `You're all set. Hana can see it.` on ready, no-difference marked cards, every method; hidden while the inline check shows; early and difference cards keep their own lines | No completion line | The user asked to celebrate completion; it's true (Hana's row shows it) and isn't a money claim. "All set" would be untrue on early cards. Copy/layout only | Open |
| SV-UX-4 | Confirmation after Pay | No toast. Card label + amount ("You sent Hana / $15.10") + `Sent via Venmo`; footer `Sent to Hana · $15.10`; iOS announces `Sent to Hana, $15.10` | A toast or overlay "Sent!" | Sam is in Venmo when it would show; the lasting card is what he sees on return. No-modal rule. Copy/layout only | Open |
| SV-UX-5 | Inline question wording on the card | Card: `Did it go through?`; footer and notification keep `Did your $15.10 to Hana go through?`. Yes → completion line, announce `You're all set`; No, undo → back to the owes card, announce `Undone. Not marked as sent.` | Full sentence everywhere | Name and amount sit right above it on the card; the repeat wraps on a phone. Copy/layout only | Open |
| SV-UX-6 | Notification sound and lock-screen preview | No sound; iOS hidden-preview placeholder `Check your SplitIt payment`; no item names, bill name or handles. User's title/body/actions unchanged | Default sound; a generic body for everyone | Privacy-aware without changing the user's fixed wording: people who hide previews get the generic text. A chime a minute after paying feels like a nag. **Changes the spec** (D3 content; Task 1 verifies both option names) | Adopted in A1 (D3; SV-ENG-21); option name verified in Task 8 |
| SV-UX-7 | Permission pre-prompt | No extra screen; note `We'll check back in a minute to make sure it went through.` under the Pay button on the card and in the footer's notes, native, only while undetermined | A pre-permission screen; the spec's "a minute later" wording | One line, seen wherever the first Pay tap happens. Copy/layout only | Open |
| SV-UX-8 | Permission denied | Nothing visible changes; no "turn on" link | A Settings nudge | Inline question covers it. Confirms spec (SV-17) | Open |
| SV-UX-9 | Cash flow | One tap, no confirm; `Paid in cash` + completion line + `Undo`; announce `Marked paid in cash` | A confirm step | Undo is right there. Confirms spec | Open |
| SV-UX-10 | Footer with Cash selected (or no payer Venmo), ready plan | The footer offers the card's primary: `I paid Hana $15.10 in cash` + Details. Early stays "Settle up" | Spec: "Settle up" primary | Cash payers otherwise need two screens while Venmo payers need one tap. **Changes the spec** (D5 footer; criteria 37–39 gain a footer case) | Adopted in A1 (D5 footer, criterion 39) |
| SV-UX-11 | Footer after Not received | Primary `Settle up` + warning caption `Hana hasn't received your $15.10`; no Pay footer in that state | Spec: the Pay footer | Sam should see why on the card before money moves again. **Changes the spec** (D5 footer; criterion 42) | Adopted in A1 (D4, D5 footer, criterion 42) |
| SV-UX-12 | Footer captions by method | Ready marked: `Sent to Hana · $15.10` / `Paid Hana in cash · $15.10` / `Marked paid · $15.10` (live). Early marked: `<method words> · N left to claim`. Payer all settled: secondary + `Everyone's settled up` | Spec: `Sent via Venmo` / `Paid in cash` / `Marked paid` only | The bill screen shows no amount near the footer; the payer's finish deserves a word too. **Changes the spec** (D5 footer table) | Adopted in A1 (D5 footer table) |
| SV-UX-13 | Not-received confirm and result | `Didn't get Sam's $15.10? This clears it and lets Sam know.` (`Not received` danger / `Cancel`); after: `Not yet` + `Not received · $15.10`; announce `Marked not received. Sam will see it.` | Spec's "Mark Sam's $15.10 as not received? Sam will be asked to check." | Asks Hana's actual question and states both effects plainly. Copy/layout only | Open |
| SV-UX-14 | Aggregate wording | Counts say "settled up": `1 of 3 settled up`, `1 settled up so far`, aside `All settled up`, payer label `Everyone's settled up. Nice.` + method line `2 sent via Venmo · 1 paid in cash` (one ower: `Sam sent it via Venmo`) | The user's example "Everyone's paid. Nice."; keep "N of M paid" | Venmo auto-marks are "sent", and the user's rule is never to say something untrue about money; "settled up" is the app's own status word. **Departs from the user's example wording** (flagged). Copy/layout only | Adopted in A1 (SV-ENG-28); **needs your look** (wording departure) |
| SV-UX-15 | Sam's Home pill after Not received | `Not received · $15.10` (accent, Waiting on you). `my_bills()` also returns the caller's own `not_received_cents` / `not_received_by`; `billMarker` gives `not_received` when it's the current payer's flag and the viewer is unmarked. One `WAITING_MARKERS` entry in `lib/homeSections.ts` needs splitit-app-96's OK; if declined, the spec's `You owe $15.10` | Spec: plain `You owe $15.10` | Sam gets no notification; without this, a bill he thought was done reappears as "You owe" with no reason. Own row, no new exposure. **Changes the spec** (D7, D8 `my_bills`, Task 7) | Adopted in A1, amended: data from settle data, not `my_bills` (SV-ENG-8); group from the balance in Home groups v2, no `WAITING_MARKERS` line (SV-ENG-18) |
| SV-UX-16 | Home "sent" pill | Muted, no icon, stays in Recent, list order unchanged; `other` copy `Sam paid you $15.10` (a11y `Sam says they paid you $15.10`) | Accent tone; float news to the top; `Sam marked $15.10 paid` | Colour means "your move" on Home (SE-VIS-1); a payer's row is already in Recent, so nothing jumps sections. Copy/layout only | Copy and tone kept; placement superseded (A1): no Recent, `sent` in Owed to you only while someone owes (SV-ENG-17) |
| SV-UX-17 | Open-failed copy | Adds `, then tap Paid another way.` (native open failure and web blocked popup) | Unchanged | Tells the person how to record a payment they had to make by hand. Copy/layout only | Open |
| SV-UX-18 | Undo on difference cards, and the more-send undo confirm | `Undo all $15.10` for every method; confirm `Undo all $16.20? You can only undo the whole amount, not just the $1.10.` (`Undo all` / `Keep it`) | `Undo "I paid"`; spec SV-13's `This clears your whole "Sent" of $16.20` | Says exactly what it clears, for any method. Copy/layout only | `Undo all $X` kept; the more-send confirm is unreachable after SV-ENG-4 (no check-in for "more") |
| SV-UX-19 | Undo rule "changed" message | `Your payment to Hana changed since then, so nothing was undone. Check below.` | Spec's version without "so nothing was undone" | Says whether anything happened. Copy/layout only | Open |
| SV-UX-20 | Contract O fallback card | Primary `Yes, I sent it`, handle note, ghost `Open Venmo again` (opens only); footer adds `Not yet` | Remove "Open Venmo again" | Same shape as today's 3.2. Confirms spec | Open |
| SV-UX-21 | Inline question and screen readers | Not a live region; Yes / No, undo labels carry the context (`Yes, it went through`; `No, undo. It didn't go through`) | Live question | It appears with the live "Sent via Venmo" line; two announcements would talk over each other. Copy/layout only | Open |
| SV-UX-22 | More-due summary line | `You've sent $15.10.` (venmo) / `You've paid $15.10.` (cash, other) | `You've marked $15.10 paid.` | Accurate per method, plainer. Copy/layout only | Open |

### 2026-11-26 · Sent via Venmo, visual design (doc: [docs/specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo-visual.md](specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo-visual.md))
Decided by the visual designer. No new colours, sizes or spacing values. Contrast numbers are light / dark on `surface` unless stated. §10 of the doc lists the model and primitive changes for the eng design; §11 lists the proposals that need a copy or behaviour decision (P1–P4).

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SV-VIS-1 | Method chooser control | Existing `SegmentedControl`, **hugging** (not stretched), left-aligned, today's solid-teal selected look, no check icon; it opens the action group: 16 above it, 12 to the primary | Full-width segmented (brief SV-UX-1); a tinted selected segment; chips; "Pay with: Venmo ▾" link | Full width puts a ~153-pt solid teal thumb right above the teal button, so it reads as a second button. A tint is 1.02 / 1.05 against the track (why VP-39 replaced VP-6). The link hides the choice and adds a tap for cash payers. Hugging, the thumb is ~73 pt: a setting, not an action. Selected indicator 4.92 / 8.20 vs track | Adopted in A1 (SV-ENG-26); departs from the brief's "full card width" |
| SV-VIS-2 | Chooser states | `disabled` while a card write is in flight (0.5, not pressable); radio semantics (`radiogroup` "How you're paying", `radio` segments) | Leave the segments as `button` + `selected` | The brief asks for both; the primitive has neither. The radio part changes a11y semantics, so it's the engineer's call | Adopted (SV-ENG-24: opt-in radio semantics, Task 12) |
| SV-VIS-3 | What "done" looks like | The primary disappears; one success line (check from tone); a muted completion line 4 pt under it in the head group; one quiet ghost Undo. No tinted block, toast or green label | A success Banner; a green completion line | Less on screen is the strongest "done" signal. One green line per card; the warm sentence doesn't need colour | Open |
| SV-VIS-4 | Payer's finish | Only the label's words change (`Everyone's settled up. Nice.`); the amount stays as the anchor; the method line is the card's one success line; `1 of 3 settled up` stays muted until then | A larger label; drop the amount | Noticed without movement; the collected total is what Hana came for | Open |
| SV-VIS-5 | Inline "Did it go through?" | Own block after the head group: muted 13-pt question, then `Yes` and `No, undo` as flush ghosts 16 apart, on the text edge. No box. Card Undo hidden meanwhile. More-send confirm = the existing inline warning Banner | A tinted prompt card; a footer-style primary `Yes` | Quiet but findable: the only teal on the card at that point. No default answer needs pushing, since ignoring it is "yes" | Open |
| SV-VIS-6 | Short flush labels sit off the edge | `Button` flush content aligns to the start (card) or the end (row controls); box stays ≥ 44 × 44 | Leave centred (`Yes` ~8 pt in, `Undo` 4 pt) | A label that's indented next to a left-aligned caption looks like a bug | Adopted (SV-ENG-24, Task 12) |
| SV-VIS-7 | Motion | None on arrival. One optional 200-ms ease-out layout transition (Reanimated `LinearTransition`, native only) when the inline question is answered, so the Everyone card doesn't snap; instant under Reduce Motion (`ReduceMotion.System`) and on web | Fade-ins; a success pulse; haptics | Sam is in Venmo when the card changes, so an entrance would replay on every visit. The transition only smooths a 44-pt jump | Open |
| SV-VIS-8 | Payer row method statuses | `Sent via Venmo` / `Paid in cash` / `Paid` all success tone (5.69 / 10.69) with the check from tone; the existing 120-pt status column whenever any row shows a method status | One colour per method; keep `minWidth: 64` | Words say how, colour only says settled. ~115-pt statuses next to `Not yet` would make the money column drift | Adopted (SV-ENG-25: `wideStatus` covers method statuses) |
| SV-VIS-9 | "Not received" control | `TextLink` (13/600 teal), own line, flush right, where the payer's Undo was; `marginTop: space.sm` when another control sits above it (12 between targets); ≥ 40 pt from any target below; the existing inline confirm. `TextLink` gains `minWidth: 44`, `disabled`, `loading` | Ghost button like Undo/Request (brief) | A rare correction shouldn't be the loudest repeated thing on a list of good news; still teal and always in one place. Spacing plus the confirm guard against mis-taps. 5.47 / 9.29 | Adopted (SV-ENG-24/25: `TextLink` options in Task 12, row `notReceived` slot) |
| SV-VIS-10 | Sam's not-received line | First head line, warning 13/600 with TriangleAlert from tone (7.52 / 11.18); no banner, no tint | A warning Banner above the card | Calm, says what to do, and the label/amount slot doesn't move. Triangle vs circle keeps it from reading as an error | Open |
| SV-VIS-11 | Home pills (groups v2) | `not_received`: accent (4.84 / 7.80), **Waiting on you**. `sent`: muted (6.79 / 7.04), no icon, in **Owed to you**, list order kept; `differences` (accent) still beats it | Accent `sent`; float news to the top | Colour on Home means "your move"; an arrival is news. No reshuffling. Proposal P4 (an unseen `sent` keeps a fully paid bill in Owed to you rather than the collapsed Settled group) is splitit-app-96's call | Tones adopted; **P4 declined by the user** (2026-11-26, SV-ENG-17): a fully paid bill goes straight to Settled |
| SV-VIS-12 | Footer captions and check-in footer | Today's muted captions with the check icon in `textMuted` for all method captions. Cash footer = the Pay footer's layout. Check-in footer: question in `label`, then `Yes` and `No, undo` as ghosts with `Details` at the right; no primary | A primary `Yes` like today's `I paid` | The footer stays chrome (SE-VIS-8); doing nothing already means yes | Open |
| SV-VIS-13 | Not-received footer icon | TriangleAlert drawn in `warning` (7.52 / 11.18); caption text stays muted | All muted (SE-VIS-8) | The one footer state where something went wrong that Sam didn't do himself; a grey triangle beside a filled button was easy to miss. 16 pt doesn't compete | Open; exception to SE-VIS-8 |
| SV-VIS-14 | Danger button inside a warning Banner (confirms) | Left as is in this slice (label 6.30 / 7.60 carries it; shape 1.04 / 1.08) | A text-only red button in Banner confirms | Existing app-wide pattern (row Undo); changing it belongs in a primitives pass (proposal P3) | Open |

### 2026-11-26 · Sent via Venmo, Amendment A1 (spec: [docs/specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo.md](specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo.md), "Amendment A1"; eng review: [docs/specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo-eng.md](specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo-eng.md))
The PM folded the eng review (F1–F12, §§1–9, §11), the UX and visual changes and the user's 2026-11-26 decisions into the spec before the build. Rows marked **user** record the user's own decision.

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SV-ENG-1 | Device storage keys (F1) | One JSON blob per user, `splitit.sentcheck.v1.<userId>` with entries `<billId>.<payerId>.<markAtMs>`, pruned after 2 days; `splitit.payMethod.v1.<userId>`; `splitit.notifAsked.v1`. Every builder tested against `^[\w.-]+$` | One key per send with `:` separators (the plan) | SecureStore throws on `:` and can't list keys, so per-send keys couldn't be pruned | Open |
| SV-ENG-2 | Native send order (F2) | Record → schedule the check-in → open Venmo | Write, open, then schedule (the plan) | iOS may suspend JS once Venmo is in front, so a schedule after the open can be lost | Open; amends SV-5 |
| SV-ENG-3 | Stale-screen double send (F3) | `mark_paid` returns `(settled_at, created)`; Venmo opens only when `created`; otherwise Banner "You've already marked this. Check below." | Return only the time | The "already marked" no-op returned ok, so a stale screen or a second device could open Venmo for a second payment | Open; amends SV-1, SV-6 |
| SV-ENG-4 | Check-in for "more" sends (F4) | None (no notification, no inline question) | Keyed on the difference call's time | `settle_difference` keeps the first `settled_at`, so the undo rule could never match; "more" is rare | Open; makes SV-13's confirm moot |
| SV-ENG-5 | Not received vs a re-send of the same amount (F5) | `mark_not_received` also takes `p_expected_at` | Cents only (the plan; ABA accepted) | A newer re-send of the same amount could be cleared by an old tap; a value isn't a version (lesson 2026-11-25) | Open |
| SV-ENG-6 | Undo rule order (F6) | Stored `'undo'` → noop; mark gone → store, silent; match → unmark; else → "changed" | Match → unmark, else "changed" | A second "No, undo" (notification then inline) would show a false "changed" message | Open |
| SV-ENG-7 | When Hana's update clears (F7) | `markBillSeen` on each new data version while the bill/Settle screen is focused (2 s debounce, starting value) plus once on blur | On open and on blur (SV-20) | Blur raced Home's focus refresh, which could read the old `seen_at` and show the update again | Open; replaces SV-20 |

### 2026-11-26 · Sent via Venmo, Tasks 4 and 5 (implementer; `lib/activityText.ts`, `lib/sentCheck.ts`, `lib/resumeGate.ts`)

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SV-T4-1 | `not_received` wording | D6 as written: `Hana hasn't received Sam's $15.10` / `You marked Sam's $15.10 as not received` / `Hana hasn't received your $15.10` | The task brief's paraphrase `Hana marked your $15.10 not received` | The spec is the one source for implementers; the brief quoted it loosely | Open |
| SV-T4-2 | Old events vs bad methods | No `method` key → today's wording (events from before this slice). `method` present but not one of the methods (including `null`, `'Venmo'`) → malformed, null. On `not_received` the method is required | Treat any unknown method as `other` | Old events have no key; the DB always writes a known method, so anything else is a forged or broken row (D6: unknown method → null) | Open |
| SV-T4-3 | Names mid-sentence in the new sentences | Lower case `someone` / `a friend` (`Sam sent someone $15.10 via Venmo`), like the newer sentences; the unchanged `other` wording keeps `Someone` | Capitalised everywhere | Matches paid_difference/paid_back; the old sentence's tests pin its wording | Open |
| SV-T4-4 | Consistency checks on the two-sided details | `not_received`: the subject is the `payer_id` in details (so a deleted payer account still reads right); malformed if a known actor isn't that payer, or `user_id = payer_id`. `paid_back`: `payer_id` optional (old events), but when present it must be a UUID, differ from `user_id` and equal a known actor | No cross-checks | The DB always writes actor = payer for these; a mismatch can only be a broken row, and showing it would misstate who did what | Open |
| SV-T5-1 | Key builders on bad input | Check every input (UUIDs, a non-negative safe-integer `markAtMs`) and return `null` instead of a key; a seeded fuzz test checks nothing else ever comes out | Build the string and let SecureStore reject it | SecureStore throws on a bad key; a null is something callers must handle and can't reach storage | Open |
| SV-T5-2 | Blob I/O | `readSentCheckBlob` / `storeSentCheckAnswer` take the storage as an argument; each storage call has a 2 s limit (`SENT_CHECK_STORAGE_TIMEOUT_MS`, starting value); writes per user run one at a time; a write whose read fails or times out writes nothing | No limit; unserialised read-modify-write | Card and footer can answer at once (both must land); a lost read must not wipe other answers. Residual: a `setItem` that times out could still land late over a newer blob (unlikely with SecureStore) | Open |
| SV-T5-3 | Malformed blob | Not JSON / not an object → empty; inside a good blob, malformed entries (bad key, answer, time, extra fields) are dropped one by one | Whole blob empty on any bad entry | One bad entry shouldn't make every other send ask again | Open |
| SV-T5-4 | Answer precedence | Ranks `shown` < `yes` < `undo`; a weaker answer changes nothing (time kept); an equal one refreshes the time | — | D3: undo beats yes, neither overwritten by shown | Open |
| SV-T5-5 | "Not for more sends" (F4) in `sentCheckInline` | Input has `lastSend?: 'share' \| 'more'` and `hasDifference`; `'more'` or a due difference → null. Task 9a should also store `'shown'` for the mark's key after a "more" send, so a later visit doesn't ask | Infer it from the mark alone | A "more" send keeps the first `settled_at`, so the data alone can't tell a topped-up mark from the original send | Open; Task 9a's part done (`useVenmoSend` stores `'shown'` for the mark's key after a "more" send) |
| SV-T5-6 | Mark time ahead of the device clock | Still shows (only `now − markAt > 30 min` hides it) | Hide marks "in the future" | Server and phone clocks differ by seconds; hiding would drop the question right after the send | Open |
| SV-T5-7 | `undoDecision` result | `'noop' \| 'silent' \| 'unmark' \| 'changed'`; a stored `'yes'` or `'shown'` doesn't block an undo | Return action flags | Four named outcomes, as A1 F6 lists them; "No, undo" after an earlier Yes is the newer answer | Open |
| SV-T5-8 | Payer name on the lock screen | Whitespace collapsed; control, format (bidi, zero-width) and lone-surrogate characters removed; at most 40 code points; blank → `Did your $15.10 go through?` | Send the name as stored | Text leaving the app (lesson 2026-11-25, control characters) | Open |
| SV-T5-9 | `parseSentCheckData` | Exactly the five keys; UUIDs; cents in the settle range (1 to 99,999,999); `markAtMs` a non-negative safe integer; `userId` equal to the session's; signed out → null | Ignore extra keys | Criterion 52 (extra keys carrying URLs are rejected) | Open |
| SV-T5-10 | Resume gate API | `settleResume(kind)` and `whenResumed(timeoutMs?)` (the task brief's name; spec D3 says `resumeSettled()`). It never hangs: after `RESUME_GATE_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000` (starting value) it resolves `'timed_out'`, which the responder treats like `'signed_in'`. First call wins; an unknown kind counts as `'signed_in'`. Also `resumeKindNow()` and `resetResumeGateForTests()` | Wait with no limit | A hang would leave a cold-start "No, undo" stuck; timing out to "drop" is also the spec's fallback if the layout never calls `settleResume` | Open |
| SV-T5-11 | Notification shape | `sentCheckNotification` returns the whole request: `identifier`, `content` (`sound: false`, no `badge` key, `categoryIdentifier: 'sent_check'`, `data`) and `trigger { seconds: 60, repeats: false }`; actions and placeholder are constants (`SENT_CHECK_ACTIONS`, `SENT_CHECK_PREVIEW_PLACEHOLDER`); null if any fact is invalid | Content only | Task 8 maps one object onto expo-notifications; no notification beats a wrong one | Open |

### 2026-11-26 · Sent via Venmo, Task 12: UI primitives (product-engineer; `components/ui/SegmentedControl.tsx`, `Button.tsx`, `TextLink.tsx`)

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SV-T12-1 | Defaults stay identical | New `disabled` props are passed to `Pressable` as `undefined` when off, so existing callers' `accessibilityState` is exactly what it was (no added `disabled: false`); tests pin it with `toEqual` | Always pass `disabled={false}` | "Every existing use looks and behaves the same", including what the screen reader gets | Open |
| SV-T12-2 | The radiogroup's label on native | The track gets `accessibilityRole="radiogroup"` + `accessibilityLabel` but is **not** `accessible` | Make the track `accessible` | `accessible` would merge the radios into one element on iOS/Android. Consequence: the group label ("How you're paying") is reliably announced on web only; VoiceOver/TalkBack likely skip a non-accessible container's label. Task 9a can put the context in each option's `accessibilityLabel` if the device check confirms it's skipped | Open, **needs a device check** |
| SV-T12-3 | Radio state | Radio segments carry only `checked`; button segments only `selected` | Set both | One state per role: `checked` is a radio's state, `selected` a button's (criterion 54 lists exactly these) | Open |
| SV-T12-4 | `TextLink` `loading` | Implies `disabled` (dimmed to 0.5, presses blocked) and adds `busy: true` to the caller's state; the spinner (`ActivityIndicator` small, `primary`) replaces the icon | Spinner at full opacity, link still pressable | Same rule as `Button` (`loading` is inactive), and the visual design dims every row's link while one is in flight | Open |
| SV-T12-5 | `Button flush` 'start' / 'end' text alignment | `justifyContent` flex-start / flex-end on the content, and `textAlign` left / right on the label (so a wrapped label also hugs the edge) | Only move the content row | A label that wraps at large text should keep the same edge | Open (LTR only; check `textAlign` if RTL is ever added) |

### 2026-11-26 · Sent via Venmo, Task 2: migration (supabase-engineer; `supabase/migrations/20261126080844_sent_via_venmo.sql`)
Spec: [docs/specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo.md](specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo.md) (Amendment A1, D1, D4, D8; criteria 1–21, 49–50). Tests: `supabase/tests/database/sent_via_venmo.test.sql`.

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SV-DB-1 | `settle_difference`'s `p_method` on a pay-back | Validated in both directions (null or unknown → `22023 invalid_method`), then ignored for pay-backs | Not checked at all on pay-backs | One rule for the argument; the app always sends one (Task 3 defaults to `'other'`), so only a bad direct call is refused. "Back ignores the method" is still true: it never changes what's stored | Open |
| SV-DB-2 | `paid_back.method` | The mark's stored method **before** the change (for a pay-back to 0, the cleared mark's method) | `p_method` | `p_method` is ignored for pay-backs, so logging it would record something nobody chose. Matches `not_received`, whose method is the cleared mark's (D6) | Open |
| SV-DB-3 | `paid_difference.method` | `p_method` (how this extra amount was paid) | The resulting stored method (which may be `'other'` after a mixed history) | D6's sentences describe this payment ("Sam sent Hana $1.10 more via Venmo"), even when the stored mark becomes `'other'` | Open |
| SV-DB-4 | `set_payer`'s clearing statement | One UPDATE, always run, matching rows with a mark **or** a flag; replaces the old `if v_marks > 0` guard | Two UPDATEs (marks, then flags), each guarded | A bill can carry a flag with no marks. A statement that touches no row sends no Realtime hint (`broadcast_bill_change` skips empty statements), so nothing new goes out when there's nothing to clear. `payer_changed.cleared` still counts marks only | Open |
| SV-DB-5 | How the reset trigger counts cleared marks | A separate `count(*)` of marked rows before the clearing UPDATE (under the bills row lock it already holds) | Counting the UPDATE's returned rows, as before | The UPDATE now also touches flag-only rows, and Postgres 17's `RETURNING` can't see the old values to tell them apart | Open |
| SV-DB-6 | How M9 (the backfill) is tested | pgTAP checks the three new constraints are validated (so every row that existed at migration time passed) and that fixture rows obey the rule. The backfill was also checked once on the local DB when applied: 1 of 1 existing marks became `'other'` | A whole-table count in pgTAP | Rows that existed before the migration can't be fixture rows (the tests run after it), and whole-table counts are banned (the local DB holds real data) | Open |
| SV-DB-7 | How M17 (a racing unmark) is tested | A test-only BEFORE UPDATE trigger, created and dropped inside the test's transaction, skips the row so `mark_paid`'s conditional UPDATE matches nothing while the row is unmarked; asserts `(null, false)`, no error, no event | Static check only | pgTAP runs one session, so a real race can't happen; this reaches the exact state the no-op branch reads after a racing unmark | Open |
| SV-DB-8 | How M18 (`notify pgrst`) is tested | pgTAP reads the statements the CLI records in `supabase_migrations.schema_migrations` for version `20261126080844` | A grep outside pgTAP | Keeps the [M18] tag in the pgTAP file. The CLI records each statement on `migration up` and `db reset` | Open |
| SV-DB-9 | How the one list is written | `select p in ('venmo','cash','other');` exactly as D1 spells it (no spaces). The list appears nowhere else in the migration, comments included | With spaces after the commas | Task 3's drift test parses this function from the newest migration that defines it; matching D1's text exactly makes that parse predictable | Open |
| SV-DB-10 | Nulls in `private.is_payment_method` | Null in, null out. Callers use `coalesce(…, false)`, and the not-received check also requires `not_received_method is not null` | Return false for null | A CHECK that evaluates to null passes, so without the explicit not-null a partial triple could get through. The settled-method check is `settled_method is null or …`, which is intended | Open |
| SV-DB-11 | `my_bills.seen` | Computed as `s.seen_at is not null` from a left join on `bill_seen` (was an `exists`), next to the new `seen_at` | Keep `exists` and add a scalar subquery | Same answer (`seen_at` is not null, primary key `(bill_id, user_id)`), and one read gives both columns. `bill_markers.test.sql` passes unchanged | Open |
| SV-DB-12 | `mark_not_received`'s idempotent retry | As D4 step 6 says: unmarked, same `not_received_cents`, flagged by the caller → success. It doesn't compare `p_expected_at` or the method | Also require the same time | The flag stores no time, and the row is already in the state the retry asks for. Permission checks still run first (tested: the non-payer host retrying gets 42501) | Open |
| SV-DB-13 | Existing pgTAP files: more than call signatures | Besides appending `'other'` to 3-arg `mark_paid` and 5-arg `settle_difference` calls and the regprocedure strings, six exact event-details expectations gained `method` (and `payer_id` on `paid_back`), and eight owner-side direct writes in `settle_payer.test.sql` gained `settled_method` (the three 23514 cases there now pass a valid method, so each still fails only on the rule it names) | Leave them failing | The new check `bill_participants_method_with_mark` and D6's details require it. Assertion counts are unchanged | Open |
| SV-DB-14 | Cases beyond the spec list | Added [N16] (Not received after a difference: the stored net 1620 is the token, the old 1510 is stale). There is no N12 (the spec's criteria skip it) | Only the listed cases | The difference path changes `settled_cents` without changing `settled_at`, so it's the other way a token goes stale | Open |
| SV-DB-15 | `unmarked_paid` details | Unchanged `{ user_id, cents }` (no method) | Add `method` | D6 doesn't list it, and `lib/activityText.ts` parses today's shape | Open |
| SV-DB-16 | The backfill's Realtime hint | The backfill UPDATE fires `bill_participants_broadcast_update` once at deploy: one "changed" hint per bill that has a mark, so open screens refetch once | Disable the trigger for the backfill | Harmless (a refetch), and disabling a trigger in a migration is easy to get wrong | Open |
| SV-DB-17 | Function owners | `alter function … owner to postgres` stated on every created security definer function (`mark_paid`, `settle_difference`, `mark_not_received`, `my_bills`) | Rely on the migration role | Same as `my_bills` and the reset trigger before; pgTAP asserts it | Open |

### 2026-11-26 · Sent via Venmo, Task 3: data layer, methods, web opener (implementer; `lib/paymentMethods.ts`, `lib/bills.ts`, `lib/settle.ts`, `lib/paylinks.ts`)
Spec: [docs/specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo.md](specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo.md) (Amendment A1, D1, D2, D4, D7; criteria 22–24).

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SV-T3-1 | Fixture rows without the new participant keys | An **absent** `settled_method` key reads as `'other'` on a mark (null without one); absent `not_received_*` keys read as no flag. A key that is present must be valid: a mark with `settled_method: null` or an unknown method, a method with no mark, or a partial triple is malformed (`network`) | Treat a missing key as malformed too | The select always names the columns, and a database without them fails the whole select (42703), so only hand-built test rows lack them (the `parseKind` precedent). Strict parsing would have broken, at runtime, router tests in four files owned by other tasks and sessions (`homeLayout`, `homeMarkers`, `settle`, `billScreen`), which the spec planned only as type errors | Open, **departs from a literal reading of criterion 23** ("a marked row with no method → malformed" is met for `null`, not for an absent key) |
| SV-T3-2 | `my_bills` row with no `seen_at` key | Reads as `seenAt: null`; a present value must be null or a timestamp | Malformed | A web build that reaches a database without the migration still lists bills (null means "go by `seen`", D7). Also keeps `__tests__/homeLayout.test.tsx` and other row fakes passing without edits | Open |
| SV-T3-3 | `markPaid`'s result when the response isn't one `(settled_at, created)` row | `network` (null data, empty list, two rows, `created` not a boolean, `created: true` with no time, an unparseable time). `created: false` with a null time is ok (a racing unmark) | Treat unknown shapes as `created: true` | Unknown = "the outcome is unknown", which the send hook already handles as contract O; never opens Venmo on a guess that a mark was made | Open |
| SV-T3-4 | `markNotReceived`'s `expectedAt` | Sent exactly as fetched (the `settled_at` string with its microseconds); refused locally if it doesn't parse | Normalise through `Date` | A `Date` keeps milliseconds only, so a re-formatted time would never equal the stored `timestamptz` and every Not received would fail `mark_changed` | Open |
| SV-T3-5 | Local refusal order | Bad bill → `not_found`; bad user/payer/cents (or time) → `invalid_bill`; then a bad method → `invalid_method` | Method first | Same order as the database (invalid_amount, then invalid_method) | Open |
| SV-T3-6 | `invalid_method` copy | `That way of paying isn't available. Choose another and try again.` | Spell out the methods | Copy mustn't repeat the list (contract M), and the app never sends a bad method, so this shows only after a database change | Open |
| SV-T3-7 | `availableMethods` | The chooser's options only: `['venmo', 'cash']` with a valid payer Venmo username, `['cash']` otherwise. `other` is never in it (it's the separate "Paid another way" link) | Include `other` | With `other` included, a payer without Venmo would give two options and a chooser, against SV-29 | Open |
| SV-T3-8 | Where `notReceived` comes into the plan | `settlePlan(..., { notReceived })` (optional map, like `marks`); every entry gets `notReceived` (null when absent), the payer's included. New `settleFactsOf(participants)` builds both maps. A flag never changes amounts or counts | Fold it into `SettleMarks` | A flag sits on an unmarked person, so it isn't a mark; the option keeps every existing call compiling | Open |
| SV-T3-9 | Drift test comparison | Same set of methods (no duplicates), order ignored; reads the last definition in the newest migration that defines `private.is_payment_method`; fails if none is found | Exact order | SQL `in (...)` has no order; requiring one would fail on a harmless rewrite. A missing definition fails, so the test can't pass without its list | Open |
| SV-T3-10 | Web opener shape | `openPaymentLinkWeb(url)` (sync, `window.open(url, '_blank')` first, then `opener = null`; null when blocked, throwing or missing), `webOpenStillOpen(w)` (a throwing `closed` counts as closed), and `webOpenOutcome(w, mobile, waitMs = WEB_OPEN_CHECK_MS)` → `'blocked' \| 'ask' \| 'record'`. `WEB_OPEN_CHECK_MS = 300` lives in `lib/paylinks.ts`. `mobile` comes from `detectMobileWeb` (the caller passes it) | Put the wait and the desktop/mobile rule in the hook | The A1 rule (desktop asks, mobile checks the tab, blocked writes nothing) is pure, so it is unit-tested here with fake timers; Task 9a only wires it | Open |
| SV-T3-11 | Test helpers | `fakeMark(cents, at, toUserId, method = 'other')` and `fakeNotReceived(cents, toUserId, method = 'venmo')` in `test-utils/fakes.ts` | Each test file builds its own | The spec's defaults (`'other'`, the plan payer, null) in one place, so Tasks 6, 7 and groups v2 can fix their mark helpers in one line | Open |

Spec: [docs/specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo.md](specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo.md) (Amendment A1, D7; criteria 29, 58).

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SV-T7-1 | How `billMarkerView('sent')` gets the seen facts | Optional `summary?: Pick<BillSummary, 'seen' \| 'seenAt'>` on `MarkerViewContext`. Without it the `sent` pill is null (never a guessed pill); every other marker reads as before. `billRowA11yLabel` passes it | Required field; or fall back to the `left_to_pay` pill when it's missing | Required would break `homeRowView` in `lib/homeSections.ts` (splitit-app-96's file, beyond the pre-approved lines). The fallback would show words for a marker the view didn't check. **Needs one line in `homeRowView`** (`summary` in the ctx) before Home draws the `sent` pill; until then a payer's row with an unseen send shows no pill | Open; the `homeRowView` line has landed (checked 2026-11-27: `lib/homeSections.ts` passes `summary` to `billMarkerView`) |

The two list edits are forced: their test asserts the fixtures cover every `HomeMarker` and the set's exact contents. Groups v2's Task 1 replaces all of it with `MARKER_GROUPS` (`waiting_on_others: ['waiting']`) | Superseded by groups v2 (checked 2026-11-27: `WAITING_MARKERS` is gone; `MARKER_GROUPS` in `lib/homeSections.ts` has `waiting_on_others: ['waiting']`) |
| SV-T7-3 | `not_received` vs an early plan where you can't settle now | `not_received` is checked before the early `canSettle` rule, so the pill shows whenever you're unmarked, owe, and the flag names the current payer | Hide it when `canSettle` is false | D7: it ranks before `owes` and needs only those three facts. It's news Sam needs even if he can't pay this minute | Open |
| SV-T7-4 | "Someone still owes" on an early payer plan | Computed from the entries (another person with role `owes` and no mark), since `SettleEarly` has no `paidCount` / `owingCount` | Add counts to the early plan | D7 said to verify; the early plan has no counts, and `lib/settle.ts` is out of scope. No money maths, only roles and marks | Open |
| SV-T7-5 | Own pills for a Venmo mark beyond the four in D7 | `paid_back_due` with a Venmo mark reads `Sent · $1.10 back to you` (a11y `Sent, Hana owes you $1.10 back`); `paid_more` Venmo a11y `Sent, you owe $1.10 more`. Cash and other keep `Paid` | Keep `Paid · …` for every method there | Decision 1: Venmo sends are "Sent", never "Paid". D7 lists only the pill for `paid_more` | Open |

You can pay once the bill is ready`) | D7's `Claimed · waiting on others` | **User decision** (2026-11-26, via splitit-app-96): "waiting on others" clashed with the "Waiting on you" heading.
