### 2026-11-26 · Sent via Venmo, Task 8: notifications module (native-mobile; `expo-notifications` 57.0.26)
Spec: [docs/specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo.md](specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo.md) (Amendment A1, D3; criteria 26, 44–46, 53, 55, 56). Facts checked in the installed package (`node_modules/expo-notifications`).

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SV-T8-1 | Push entitlement | `plugins/withoutPushEntitlement.js` deletes `aps-environment`; listed before `"expo-notifications"` in `app.json` | Paid Apple team; inline-only | The free team can't sign push and local notifications don't need it. The order was proven by running it: reversed order keeps `aps-environment: development`; this order leaves `{}`. A scratch `expo prebuild --platform ios --no-install` wrote an empty entitlements file | Open (signing still unverified on the phone, U1(a)) |
| SV-T8-2 | `scheduleSentCheck` signature | Takes the whole `SentCheckNotification` from `sentCheckNotification(...)` | Task 8's `scheduleSentCheck(content, id)` | One value from the pure builder: the identifier, content and 60 s trigger can't be mixed up between calls | Open, **departs from the spec's signature** |
| SV-T8-3 | Hidden-preview option name | `previewPlaceholder` on `setNotificationCategoryAsync`'s options (`src/Notifications.types.ts:759`; iOS maps it to `hiddenPreviewsBodyPlaceholder`, `TriggerRecords.swift:316,375`) | Reword | The spec's guess was right | Verified (source) |
| SV-T8-4 | Provisional / ephemeral (`ios.status` 3, 4) | Read as `granted` (we schedule; never re-ask) | Treat as undetermined and ask | We never request provisional, so it only appears if iOS or the user set it. It can deliver (quietly), and asking again would show nothing new | Open |
| SV-T8-5 | A failed or hung permission read | `denied` (silence: no note, no prompt, no schedule) | `undetermined` | "Unknown" must never show the "We'll check back" note or a prompt | Open |
| SV-T8-6 | The "asked once" flag | Written before the prompt; if the flag can't be read or written, don't ask | Ask anyway | "Ask once" holds even when a prompt is interrupted or storage is failing. iOS asks only once anyway | Open |
| SV-T8-7 | Time limits | Schedule 1000 ms total (category + channel + schedule), other calls 2000 ms, the permission prompt 60 s (all starting values, in `lib/checkInNotifications.types.ts`) | One limit for everything | The prompt waits on a person; the rest shouldn't hold up Pay. A schedule that lands after its limit is cancelled and dismissed, so "no notification" stays true | Open |
| SV-T8-8 | Category registration fails | Still schedule (no buttons; a body tap still opens Settle up) | Skip the notification | A check-in without buttons is still useful; the inline question covers the rest | Open |
| SV-T8-9 | Android channel | `sent_check`, name `Payment check-ins`, importance HIGH, `sound: null`, `showBadge: false`; created before the permission prompt and before scheduling | Default channel | Android 13+ shows the prompt only once a channel exists; HIGH gives a banner (Unverified on a device, R4) | Open |
| SV-T8-10 | Which responses count | Identifier starts `sentcheck.` and matches the device-key pattern; action `yes`, `undo` or the body tap (`DEFAULT_ACTION_IDENTIFIER` → `'open'`). Others (e.g. dismiss) are ignored. `data` is passed raw for `parseSentCheckData` | Filter on category | The identifier is ours by construction; the session allow-list stays in the responder (Task 9b) | Open |
| SV-T8-11 | Dedupe scope | Per app launch, across hook re-mounts (last 100 keys); the last response is cleared after each delivery | Per mount | A sign-out/in remount must not handle the same "No, undo" twice (criterion 41) | Open |
| SV-T8-12 | Cancel scope | `cancelSentChecks(userId)` matches the identifier prefix **or** `data.userId`; `cancelAllSentChecks()` (sign-out) clears every `sentcheck.` notification, pending and presented, but never other apps' or future kinds | `cancelAllScheduledNotificationsAsync` | Signed out we may not know the user; other notification kinds must survive | Open |
| SV-T8-13 | Jest stand-in | `test-utils/jestSetup.ts` mocks `expo-notifications` with calls that reject (async) or throw (sync) "expo-notifications not mocked in this test"; the module turns those into "no notification" | Calls that resolve | Same rule as expo-contacts/expo-sms: a forgotten mock can never schedule by accident | Open |

### 2026-11-26 · Sent via Venmo, Task 6: settle view models (implementer; `lib/settleView.ts`)
Spec: [docs/specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo.md](specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo.md) (Amendment A1, D2–D5; criterion 28). Copy: UX brief §12.

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SV-T6-1 | How the device facts reach the view | `SettleViewInput` gains required `billId`, `rememberedMethod`, `sentCheck: { answers, now, lastSend? } \| null` and `permissionUndetermined`; `settleViewInputFor` takes an optional 5th `device` argument and defaults to none (null / null / false) | Optional fields on the input; the screen computes the check itself | One place decides the check for the card and the footer, so answering one answers both. Required fields keep every hand-built input honest; the default argument keeps today's screens compiling until Task 9a passes real values | Open |
| SV-T6-2 | `settleViewInputFor` and the flags | Uses `settleFactsOf(participants)` and passes `{ allowEarly: true, notReceived }` to `settlePlan` | — | The live check found the flags were dropped, so Sam's warning could never show; a test now goes from a fetched participant row to the card's warning line | Done (main session's finding) |
| SV-T6-3 | Chooser options' labels | Each option carries `a11yLabel` `"Venmo, how you're paying"` / `"Cash, how you're paying"`; the group label stays `How you're paying` | Group label only | SV-T12-2: the radiogroup's label may not be read on iOS/Android | Open, device check (U4) |
| SV-T6-4 | When Venmo counts as offered | `availableMethods(payerVenmo)` **and** a pay link exists for that amount (`venmoPayUrl` non-null). Over the link cap (100,000,000¢) the card is cash only, and the ready footer is the cash footer | Offer Venmo whenever the username is valid | Never show a Pay button that can't build a link; today's rule (no link → no Pay) kept, now with the cash primary instead of "I paid" | Open |
| SV-T6-5 | Inline check on early marked cards | Asked on early marked cards too (a share sent via Venmo while the bill is early); the settle-early line stays, the completion line is ready-only. Early footers ask too (`sent_check`) | Ready cards only | D3's conditions don't depend on the plan being complete, and the early send schedules the same notification | Open |
| SV-T6-6 | Not-received line on early owes cards and in the contract O card | Shown on the early owes card (first line, before the may-change line). **Not** shown on the contract O card ("Did you send…?"); the early footer stays `You can pay now · …` (no warning caption) | Warn everywhere | The contract O card is about the send he just made; the early footer never offers Pay (SE-9), so the warning there adds nothing the card doesn't already say. The U! footer rule (D5) is "Ready, owes" | Open |
| SV-T6-7 | Not-received line amount | The flag's cents (the cleared mark), not today's share | Today's share | D4 copy says "your $15.10", the amount Hana didn't get | Open |
| SV-T6-8 | "Yes, I sent it" storing `'yes'` | The mark action carries `confirmsSend: true`; Task 9a stores `'yes'` for the returned mark's key | A separate action kind | The write is the same `markPaid(…, 'venmo')`; the flag tells the screen to answer the check at once (D2) | Open; wired in Task 9a (`useVenmoSend` stores `'yes'` when `confirmsSend`) |
| SV-T6-9 | Check answers' payload | `sent_check_yes` / `sent_check_undo` carry the whole `SentCheckInline` (key, billId, payerId, cents, markAtMs), not just `key` | `key` only (D5's sketch) | `undoDecision` needs cents, markAtMs and payerId; the screen shouldn't re-derive them | Open |
| SV-T6-10 | `differenceArgs` | Returns `[userId, payerId, from, to, method]` | Separate method lookup | Matches `settleDifference`'s argument order, so `settleDifference(id, ...differenceArgs(a))` sends the method | Open |
| SV-T6-11 | Pay-back record's method | The payer's "I paid it back" sends `'other'` | Pass the mark's method | The DB ignores the method on pay-backs (D1, SV-DB-1); `'other'` is what the app always sends | Open |
| SV-T6-12 | Row a11y and statuses | Status `Sent via Venmo` / `Paid in cash` / `Paid` (success) on every marked row, difference rows keep their difference status; a11y `Sam, $15.10, sent via Venmo` / `paid in cash` / `marked paid`. `wideStatus` is true for any marked row (`Paid` counts, as visual §4.1 says "in practice, any marked row") | Wide only for Venmo/cash | One width rule; the money column doesn't drift between rows | Open |
| SV-T6-13 | Not received on rows | Only when the viewer is the payer, the row isn't theirs, and `mark.toUserId === viewer` (§11). Shown on every marked row incl. difference and early rows; the payer never gets Undo (also when host). The model carries the confirm copy and labels, and the `not_received` action with the mark's exact `at` string as `expectedAt` | Keep Undo for a host-payer | SV-10: one control for the payer, and Sam is told | Open |
| SV-T6-14 | Not-received sub-line visibility | `Not received · $15.10` on the unmarked row for every viewer who sees it (payer, others, Sam's own row) | Payer only | Same fact is in Activity; one rule is simpler | Open |
| SV-T6-15 | Undo labels | Marked venmo: `Didn't go through? Undo`; cash/other: `Undo` (a11y `Undo`, hint `Clears Paid in cash for $15.10`); difference cards: `Undo all $15.10`. The non-payer host's row Undo copy (`Undo Sam's "I paid"?`) is unchanged | Keep `Undo your I paid` | The brief's §10.1 labels; "I paid" no longer describes a Venmo send. The row Undo is a different person's control the brief leaves as is | Open |
| SV-T6-16 | Ready marked footer variant | **Secondary** `Settle up` (was primary) with the method caption; payer all settled also secondary with `Everyone's settled up`; both hint `See who paid` | Keep primary | D5's table; nothing left to do is quieter. `billFooterAction` no longer turns every secondary into primary: only the blocked "N left to claim" footer (new `incomplete: true` flag) | Open |
| SV-T6-17 | `markChanged` copy and actionError | `Your payment just changed. Check the amount and try again.`; with `{ notReceived: true }`: `mark_changed` → `That payment just changed, so nothing was cleared. Check below.` and `not_allowed` refetches; `invalid_method` → `BILL_ERROR_COPY.invalid_method`, no refetch | Keep the `"I paid"` wording | "I paid" isn't the action any more. `BILL_ERROR_COPY.mark_changed` in `lib/bills.ts` still has the old words (not this task's file) | Open; `lib/bills.ts` copy noted for later |
| SV-T6-18 | Strings left unchanged | `blockedMarkSaved` (`Your "I paid" is saved.`), `markUndoneBanner` (`Your "I paid" was undone…`), the row Undo confirm | Reword them now | Not in A1's change list; each is a separate copy decision | Open, needs your look |
| SV-T6-19 | `NoticeSnapshot.notReceived` | Optional (`absent = false`), set by `noticeSnapshot` | Required | Same pattern as `myShareCents`, so any hand-built snapshot keeps compiling | Open |
| SV-T6-20 | Copy constants for Task 9a | `SETTLE_COPY.alreadyMarked`, `undoneNotSent`, `undoFailed`, `announceAllSet`, `announceCash`, `permissionLine`; builders `sentCheckChangedText`, `announceSentText`, `announceNotReceivedText`, `chosenMethod` | Let 9a write them | All settle copy lives in this file | Open |

Spec: [docs/specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo.md](specs/2026-11-26-sent-via-venmo.md) (Amendment A1, D2–D5, Task 9a; criteria 37–40, 42–46).

A disabled answer is honest; a button that does nothing isn't | Closed by Task 9b (the bill screen passes `onCheck`) |
| SV-T9a-2 | Where cancel-on-return lives | In `useVenmoSend` (so both screens get it): a background → active transition calls `cancelSentChecks(userId)`; inactive → active doesn't | A listener in each screen; in the responder (9b) | One hook already owns the send; tracking "went to background since the last active" also covers iOS's background → inactive → active order | Open |
| SV-T9a-3 | One write at a time across the screen and the hook | The screen passes its `writing` ref into the hook | Separate guards | Pay, cash, Undo, the check's undo and Not received can't race each other on one card | Open |
| SV-T9a-4 | Network or timeout, then Venmo fails to open (native) | Show the open-failed line; if the late mark lands as `created`, `unmarkPaid` it | Leave a late mark in place | Otherwise the card would say "Sent via Venmo" for a Venmo that never opened (never say anything untrue) | Superseded by SV-R-3 (review R4): re-read the bill and clear only a matching late send |
| SV-T9a-5 | Web, mobile, recorded, but `created = false` | The "already marked" Banner and a refetch (Venmo is already open in the tab) | Treat as success silently | The person should know the second tab didn't add a second payment | Open |
| SV-T9a-6 | Web write refused after Venmo opened (`payer_changed` etc.) | `actionError`'s copy + refetch, as on native | Spec's "copy saying to check Venmo" | No such copy exists in `lib/settleView.ts`; new copy is the PM's call. Listed as a deviation | Superseded by SV-R-1 and SV-R-2 (review R1): "Venmo opened, but … Don't send it" copy (`afterOpenRefusal`) and the opened tab is closed |
| SV-T9a-7 | Announcing the inline "Yes" | iOS **and Android** `announceForAccessibility('You're all set')`; web reads the completion line | A visible "You're all set" caption in the card's announcement slot | The completion line isn't a live region in the model, and a second visible "You're all set" right under "You're all set. Hana can see it." would repeat itself | Open |
| SV-T9a-8 | The card waits for the device reads | The card shows the loading state until the remembered method and the answers blob are read (each read has a 2 s limit) | Render at once with defaults | The chooser and the inline check would otherwise jump after the first paint | Open |
| SV-T9a-9 | Pay back's open-failed line | New `payBackOpenFailedText`: `… in the Venmo app, then tap I paid it back.` (lib/settleView.ts, with a test) | Reuse `openFailedText` (`… then tap Paid another way.`) | The payer has no "Paid another way" on that row; the line would point at a control that isn't there | Open, needs your look (copy) |
| SV-T9a-10 | `markBillSeen` on Settle | New `lib/useMarkSeen.ts`: after each new data version while focused, debounced `SEEN_DEBOUNCE_MS = 2000` (starting value), plus once on blur | Inline in the screen | Task 9b needs the same rule on the bill screen; one hook keeps them alike | Open |
| SV-T9a-11 | Remembered method helpers | `readRememberedMethod` / `rememberMethod` in `lib/useVenmoSend.ts` (only `venmo` / `cash`, 2 s limit, never throw) | Add them to `lib/sentCheck.ts` | `lib/sentCheck.ts` is Task 5's; the key builder (`payMethodKey`) is reused from it, not redefined | Open |

### 2026-11-27 · Sent via Venmo, Task 9b: bill footer, responder, seen (product-engineer; `app/(app)/bills/[id]/index.tsx`, `components/SettleFooter.tsx`, `components/SentCheckResponder.tsx`, `app/(app)/_layout.tsx`, `app/_layout.tsx` (resume gate), `lib/sentCheckUndo.ts`, `lib/signOut.ts`)

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| SV-T9b-1 | Resume gate wiring (F10) | Used, not the fallback. `ResumeAfterSignIn` settles once per launch in a `finally`: `'none'` only when a restored session had nothing to resume; `'signed_in'` when it navigated (invite or off an auth screen), when `takePendingInvite` threw, or when the app **started signed out**. The brief's `'restored'` is the gate's existing `'none'` (`lib/resumeGate.ts` takes `'none' \| 'signed_in'`) | Rename the gate's kind to `'restored'` (a 9a file and its tests); settle `'none'` on a signed-out start | Started signed out, any cold-start response needs a sign-in first, and that sign-in's resume owns navigation. Settling `'none'` there would let the response race it | Open |
| SV-T9b-2 | When "No, undo" from a notification navigates | **After** the undo rule has run (re-read + `unmarkPaid`), then `router.push('/bills/<id>/settle')`, also when it fails | Push first, undo in the background | Pushed first, Settle up would load with the mark, then see it vanish on the live refetch and show `Your "I paid" was undone. Check Activity to see who.`, which is wrong for your own undo. The cost is ~1 round trip on the screen you were on | Open |
| SV-T9b-3 | Feedback for a notification "No, undo" | iOS/Android announcement only: `Undone. Not marked as sent.`, the "changed" text, or `Couldn't undo. Try again.`. No Banner on Settle up | A one-shot notice that Settle up reads on mount (edits `settle.tsx`, 9a's) | Kept 9b inside its files. Visually, Settle up shows the result (owes again, or the current mark). On a failure the inline question is still there (nothing stored) | **Needs your look**: the rare "changed" case shows no visible reason |
| SV-T9b-4 | Live responses and the gate | Live responses also await `whenResumed()` but are never dropped (only `source: 'cold'` is) | Skip the wait for live ones | Costs nothing once settled; keeps one path. If the layout ever stopped settling, live answers would wait 5 s (the gate's limit) rather than race | Open |
| SV-T9b-5 | Cold-start "Yes" | Dropped like any cold response when the gate isn't `'none'` | Always store a "Yes" | The brief says drop cold responses for `'signed_in'` / `'timed_out'`; a dropped Yes is harmless (the inline question may show once) | Open |
| SV-T9b-6 | One undo runner | New `lib/sentCheckUndo.ts` (`runSentCheckUndo`, injectable deps, never throws, skips the re-read when `'undo'` is already stored), used by the bill footer and the responder | Copy Settle up's inline code twice more | One set of steps for the undo rule. Settle up (9a) still has its own inline copy; moving it onto the runner is a follow-up in a 9a file | Open; the follow-up is done (review R6, SV-R-7: Settle up's card "No, undo" runs `runSentCheckUndo` too) |
| SV-T9b-7 | "Shown" from the bill footer | Stored on blur like Settle up, **except** when leaving through this bill's own `Details` / `Settle up`; answers are re-read on every focus | Store on every blur (D3 literally); never from the bill screen | Tapping Details to look closer shouldn't make the question vanish on the page it opens. Re-reading on focus means a Yes / No given on Settle up shows on return | Open |
| SV-T9b-8 | Bill screen waits for the device reads | Loading until the remembered method and the answers blob are read (2 s limits each), as Settle up does (SV-T9a-8) | Render the footer with defaults first | The footer would otherwise flip from Pay to Cash or into the question after first paint | Open |
| SV-T9b-9 | Pay's busy state in the footer | `SettleFooter` shows the spinner on Pay too (was cash only) while the send hook's write runs | No feedback | Native Pay records before Venmo opens (up to 4 s); a dead-looking button invites a second tap | Open |
| SV-T9b-10 | `BILL_ERROR_COPY.mark_changed` still says `Your "I paid" just changed…` | Not changed | Rewrite to "sent" wording | A1's copy table gives no exact string for it (the closest, `SETTLE_COPY.markChanged`, is `Your payment just changed. Check the amount and try again.`). Reported | **Needs your look** |
