"Couldn't open Venmo…" keyed on recipient **and** amount, since it names the amount | Key both on recipient only | Lessons 2026-11-25 (prompt keyed on the facts that make it true; open-failed line outlived its amount) | Open |
| GR-T10-12 | `groupSendPlan` failure endings | Native: record refused → `refused` (nothing opened); unknown → open, then `ask`; open failed (either) → `undo`, then `open_failed` even if the undo fails (the refetch then shows the payment with its Undo). Web: blocked → `open_failed`, no write; desktop never records without Yes. Record limit `GROUP_SEND_RECORD_TIMEOUT_MS = 4000` (SV's 4 s; no shared constant exists in SV's code yet) | Import SV's constant | None exported; move to a shared one if SV adds it | Open |
| GR-T10-13 | Received payments on the recipient's card | Every live payment to the viewer that someone else recorded, newest first, each with "Not received" | Only recent ones | Trip groups are small; Who paid what lists them anyway | Open |

T9 follow-up for GR-M6 / GR-T10-9 (implementer, 2026-11-26):

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GR-T9-14 | Where `SnapshotNotReceived` is defined | In `lib/groups.ts` (the data layer owns the snapshot's shape). `GroupSnapshot.notReceived` is required and parsed from the required `not_received` key (`[]` allowed). `lib/groupSettleView.ts` should re-export or import it and drop its local copy and the optional `WithNotReceived` cast (T10's file, not edited here) | Import T10's type into the data layer | Data layer → view layer would be a backwards dependency; one definition either way | Open (T10 follow-up) |
| GR-T9-15 | `not_received` checks | Fail closed (whole read `network`): lowercase uuid id (unique), `from`/`to` members and different, method by `isPaymentMethod`, cents 1..99,999,999 (`isSettleCents`), `undone_at` an ISO 8601 date-time (shape regex plus a valid date). Keys as GR-M6 lists them: `id, from, to, cents, method, undone_at`. "Involving the viewer" isn't checked (the parser doesn't know the viewer) | Also check the viewer | T6 decides which rows; T12 confirms the key names live | Open |

T4 payments (supabase-engineer, 2026-11-26; `supabase/migrations/20261126090704_group_payments.sql`, test `supabase/tests/database/group_payments.test.sql`):

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GR-T4-1 | Undo column names | `undone_at`, `undone_by`, `undone_reason` (spec T4 text says `deleted_at` / `deleted_by`) | `deleted_*` as in expenses | A payment is undone, not deleted; GR-M6 and T6's `not_received` list use `undone_at`. Live = `undone_at is null` (C-G2's "deleted_at is null" for payments). T6 reads these names | Open (for T6) |
| GR-T4-2 | How `undone_reason` is decided (GR-M6) | By who calls `undo_payment`: `to_user` → `not_received`, `from_user` → `sender`. No new parameter (lib's `undoPayment` sends only `p_id`). A table check pins it (`not_received` ⇔ `undone_by = to_user`). Logged in `payment_undone` details | A `p_reason` argument; decide by recorder | Matches `lib/groupActivityText.ts`, which already reads an undo by `to` as "didn't receive". Consequence: a recipient undoing a payment they recorded themselves ("Got paid another way") is also `not_received`, so the sender sees "hasn't received" for it (true: the recipient says they didn't get it) | Open |
| GR-T4-3 | A repeat undo | No-op for either party; the first undoer and reason stay | The other party's undo overwrites the reason | One reason per payment; a no-op writes nothing (lesson 2026-11-22) | Open |
| GR-T4-4 | `record_payment` retry match | Same id + same group, from, to, cents, method, source **and the same recorder (caller)** → success, no write, no event, undone stays undone (A1.6); anything else → `id_conflict` | Ignore the recorder | Same rule as GR-T3-2: another person reusing an id is a clash, not a retry | Open |
| GR-T4-5 | Check order | Signed out / caller not a member → 42501; `p_id` null → `invalid_id`; cents → `invalid_amount`; method / source / pair / from or to null / from = to → `invalid_payment`; caller not from or to → 42501; a party not a member → `not_member`; then the insert | Authorize the party rule first | "Can't see it" answers first (GR-T2-8); field errors before the party rule so a bad call by a third member reads as the field error. A member's cents-0 call from a third member gets `invalid_amount`, not 42501 | Open |
| GR-T4-6 | `service_role` on `group_payments` | INSERT / UPDATE / DELETE / TRUNCATE revoked (SELECT kept) | Leave Supabase's default | Money rows can't be forged with the secret key; functions (and T5's attach) run as their owner. T3 didn't do this for expenses (noted, not changed) | Open |
| GR-T4-7 | `p_cents` type | `bigint` (column `integer` 1..99,999,999), as GR-T3-8 | integer | An oversized number reaches the range check (`invalid_amount`) instead of a cast error | Open |
| GR-T4-8 | `payment_undone` details | `{payment_id, undone_reason}` (coordinator's request); `payment_recorded` `{payment_id, from, to, cents, method, source}` | Repeat from/to/cents in `payment_undone` | No duplicated fields; Activity joins on `payment_id` via `paymentsFromEvents` | Open |
| GR-T4-9 | Table checks beyond C-G7 | `bill_id` only with `source = 'bill_mark'`; `recorded_by` and `undone_by` are `from` or `to`; undone triple all-or-none; pair check repeated on the table; `bill_id → bills on delete set null` | Function checks only | T5 writes `bill_mark` rows as postgres; the table still refuses a bad pair | Open (for T5) |

T5 bills in groups (supabase-engineer, 2026-11-27; `supabase/migrations/20261127060131_group_bills.sql`, test `supabase/tests/database/group_bills.test.sql`, 97 assertions):

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GR-T5-1 | `add_participant` on a group bill | Re-created with an explicit `22023 in_group` right after the host check, for any target | Rely on the members-only trigger alone (GR-10) | Adding a member who is already on the bill is a silent no-op that never reaches an INSERT trigger, so criterion 28 couldn't pass. The other three (`join_bill`, `remove_participant`, `leave_bill`) are unchanged and refused by the triggers | Open |
| GR-T5-2 | `remove_participant` / `leave_bill` for a member who isn't removable (has a claim) | Their existing `cannot_remove` / `cannot_leave` comes first; `in_group` only when the delete would happen | Re-create both with an explicit group check | Both are refusals; the screen hides these controls on group bills (A1.8). Keeps other features' function bodies untouched | Open |
| GR-T5-3 | Attach check order | not_allowed → same group (no-op) → `already_in_group` → `participants_not_members` → `not_complete` | `not_complete` before `participants_not_members` | Follows the spec's list order; the app shows "invite them first" before "finish claiming" | Open |
| GR-T5-4 | `detach_bill_from_group` on a bill not in a group | No-op success (host only; non-host 42501) | 42501 or an error | A retry after a lost response succeeds, same as attach's same-group retry (S3). A group change between the unlocked read and the lock retries up to 3 times, then `40001 bill_changed` (shown as `network`; only reachable with repeated detach+attach races) | Open |
| GR-T5-5 | What the mark trigger refuses | Any write leaving `settled_at`, `settled_cents` or `not_received_cents` non-null on a group bill | Only `settled_*` | Also covers `mark_not_received` and any future writer; attach clears marks after setting `group_id`, so its own clearing passes | Open |
| GR-T5-6 | Lock mode on bills in `join_group` | FOR KEY SHARE, ascending id (the lock the participant inserts take anyway) | FOR UPDATE as attach/leave | Membership changes are already serialized by the group's NO KEY UPDATE; a lighter lock doesn't block claims while someone joins | Open |
| GR-T5-7 | Leave history for bills | Hosts or pays a group bill, or has a claim on one (bills locked FOR UPDATE first, B2d) | Also refuse participants with no claims | C-G10; a participant with no claims has no money on the bill | Open |
| GR-T5-8 | `people_i_split_with` with group links | `my_links()` adds one row per (co-member, group) with `bill_id` null and the later `joined_at`; `shared_bills = count(distinct bill_id)`, ranking keys unchanged | A separate group-links function | One place for rule L1 (+ U-G5); `add_participant` also sees co-members as linked (GR-N5) | Open |
| GR-T5-9 | Mark on the payer's own row at attach | Not converted (it would fail `from <> to`), still cleared | Raise | Can't happen today (mark_paid refuses `is_payer`; set_payer clears marks); avoids a 23514 if old data has one | Open |
| GR-T5-10 | Several payers (GR-M5) | No refusal added: no `bill_payers` table existed on 2026-11-27 | — | The who-paid session adds the refusal with its table | Open (for who-paid) |

T6 group snapshot (supabase-engineer, 2026-11-27; `supabase/migrations/20261127061004_group_snapshot.sql`, test `supabase/tests/database/group_snapshot.test.sql`, 46 assertions). Size spike (criterion 37), 40 bills × 30 rows × 6 claimers on every row (7,200 claims), through PostgREST: HTTP 200, 465,162 bytes (≈0.47 MB, a little above A1.7's 0.35–0.45 estimate, under the 1 MB budget), all 1,200 rows present (above `max_rows = 1000`, so a scalar jsonb isn't cut), ≈50–60 ms warm (250 ms first call), 17–33 ms inside the DB; `fetchGroupSnapshot`'s parse + `groupBalances` on it took 27 ms in Jest:

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GR-T6-1 | Where the parser and C-G11's text differ | Follow `lib/groups.ts`: rows `{id, total_cents, kind, name, claims}` with `name` only on unclaimed rows (A1.7); payments add `method` (A2.1); a required `not_received` key with `from` / `to` (not `from_user` / `to_user`) and `undone_at`. C-G11's body still shows the pre-A1 shape; T12 reconciles the spec text | Follow the spec body | The parser fails closed on any other shape; the coordinator said parser wins | Open (reconciled by T12: spec A3.1, GR-T12-1) |
| GR-T6-2 | Which recipient-undone payments `not_received` holds (GR-M6) | `undone_reason = 'not_received'`, `undone_at > now() - 30 days`, the viewer is from **or** to; newest first (`undone_at desc, id`, the partial index T4 made) | Only where the viewer is the sender (the only one with a notice) | The coordinator's brief says from or to; the recipient's copy can show it too, and it's at most a few rows | Open |
| GR-T6-3 | Orders inside the payload | members `joined_at, user_id`; bills `created_at, id`; rows `group_position, unit_index`; `participant_ids` and expense `shares` by `user_id`; expenses and payments `created_at, id` | Leave inner arrays unordered | Stable output, so the same data gives the same snapshot (diffs, memoization); every order is served by an existing index | Open |
| GR-T6-4 | How "members only" is checked | Inside the same SELECT as the payload (a condition on the group row), then 42501 `not_allowed` if nothing came back; signed out, null id, unknown group, non-member and a member who left all get the same answer | A separate membership query first | One statement over one snapshot (C-G11): the membership and the data can't come from different moments | Open |
| GR-T6-5 | Execute grants | `authenticated` only (`anon` and PUBLIC revoked; `service_role` keeps Supabase's default) | Revoke from `service_role` too | It's a read with an `auth.uid()` check, so `service_role` (no uid) always gets 42501 anyway | Open |

T7 assigning items (supabase-engineer, 2026-11-27; `supabase/migrations/20261127062139_assign_item.sql`, test `supabase/tests/database/assign_item.test.sql`, 64 assertions; `group_bills.test.sql`'s every-kind assertion now lists 24 kinds):

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GR-T7-1 | A target who left the group while `assign_item` waited for the bill lock | 22023 `claims_changed` (the screen refetches). The caller leaving, or the bill leaving the group, while waiting stays 42501 `not_allowed`; a target who isn't a member at the first (unlocked) check stays 42501 | 42501 for every under-lock change, as A1.6's sentence reads | A1.6 says "42501 if any changed", criterion 103 says a target removed by `leave_group` concurrently surfaces as `claims_changed`. The two disagree only for the target; `claims_changed` is the answer the app can act on (refetch shows the member gone). **Spec text needs reconciling (T12)** | Open (reconciled by T12: spec A3.2, confirmed live) |
| GR-T7-2 | Shape of `p_expected` | Same rules as `p_parts` (object, uuid keys, integers 1..20); badly shaped -> 22023 `invalid_parts`. Its keys need not be members (a stale map may name someone who left) | Accept anything and let the comparison fail as `claims_changed` | The wrapper (`lib/groups.ts` `assignItem`) already checks both with `isPartsMap`; a malformed token is a client bug, not a stale screen | Open |
| GR-T7-3 | How maps compare | By uuid (any letter case) and integer value: both maps are canonicalised before comparing, so upper-case keys for the current map are a no-op success | Compare the raw jsonb | The no-op retry (S3) must not depend on how the client spelled the ids; duplicate spellings of one uuid are refused as `invalid_parts` (criterion 41) | Open |
| GR-T7-4 | The row deleted (by `update_bill`) while waiting for the bill lock | 22023 `claims_changed` | 42501 as for a missing row at the first check | The screen showed the row; a refetch shows it gone. A row that never existed still gets 42501 (one answer for "can't see it") | Open |
| GR-T7-5 | `assigned` / `unassigned` details | `{ user_id }`, plus `unit: [unit_index, group_size]` only for a row of a multi-unit group (key absent otherwise); no `split` | `{ unit, split, user_id }` as T7's bullet lists | GR-T11-3 and `lib/activityText.ts` read `{ user_id, unit? }`; the split of an assigned row shows through `split_changed` and the claims themselves. `claimed` / `unclaimed` keep `{ unit, split }` exactly | Open |
| GR-T7-6 | `split_changed` from `assign_item` | One event, only when a person who stays gets different parts; `before` / `after` are the row's full maps before and after the call (as `set_item_split` logs them). A fresh row assigned with uneven parts logs only `assigned` events (criterion 39) | Also log `split_changed` for uneven inserts; or restrict before/after to the people who stay | Follows C-G12 and criterion 39/40 literally; full maps make the existing "changed the split: … (was …)" sentence read right | Open |
| GR-T7-7 | `line_items.split_set_by` / `split_set_at` | Set to the caller when the call changes a stayer's parts or inserts a claim with parts other than 1 (so the row reads "split by Cy" rather than an anonymous "Custom split") | Only when stayers' parts change (as `set_item_split`) | `assign_item` is the only way to set parts on a fresh row in a group bill; leaving `split_set_by` null would hide who set the uneven split | Open |
| GR-T7-8 | Execute grants | `authenticated` only (`anon` and PUBLIC revoked; `service_role` keeps Supabase's default) | Revoke from `service_role` too | Same as GR-T6-5: `service_role` has no `auth.uid()`, so it always gets 42501 `not_allowed`; events can't be forged through it | Open |
| GR-T7-9 | The under-lock re-check also requires the caller to be on the bill | Yes (42501 otherwise): `private.lock_bill_key_share` takes the lock only for a participant, so this proves the lock is held | Lock inline with `for key share` (the function is security definer) | Keeps the one helper every claim path uses (spec T7 bullet, lock order) and can't run unlocked if a member somehow lacks a participant row | Open |

### 2026-11-27 · Who paid, Task 2b (main session)
- **WP-51 (money, accepted by main session):** the "current plan key" also covers which payments were made under an earlier key (`currentPlanKey` in `lib/transfers.ts`), so a stale-screen payment or an undo can't leave current payments pinned to routes that no longer exist (found by the 2,000-run simulation). It changes nothing when there's no earlier-key history. Status: Open for the user's review.
- **WP-52:** refunds to overpayers repeat until none are left (a single pass could leave a refunder owed). Status: Open.
- **WP-53 (user, 2026-11-27; decided):** the "Who paid?" editor is a separate page (session 0b's `app/(app)/bills/[id]/payers.tsx`), not the spec's bottom sheet. Task 9 shrinks to the "Paid by…" line, and `payersSheetModel` isn't built. Spec Amendment A2. Status: Decided.

### 2026-11-27 · Who paid, Task 8 (implementer): Home markers and Activity
- **WP-T8-1:** both `blocked_payers` pills reuse the `waiting_on_others` marker: the host sees "Fix who paid" (accent) and everyone else sees "Who paid is being updated" (muted). The copy is picked by `hostId`. Alternative: a new `fix_payers` kind, which needs a line in `MARKER_GROUPS` plus a fixture. Why: no change to `homeSections`, and both pills already land in "Waiting on you" (D10). Open.
- **WP-T8-2:** the `blocked_payers` pill shows for every guest, even one who hasn't claimed, because the spec says "others". `new` and `unclaimed` still come first. Open.
- **WP-T8-3:** on routes, what you owe beats what you're owed: someone who owes and is owed sees "You owe…". Open.
- **WP-T8-4:** several marks are summed into one pill, and the method word ("Sent") is used only when every mark shares it. Open.
- **WP-T8-5:** the holder in T0 (paid, and passed it on) reads "Settled up". Open.
- **WP-T8-6:** `payers_changed` Activity copy:
  - "Hana changed who paid: Hana $60.00, Sam $40.21" (the lead first), or "… to Dee" when one person paid;
  - "Pat's $9.00 to Sam is passed on to Dee", or "2 payments to Sam are passed on";
  - after a deleted account: "Who paid went back to Hana", plus "1 payment with a deleted account was removed" (this wording is the implementer's; A1 gives none);
  - no final period on a line that stands alone.
  Open.
- **WP-T7-1:** on a split bill, a difference on one route reads "It's now $X to Hana. You've sent $Y." rather than "Your share is now…", because one route's amount isn't your share. Open.
- **WP-T7-2:** when you owe 2 or more people, the footer shows "Settle up" and the caption "You owe 2 people · $30.00", with no Pay button. Each card has its own Pay, and only the first card's is filled until you mark it. Open.
- **WP-T7-3:** who-paid confirms say that payments still count and get passed on (nothing is cleared), so their buttons are primary. Names are listed up to 3, then "Hana, Ana and 2 others". The stale-plan hints are "Who paid needs fixing before anyone can settle up" and "Waiting for Hana to update who paid". Open.
- **WP-T7-4:** the payer's line "You paid $50.00 · your share is $25.00" shows only on split bills. Open.
- **WP-T10-1:** the S12 leave/remove reason also covers a single payer who isn't the host: "Sam paid the bill. Change who paid first." and "You paid the bill. Ask Hana to change who paid before you leave." The spec only covers co-payers. Without this, that row would show no Remove button and no reason. Open.
- **WP-T10-2:** every mark and difference write sends the plan key (`p_plan_key`), on one-payer bills too, as A-28 says. It used to be sent only on split bills. Open.
- **WP-T10-3 (user, 2026-11-27; decided):** with several cards on Settle, each card keeps its own Venmo/Cash chooser. Decided.
- **WP-R9 (main session, from review R9):** the back control on the Who paid page reads "Back" rather than "Settle up", because the page opens from the bill screen too. Open.
- **WP-R10 (main session, from review R10):** in % mode, a row that rounds to $0.00 is refused with a row error, instead of quietly dropping that payer. Open.
- **WP-R11 (main session, from review R11):** after a failed refresh, Home drops a kept split plan only when the lead payer changes. If only the co-payers change, the old pill can show until the next good refresh. Accepted for now and listed in before-production. Open.
- **WP-X2-1 (amends spec D10):** on split-paid bills, a guest's Home row shows "Your share" = `shareCents`, not D10's `owesCents`. Alternative: `owesCents`. That matches the pill when earlier money is passed on ("You owe Hana $26.00" next to "Your share $14.00"), but it reads $0.00 after paying or overpaying. Why: it's the same meaning as single-payer bills, and it never shows $0. Open.
- **WP-X2-2:** a co-payer who owes the lead sits in "Waiting on you" but sees the bill total, as every payer does. Alternative: their own amount owed. Open.
- **WP-X2-3:** an incomplete split bill (a plain blocked plan) names no payers, so only the lead counts as a payer. A non-lead co-payer sits in "Waiting on you" until every item is claimed. A fix needs the payers on `BillSummary`. Open.
- **WP-X2-4:** a blocked_payers bill always goes in "Waiting on you", for co-payers too. Otherwise a co-payer would see "Owed to you" next to a "being updated" pill. Open.
- **WP-T8-7:** a co-payer who isn't the lead, on an incomplete split bill, still sees "Claimed · not ready to pay", because the check reads the lead's `payerId`. Open.
- **WP-54 (user, 2026-11-27; decided):** the copy "Undo all $X" becomes "$X payment didn't go through", and the Home pill "Paid · $X back to you" becomes "You are owed $X". Only these strings were rewritten in the single-payer regression fixture, and a reverse substitution was checked to give a byte-identical file. Status: Decided.
- GR-M7 (main session, 2026-11-27; resolves GR-T12-3): group Activity names a member who has left as "A former member" (e.g. "A former member left the group"), since the snapshot only lists current members and member_left details are `{}`. Alternative: store display_name in member_joined/member_left details (a DB change). Why: no DB change in the MVP; leaving is rare (needs no history). Open.
- GR-M8 (main session, 2026-11-27; GR-T12-4): service_role keeps write grants on groups, group_members, group_hidden, expenses, expense_shares, matching bills/claims; only group_events and group_payments revoke them. Accepted as consistent; revisit with the before-production security review. Open.

| GR-T15-4 | "Discard?" on every way out (criterion 73) | `usePreventRemove` catches the back control, the iOS back-swipe (the native stack blocks the swipe and asks) and Android back; the footer then shows "Discard this expense?" / "Discard your changes?" with Keep editing / Discard. **Browser back on web can't be stopped**: expo-router handles it with `resetRoot`, which skips `beforeRemove` (checked in `node_modules/expo-router/build/fork/useLinking.js`). Reload and closing the tab use the browser's own prompt (`beforeunload`) | Push a dummy history entry to catch browser back | Faking history entries would fight expo-router's own history. Check it and try again." with "Show their changes" (which loads the saved version into the form). The next Save sends the refetched version, so it saves yours over theirs without asking again | Block Save until you load theirs; a "Save mine anyway" second step | The message has already told them; one more step would be clutter. A form you haven't touched quietly picks up a newer saved version, so a conflict only happens to someone who really typed. **UX: needs your look** | Open |
| GR-T15-6 | Deleted while you edit, or a bad `?expense=` | One fixed message, "This expense isn't in the group anymore.", with "Go to the group". The id is never shown. What you typed in that edit is gone | Keep the form and offer "Add as new" | There's nothing left to save to; rare | Open |

After a save or delete, the app goes back to the group (it pops to the group, or replaces this screen on a cold link), and the group refetches on focus | Errors only after a Save tap | Criterion 71 requires a disabled Save; the line is there so a disabled button always says why | Open |

### 2026-11-27 · Groups T18 part 1 (product-engineer): "Who had this?" chips in the item sheet

Files: new `components/AssignChips.tsx`, `lib/useAssignItem.ts`, `__tests__/assignChips.test.tsx`; edited `components/ItemSheet.tsx` (optional `assign` prop), `lib/itemAssign.ts` (+ test: `chipNames`, `assignChipHint`, `ASSIGN_COPY`), `__tests__/itemSheet.test.tsx`, `__tests__/itemSheetNative.test.tsx`. Not wired from the bill screen yet (part 2). `components/ui/*` untouched.

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GR-T18-1 | Shape of the `assign` prop | `{ lineItemId, members: AvatarPerson[], current, refetch, version, finished }`: the sheet calls `assignItem` itself and waits on the screen's numbered refetch (useBill's `refetch()` / `version` / `finished`) | `{ members, current, onAssign }` with the screen doing the write | The busy-until-refetch, `claims_changed` and failed-refetch endings live in one tested hook; the bill screen only passes what `useBill` already gives it | Open |
| GR-T18-2 | One write at a time | While any chip or the split save is in flight, every chip and the steppers are disabled (a11y `disabled`); only the tapped chip shows a spinner. Others aren't dimmed | Let chips be tapped in parallel | Each write sends the shown map as `p_expected`; a second write before the refetch would always be `claims_changed`. Not dimming avoids a flicker on every tap | Open |
| GR-T18-3 | Chip text | First name; "You" for the viewer; when two members share a first name both show their full names; the label keeps the full name ("Ben Okafor, had this") | Full names always | Spec says first name; short chips fit more per line; duplicates can't be confused | Open |
| GR-T18-4 | Checked look | Check mark + `primary` outline + tinted fill + semibold name; unchecked: plus mark on the muted fill. Marks share one fixed slot so the chip never changes width | Fill color only | Shape, not color only; the fills are ~1.1:1 against the sheet, the outline is 5.47:1 (light) / 9.29:1 (dark). A chip is a checkbox, so the input outline fits ADR 0020 | Open |
| GR-T18-5 | Hints and announcements | Hint "Puts Ben Okafor on this item" / "Takes you off this item" (discount: "this discount"). On iOS/Android the result is announced once the refetch shows it ("Ben Okafor, had this"; a split save says "Split saved"); web relies on the checkbox's state change | No announcement | The chip is busy, then flips: without an announcement a VoiceOver user may not hear the result | Open |
| GR-T18-6 | Errors | `claims_changed` → refetch, "Someone changed this item. Check and try again." (sheet stays open, an edited split keeps its draft); `not_allowed` / `invalid_parts` / `network` → their `GROUP_ERROR_COPY`; any other code reads as `network`. A failed refetch → "Couldn't refresh this item. Try again." with Try again (hint "Loads the latest for this item"), chips enabled. No timeout for a refetch that never answers | Close the sheet on `claims_changed` | Spec criterion 84; lessons 2026-11-24/25 on refetch endings. The timeout question is still the user's (2026-11-24) | Open |
| GR-T18-7 | Group bill copy differences | No "Nobody has claimed this yet." (all chips unchecked say it) and no "Claim this item to change how it's split." (any member may change it). "Split differently" keeps its name (the spec calls it "Change split") | Keep both lines | Less on screen; the stepper's existing label and tests stay | Open |
| GR-T18-8 | Split save on a group bill | The draft stays open until the refetch shows the saved split; a failed save keeps the parts and re-enables Save split; Reset to even also goes through `assign_item` | Close the draft at once (as the non-group sheet does, which is optimistic through the screen) | No optimistic state on group bills, so a failure must keep what was set | Open |
| GR-T18-9 | Closing the sheet mid-write | The write still finishes and the bill is refetched; no message (the sheet is gone) | Block closing | The bill screen outlives the sheet; the row on the bill shows the result | Open |

| GR-T17-11 | Where the `in_group` join copy lives until part 2 | `lib/billGroup.ts`: `JOIN_IN_GROUP_COPY` (= `GROUP_ERROR_COPY.in_group`) and `joinBillProblem(error: string)`, so `app/b/[code].tsx` works before and after `BillError` gains `in_group`. | GR-T17-12 | `BillGroupLine` contents | "In Tahoe trip" as a link (opens the group) plus, for the host, a quiet "Take out" that opens the bill's group screen; for the host of a bill in no group, "Move to a group" only when the caller passes `canMove`; nothing while the group is unknown | Always show "Move to a group" to the host | Most bills never go in a group: the line would be noise for people with no groups. **Your call (see report): show it only when the host is in at least one group?** | Open |
| GR-T17-13 | Join error announced | The `/b/<code>` error message now has `accessibilityRole="alert"` | Leave it silent | Errors are announced (agent rule); no test depended on its role | Open |

### 2026-11-27 · Groups review fixes (implementer): findings R1–R10 of the T1–T16 review (verdict APPROVE)

Spec note: Amendment A4 at the end of [docs/specs/2026-11-26-groups-mvp.md](specs/2026-11-26-groups-mvp.md). GR-RV-2 and GR-RV-6 were decided by the main session; the rest are how each finding was fixed.

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GR-RV-1 | A refetch superseded by a newer one (R1) | `lib/useGroupSnapshot.ts` moves `finished` only for the newest refetch; an older answer is ignored and doesn't count as finished. Waiters (`useGroupSettle`, the group, Balances and expense screens) keep their predicate unchanged and are released by the newer refetch | Make every waiter also wait while `started > clearAt` | One fix in the hook covers every waiter; the newest refetch always ends (GR-RV-2), and its number is above every older one. `lib/useBill.ts` has the same pattern and belongs to another session (main session tells them) | Open |
| GR-RV-2 | Time limit on group calls (R2, main session) | Every group rpc (`callRpc` in `lib/groups.ts`: all writes and `group_snapshot`) and `useGroupSnapshot`'s refetch give up after `GROUP_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` = 15 s and read as `network`. The native Pay record keeps its 4 s limit | No limit; a shorter one | Safe because every write reuses its client id, so a retry after a lost answer is a no-op. 15 s is a starting guess (docs/before-production.md). Table reads (`fetchGroupEvents`, `fetchBillGroup`) aren't covered | Open |
| GR-RV-3 | Late record after a failed Venmo open (R3) | Native: when the record timed out and Venmo then fails to open, no `undoPayment` runs at once (there's nothing to undo yet, so it could only fail and say "Couldn't undo…" falsely). The late record is undone when it lands; only if that undo fails does "Couldn't undo. Try again." show, on the payment's own card | Run the undo anyway and hide its error | The person sees an error only when there is one. Mobile web's "record refused → close the tab" branch had no test; it has one now | Open |
| GR-RV-4 | Group names in Activity (R4) | `lib/activityText.ts` drops control characters (`\p{Cc}`) and lone surrogates only, like `cleanNoteName`; format characters (ZWJ) stay, so "Tahoe 👨‍👩‍👧" reads whole | Keep stripping `\p{Cf}` | Lesson 2026-11-25 on text sent to other apps: keep ZWJ | Open |
| GR-RV-5 | "From contacts" hints on a group invite (R5) | `contactSendHints(kind)` in `lib/contactInviteView.ts` and an optional `kind` prop on `ContactInvite` (default `'bill'`); `GroupInvite` passes `'group'` ("Opens a text with the group link filled in") | A second copy object | Bill callers see the same words as before | Open |
| GR-RV-6 | Converted "I paid" marks in group Activity (R6, main session) | The `bill_mark` branch of `payment_recorded` in `lib/groupActivityText.ts` is gone (dead: the DB doesn't log a `payment_recorded` for a converted mark); `bill_added` / `moved_to_group` describe the conversion. No DB change | Keep the branch | Dead code with its own test reads like a behaviour | Open |
| GR-RV-7 | Who paid what for someone who claimed nothing (R7) | A receipt is left out of a person's costs when their share is $0 and they didn't pay it. A payer with a $0 share still sees it. Expenses are unchanged (a $0 share in an equal expense is a real part of the split, A3) | Leave $0 rows in | A receipt lists everyone on the bill; "$0.00 share" there is noise | Open |
| GR-RV-8 | A3.1's `not_received` sentence (R8) | Reworded: the third member's filter is the snapshot's, not a privacy boundary (`undone_reason` on `group_payments` is readable by every member) | Leave it | The old words read like an RLS rule | Open |
| GR-RV-9 | Storing the desktop "Yes" (R9) | The inline check's key is `<group id>.<payment id>.0` (`groupSentCheckKey(groupId, paymentId)`): the id names the payment, the time part is a fixed 0. The Yes is stored at the tap, so a failed refetch then leaving can't make the check ask again | Keep `<created_at ms>` in the key and wait for the refetch | Payment ids are fresh uuids. Pre-production, so no stored answers to migrate (an old `shown` answer would only ask once more) | Open |
| GR-RV-10 | Activity sentence nudge (R10) | A named constant `SENTENCE_NUDGE = (24 - type.body.lineHeight) / 2` (1 pt) in `app/(app)/groups/[id]/activity.tsx`, with a comment | A spacing token | No token is that small; it's an optical alignment, not spacing. The bill Activity screen has the same `marginTop: 1` (another session's file) | Open |
| GR-T18-10 | Review R2-S1: what a group split save starts from | On a group bill the split draft, "changed", Save split and Reset to even all take their people and parts from `assign.current` (the map also sent as `p_expected`), never from the `claimers` prop; the steppers still render over `claimers` | Build from `claimers` (as before) | A stale or optimistic list could silently add or drop someone's claim. Tests: a person missing from the list is kept, an extra optimistic claim isn't added, Reset to even covers exactly the server map | Open |
| GR-T18-11 | Web state of the chips | Each chip spreads `ariaProps({ checked, disabled, busy })` (splitit-app-92's `lib/ariaProps.ts`) next to `accessibilityState`, so the browser gets `aria-checked` / `aria-disabled` / `aria-busy`; tested on react-native-web static markup | accessibilityState only | react-native-web 0.21 drops accessibilityState (QA F1) | Open |
| GR-T17-14 | Review R2-N1: A1 S1b up front | `attachCheck` takes the bill's state (`sharesComplete(...).complete` from `lib/split.ts`, `allZero(...)` from `lib/settle.ts`, over `splitInputFor` of the loaded bill: the same rules as the DB's `bill_is_complete` / `bill_all_zero`). With any "I paid" mark and an incomplete or all-$0 bill, it shows "Finish claiming before moving it into the group." as a warning banner and no Add button. Order: invite-first, then this, then ready. A bill with no marks can still move in while incomplete. The DB's `not_complete` stays the backstop | Only the DB refusal after a tap | Says what to do before the person taps; the copy is the spec's | Open |
| GR-T17-15 | Radio rows on web | The row moved to `components/GroupChoiceRow.tsx` and spreads `ariaProps({ checked, disabled })` (splitit-app-92's helper) next to `accessibilityState`; react-native-web omits `aria-disabled="false"`, so the web test checks `aria-checked` and `aria-disabled="true"` only when disabled | Keep the row inside the route file | The web-markup test (uiPrimitives pattern) needs to render the row on its own | Open |
| GR-RV-11 | Shared helpers (round 2) | `lib/useGroupSettle.ts`, `lib/groups.ts` and `lib/useGroupSnapshot.ts` use `within` / `TIMED_OUT` from `lib/asyncTimeout.ts`; the group Settle screen uses `isMobileWeb()` from `lib/webEnv.ts`. The local copies (and round 1's `withTimeLimit`) are gone | Keep the copies | One race helper and one phone test for the app; behaviour unchanged (tests unchanged and green) | Open |
| GR-RV-12 | A late Pay record that answers `network` (round 2, R2-N2) | After the record timed out and Venmo failed to open, a late `network` answer (a lost answer or the 15 s limit) may still have landed. The hook refetches, and if that snapshot has the payment, undoes it (an error shows on its card only if the undo fails). If that refetch fails, the payment (if any) shows later with its inline check and "No, undo" | Show the "Did your $X go through?" check at once | No payment stays behind a Venmo that never opened (SV-7), without asking the person about a send they know failed | Open |
| GR-RV-13 | Web state on groups' hand-built controls (round 2) | `ariaProps` on the expense split checkboxes and payer radios (`components/ExpenseFormRows.tsx`), Record a payment's person radios (`components/GroupPaymentForm.tsx`, `PersonOption` now exported) and Who paid what's person rows. Those rows moved to `components/WhoPaidWhatPersonRow.tsx` so they can be rendered alone. `IconButton` gains an optional `expanded` prop (sets `accessibilityState.expanded` and `aria-expanded`; omitted, nothing changes), used by the group's ⋯ options button. The Add list, the QR toggle and Record a payment are `TextLink`s, which already pass `ariaProps`; Home's hidden groups use `DisclosureRow`; Activity has no toggle | A new `expanded` IconButton variant component | react-native-web 0.21 drops `accessibilityState`; tested on react-native-web markup (`__tests__/groupAriaWeb.test.tsx`) | Open |

### 2026-11-27 · Groups: "Your groups" screen (product-engineer), route `/groups`

Files: new `app/(app)/groups/index.tsx`, `components/GroupsSection.tsx` (new `variant="page"` prop; Home's default unchanged), `lib/groupHomeView.ts` (`GROUPS_PAGE_COPY`) + test, new `__tests__/groupsList.test.tsx`. The Home header icon that opens `/groups` is splitit-app-96's.

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GR-GL-1 | How the screen gets its list | Reuse `GroupsSection` with `variant="page"`: same rows, snapshot reads, hidden groups, Unhide, error and refetch on focus. The page variant drops the block's heading and "New group" link, shows a spinner while loading, and says "No groups yet. Start one for a trip or a shared house." instead of "Start a group" | Copy the block into the screen | One row model and one set of load rules; Home's tests unchanged and green | Open |
| GR-GL-2 | Where "New group" sits | One primary full-width button under the title, shown in every state (loading, error, empty, filled); the empty message sits under it | Button only under the empty message; a bottom bar like Home's "start a bill" | Nothing moves when the list lands, and you can start a group while the list is loading or down. A bottom bar is easier to reach one-handed but adds chrome; easy to switch | Open |
| GR-GL-3 | Status reads on a long list | Every group gets a row, but only the first 10 visible (`GROUPS_HOME_DETAIL_LIMIT`, the newest joined) read a snapshot; the rest show "4 people" and no initials. Same cost as Home's block: up to 10 `group_snapshot` reads per focus | Read every group's snapshot here; a lower limit | Bounded cost; the fix for both screens is the `my_groups()` status column already in docs/before-production.md (GR-T13-2) | Open |
| GR-GL-4 | Back | "Home", `dismissTo('/')`, like the group screen | Plain back | Works on a cold deep link with no history; this screen is only ever one step from Home | Open |
| GR-GL-5 | Signed out on `/groups` | The `(app)` layout sends you to sign-in; after signing in the root resume goes to Home (it only resumes invites), not back to `/groups` | Remember and resume `/groups` | Same as every other signed-in route today; changing it means editing the root resume effect | Open |
| GR-GL-6 | "New group" in two places | Home's Groups block keeps its "New group" link, and this screen has the button | Drop one | The brief asked for both. Once Home has the Groups icon, Home's block may not need its own link, or the block itself: **your call** (Home is splitit-app-96's) | Open. **Needs your look** |

### Home: one "Add receipt" button (2026-11-27, user request)
- **HOME-ADD-1 (Changed: superseded by the (+) tab button (FEED-19), 2026-11-28):** *Home no longer has this bar. The raised (+) in the tab bar opens the scan screen instead, with the label "Add receipt, opens a photo or typing the items" ([ADR 0025](learning/decisions/0025-tab-bar-and-activity.md)). The text below is kept as history.* Home's bar now has a single primary button, "Add receipt" (hint "Take a photo of the receipt, or type the items"), which opens the scan screen. The "Type items instead" link is gone, because the scan screen already offers both "Take or upload photo" and "Enter items" (and only "Enter items" when the camera or reader isn't available). **Why:** the user asked for it: fewer choices on Home, and the choice is made one screen later. Cost: typing items now takes one extra tap.
- **HOME-ADD-2 (open):** the scan screen's title is still "Scan receipt". Renaming it to "Add receipt" would match the button, but the same title also shows while a photo is being read. Left as it is for now.

Files: `app/(app)/index.tsx` (one `blocks.push(<GroupsSection key="groups" />)` in `groupBlocks()`, between "Owed to you" and "Settled"; a Groups `IconButton` in the header), `__tests__/homeLayout.test.tsx` (new tests; `my_groups` stub), `__tests__/homeMarkers.test.tsx` (`my_groups` stub only). "Split a bill" bar not moved.

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

Never during the first load or its settle wait, and never twice | Only in `groupBlocks()` (groups invisible with no bills) | People with groups but no bills still see them | Resolved (2026-11-27, main session): option 2 |
