## 2026-11-27 · Group claim status (spec: [docs/specs/2026-11-27-group-claim-status.md](specs/2026-11-27-group-claim-status.md))
Decided by the product-manager under "build without blocking". The user's request and the relayed "slight yellow hue" follow-up are quoted in the spec.

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GCS-1 | When a group bill "needs me" | Some row is unclaimed, I have no claim on the bill, and I haven't said "not part of this". Claims win over a stale opt-out (spec C1) | Any unclaimed row, even when I've claimed; only bills I'm the host of | Matches "bills where you've not claimed any items". Someone who claimed already has done their part | Open |
| GCS-2 | Per-item or per-bill "not part of this" | Bill-level only | Per-item "not mine" on each row (the user floated it) | One tap covers "I wasn't at that dinner". Per-item needs a row-level table, and adds a third state to every row next to Claim / Mine. Unclaimed items still wait for someone either way | Open (needs your look) |
| GCS-3 | Who sees "not part of this" | Only me. No Activity line, no Realtime, not in anyone else's snapshot | Show "Ben wasn't part of this" to the group, or in the bill's Activity | Same owner-only pattern as `bill_seen` (lesson 2026-11-24). It only silences my reminder, so others don't need it. Also keeps out of `activityText.ts` (splitit-app-96) | Open (needs your look) |
| GCS-4 | What happens to an opt-out when I get a claim | The database deletes it (trigger on claims insert), including when someone else assigns me an item | Keep it and ignore it while I have claims | Otherwise, after an unclaim, an old "not part" would silently hide the reminder again | Open |
| GCS-5 | Amounts on Home's group rows | Shown: "You owe $X" / "You're owed $X" (reverses GR-20) | Keep GR-20 (no amounts) | The user asked for "you need to pay money, you need to request money" on Home | Open (needs your look) |
| GCS-6 | What "Settled up" means on a Home row | I'm within 1¢ (`isSettledUp` of my net) and nothing is waiting | Every member settled (the old "Settled" rule) | The other states are all about me. Copy changed from "Settled" to "Settled up" to match the group screen | Open (needs your look) |
| GCS-7 | How Home shows "Claim your items" | The caption in `colors.warning` (amber), with no row tint | Tint the Home row too; a dot or pill | Home's list is dense, and the tint is saved for the places you act | Changed: user, 2026-11-28 (U10, FEED-20). Group rows now have hues in two cases only: a claim row is amber (`warningSurface`), and a settled row is grey (`surfaceMuted`) with a check next to "Settled up". Tinted rows dim their content when pressed. The rows moved from Home to the Groups tab. The user approved the visuals on 2026-11-28 |
| GCS-8 | Tint inside a group bill | Every unclaimed row, until I say "not part of this" | Only while I've claimed nothing; never once I've claimed | The relayed follow-up asks for the tint on each still-unclaimed item. After opting out, nothing there is for me | Open |
| GCS-9 | Order of the group's waiting list | Bills that need me first, then the rest, each in snapshot order | Snapshot order only | What needs you is on top | Open |
| GCS-10 | The bill screen when the not-part read fails | Tint shown, line hidden, no alert | Hide the tint; show an error | Fail toward reminding. The line can't be trusted without the read | Open |
| GCS-11 | A bill taken out of the group and put back | Old opt-outs stay and apply again | Clear them on detach | Rare, and it only hides a reminder. Avoids editing `detach_bill_from_group` | Open |
| GCS-12 | Home claim copy | "Claim your items on 1 bill" / "on N bills" | "You have N bills with unclaimed items"; "N bills need your claims" | Says what to do, short enough for a row caption | Open (copy: needs your look) |
| GCS-13 | Group-screen action copy | "I wasn't part of this" and, after it, "You weren't part of this" + "Undo". The bill screen asks "Nothing here was yours?" | "Not part of this"; "Not mine" | Close to the user's words, and reads as a sentence | Open (copy: needs your look) |
| GCS-14 | Tint token | The existing `warningSurface` (light `#FFFAEB`, dark `#33260B`) | A new, stronger token | The user asked for a "slight" hue. Text contrast is ≥ 4.5:1 on it, and text always goes with it. Strength is checked in the spec's criterion 33 | Open |
| GCS-15 | The "I wasn't part of this" / Undo hints on web | Leave as is: the hints ("Stops reminding you about this receipt. It doesn't change anyone's share.") reach iOS/Android screen readers only; react-native-web drops hints. The change of caption and tint shows the result | Add a visible reassurance line under the link on every needs-me row | Keeps rows clean; criterion 29 asked for the hint in the label or a caption, so this is a small departure. Raised by T5 (2026-11-27) | Open |
| GCS-16 | Where "I wasn't part of this" / Undo sits when several receipts wait | Each waiting receipt is its own small card (rounded, continuous corners; tinted with no edge when it needs you, plain otherwise), stacked 8 pt apart under "Not in balances yet". The link (and any error) sits inside that card, directly under the caption. Same copy, labels, hints, busy/error per bill and order | One shared card with dividers and the link under each row (before); the link right-aligned on the caption's line | User: "i wasnt on this should be next to the bill card - so if there are multiple it doesnt get confusing". In one shared card the link sat next to a divider and could belong to either row. Under the caption, not beside it, because long captions and large text would squeeze a side-by-side link. Raised after T5 (2026-11-27) | Open |

## 2026-11-28 · Tab bar and Activity (spec: [docs/specs/2026-11-28-tab-bar-and-activity.md](specs/2026-11-28-tab-bar-and-activity.md))
Decided by the product-manager under "build without blocking". The user's design (U1–U12) is in the spec and isn't repeated here.

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FEED-1 | Where the feed is built | On the client, from existing RLS reads, with one pure rule function (`classifyFeedEvent`) | A security-definer read function `my_activity` | User (U11): no new stored state, and a migration only if it's genuinely needed. Every read already exists | Open |
| FEED-2 | Bills have no names | Captions say "Maya's bill"; sentences don't repeat the place | Add a bill name | No name column; the "Taco Night" examples were illustrative | Open |
| FEED-3 | Feed contents | Full history; unread lines get a coloured marker and a dot for one visit | Show only unseen items | "No cap" and pagination point to history | Open (needs your look) |
| FEED-4 | Seen boundary | A seen time equal to the item's time counts as seen | Strictly later | Simple, and tested both ways | Open |
| FEED-5 | Where the feed starts | At your `joined_at` on each bill or group | All history | Joining an old group shouldn't flood the feed | Open |
| FEED-6 | Item edits that change your share | Price/qty changed, and you claim a row with that item's name | Log row ids (a DB change) | Events store a name snapshot only | Open |
| FEED-7 | Share in lines | The current share from `computeShares`; left out in even mode before fully claimed, or when the read fails | The share at the time of the event | User asked for the current share only | Open (money: needs your look) |
| FEED-8 | A sender undoing their own payment to you | Not shown | Add `payer_id` to `unmarked_paid` (session 96's function, a DB change) | The event doesn't say who the payment was to | Open |
| FEED-9 | Group news | Shown with the expense amount; lights the Groups dot, not the Activity badge. Payments between others are never shown | Count it in the Activity badge | It's news, not about you | Open (privacy: needs your look) |
| FEED-10 | Milestones | Derived from loaded rows and current state; never counted | Store them | U11: no new stored state | Open |
| FEED-11 | "You were removed" lines | Dropped (deferred) | A notices table | Would need a stored record (U11) | Open |
| FEED-12 | Roll-up window | 10 min, consecutive lines on the same place | 5 or 30 min | Covers a table claiming together; starting value | Open |
| FEED-13 | Day headers | Today / Yesterday / weekday / "Nov 18", with the year only when it isn't this year | Always the date | Matches the user's wording | Open |
| FEED-14 | Page size | 30 lines, up to 3 fetch rounds per page; "That's everything." at the end | Infinite rounds | Bounds the reads per page | Open |
| FEED-15 | Home heading / tab | Heading stays "SplitIt"; the tab is "Tabs" with a receipt icon | Heading "Tabs" | Keeps the wordmark; tests use it as the ready signal | Open |
| FEED-16 | Refresh | No Realtime on Activity. Tabs badge on Home load; Activity badge and Groups dot on tab change, foreground and after seen writes | Realtime channels | Fewer moving parts | Open |
| FEED-17 | Groups dot rule | Others' group events except renames and link rotations, since you opened the group on this device (or since you joined) | Any event | U2 says renames and links never count | Open |
| FEED-18 | Hidden groups | Left out of the feed and the dot | Shown | "Hide for me" | Open |
| FEED-19 | The (+) button | A tab-bar button that pushes `/scan` (not a route), on web too. Supersedes HOME-ADD-1 | A dummy route | Avoids a fake screen | Open |
| FEED-20 | Groups-row hues (user U10) | Claim → `warningSurface`. Settled → `surfaceMuted` plus a check glyph. Tinted rows get a content-opacity-0.7 pressed state, so "settled" doesn't look stuck pressed. Reverses GCS-7 | Design review's alternative: a plain settled row with only the check caption | User asked for a light grey; the review found `surfaceMuted` equals the pressed colour | Open (needs your look) |
| FEED-21 | When Activity counts as seen | After its first page loads on each focus | On tab press | A failed load shouldn't clear unseen items | Open |
| FEED-22 | Payment from you, recorded by the other person | "Ana marked $40.00 from you as received" | "Ana got $40.00 from you" | Uses the "marked" wording the user set for payments | Open (copy: needs your look) |
| FEED-23 | Ordering ties | `created_at`, then id (stable, but not insertion order) | A `seq` column | U11: no new columns | Open |
| FEED-24 | Chip empty states | "No money activity yet." / "No group activity yet." Money includes share changes and settled milestones | The All empty text everywhere | The All text talks about receipts and payments | Open |
| FEED-25 | Profile links from Settle and VenmoPrompt | Must return you to where you were; a stack copy of Profile is the fallback | Switch tabs and lose Settle | Don't lose the Settle context | Open |
| FEED-26 | Tabs badge scope | The bills Home lists (`my_bills`, at most 20) | A count function | Same data as the sections (U2); a count would be a DB change | Open |
| FEED-27 | Migrations | None. Earlier "no DB changes at all" wording replaced by the user's clarification: no extra seen fields or tracking state; a migration only if genuinely needed for correctness or privacy | `group_seen`, `activity_seen`, notices, `seq`, `my_activity` | User, 2026-11-28 | Open |
| FEED-28 | When a bill counts as opened | A device-local time, written where `markBillSeen` runs | Read `bill_seen.seen_at` (no client grant; `my_bills` returns only a boolean) | Can't read the server time without a DB change | Open (needs your look) |
| FEED-29 | First run on a device | Items older than 14 days count as seen | Everything unread | A new phone shouldn't start at "9+" | Open |
| FEED-30 | Seen storage | Per device and per user (`splitit.feedSeen.<userId>`), timestamps only; kept across sign-out, lost on reinstall | Server tables | U11 | Open (needs your look) |
| FEED-31 | Marker colours (U12) | Colour only while unread: amber for needs-you, green for money, light orange (new `disputeSurface`/`dispute`) for disputes; neutral for everything else and every read line; 8-pt `primary` unread dot | Red for disputes | User: "give it a light orange"; red stays for app errors | Open |
| FEED-32 | Roll-up glyph | `users-round` | None | The design review listed no glyph for roll-ups | Open |
| FEED-33 | Claims for the Activity badge (F-h, F-i, F-k) | `fetchBadgeInputs` loads `fetchSettleData` only for bills with an `amounts_changed`, `extras_split_changed` or `item_changed` event by someone else in the unread window (batches of 20); the feed loads it for every bill on a page. Nothing is left out | Load claims for every bill on every badge refresh; or leave those kinds out of the badge | Same answer as the feed with far fewer reads (Task 2, made by the implementer) | Open |
| FEED-34 | The 14-day floor after a first run | Applies always: anything older than 14 days counts as seen, even with an older stored Activity time | Only on first run (no stored Activity time) | The badge reads are bounded by max(floor, Activity seen) (Contract S), so the feed's unread dots would otherwise disagree with the badge after 14 days away (Task 2, implementer) | Open |
| FEED-35 | Reads the feed adds to Contract A | Every event, places, names and payments read asks for `count: 'exact'` and fails closed on a cut; `bill_participants` pages are ordered by `bill_id`; bill reads add `item_added` (a fully-claimed trigger only); group reads use every kind the Groups dot counts (all but `renamed` / `link_rotated`) | Contract A's literal selects | Lessons 2026-11-25 (max_rows), A1.1, and the Task 1 hand-off; range paging needs an order to be stable (Task 2, implementer) | Open |
| FEED-36 | Pruning seen times | On each write, using the newest places list `loadFeedPlaces` saw; a time written after that list was read is kept | Prune only when the places list is passed in | Keeps the store small without dropping a bill joined after the list was read (Task 2, implementer) | Open |
| FEED-37 | A failed facts read on a feed page | Claims, payments or names failing fails the page (`network`, "Couldn't load more" + Try again), with the state kept for the retry. A failed group snapshot only drops its "settled up" milestone | Show the page without them | A missing claims read would silently drop share lines past the cursor for the whole session (Task 2, implementer) | Open |
| FEED-38 | Activity: when the dots come from a failed seen read | No dots at all (every line reads as seen) | Treat it as a first run (everything from the last 14 days marked new) | A storage hiccup shouldn't light up the whole feed; the next visit reads it again (Task 8, product-engineer) | Open |
| FEED-39 | Activity: coming back to the app (foreground) | A refresh within the same visit: the list is re-read, the dots keep this visit's seen read, and Activity isn't marked seen a second time | Treat it as a new visit (re-read seen, mark again) | Criterion 47 says once per focus; returning from the background isn't a new focus. Switching tabs or coming Back from a bill is (Task 8, product-engineer) | Open |
| FEED-40 | Activity: a refresh's size | A refresh (focus, foreground, pull) starts from page 1 but asks for as many lines as are already loaded (at least 30) | Always one page of 30 | Coming Back from a bill opened deep in the list keeps the list and its scroll position (criterion 33) while still replacing the pages from page 1 (criterion 45) (Task 8, product-engineer) | Open |
| FEED-41 | Activity: chip and line words the spec leaves open | Chip group label "Filter activity"; line hints "Opens the bill" / "Opens the group" (`FEED_SCREEN_A11Y` in `lib/feedText.ts`). Hints don't reach web; the line's label already names the place | Other wording | Short and plain (Task 8, product-engineer) | Open (copy: needs your look) |
| FEED-42 | Activity: the chips' look | The existing `SegmentedControl` (radio mode): a muted track with the selected chip solid teal | Separate pill chips with a light tint | The spec says to reuse it, and it already has the web radio keyboard pattern and 200% wrapping. It's the strongest colour on the screen, so it may read louder than "light hues only" (Task 8, product-engineer) | Open (needs your look) |
| FEED-43 | Activity: a roll-up's children | Under the line: the "Show {n}" toggle, then the children in small muted text below it, so the toggle never moves when they open | Children above the toggle; children as full lines | Focus stays on the toggle (criterion 49), and the children read as details of the one line (Task 8, product-engineer) | Open |
| FEED-44 | Activity: a tip, tax or fees edit's wording (A3.2) | "the total" is named only when it's the only changed field: "Maya changed the tip. Your share is now $5.00" (`fieldsText` in `lib/feedText.ts`) | "Maya changed the tip and the total. Your share is now $5.00" (C10 as first written) | `update_bill` lists `total_cents` with every tip, tax or fees change, so naming it said one edit twice; the share clause already gives what it means for you. Decided by the main session after the Task 11 live check (review fix round) | Open (copy: needs your look) |
| FEED-45 | Web: tab switches and browser history | A tab switch on web replaces the page without adding a browser history entry, like a phone app: the browser's Back leaves the tabs rather than stepping back through them (as QA found it) | Each tab switch adds a history entry, so browser Back returns to the previous tab | App-style, the same as the phones; a stack of tab visits makes Back feel stuck. Easy to change if you'd rather have history (QA keyboard fix round, product-engineer) | Open (your call) |
| FEED-46 | Activity: the roll-up "Show N" toggle's colour | Stays a teal `TextLink` | Muted grey text like the Home section toggles | The no-coloured-text rule allows colour on interactive controls, and teal is how the app marks a tappable word (QA keyboard fix round) | Open |
| FEED-47 | Tab bar at very narrow widths (criterion 52) | Below 300 px wide (`TAB_BAR_COMPACT_BELOW`, `tabBarLayout` in `lib/tabBar.ts`) the bar goes compact: icons only, with the screen-reader labels unchanged; a count badge is drawn as the plain dot (the label still says the number); the (+) shrinks from 56 to 44 pt (still a full touch target) in a slot of its own width, so the four tabs share the rest. Nothing overlaps from 160 px up. 300 is below every phone (320 and up), so phones always get the full bar; it catches a 375 px window at 150% zoom (250) and 200% zoom (188) | Shrink the labels; keep the labels and drop only the (+) rise; a two-line bar | Cut-off labels ("Gr…") help no one; icons alone are the usual narrow-bar pattern, and the label stays for screen readers. At 160 px the tab slots are about 29 px wide, under the 44 pt target: unavoidable at that width (QA keyboard fix round, product-engineer) | Open (needs your look at 200% zoom) |

**User visual review (2026-11-28):** the user looked at :8081 and approved the visuals as built: FEED-42 (solid teal selected chip kept), the teal badge on the selected teal tab, FEED-20 (grey tinted "Settled up" row kept), GCS-14 / group-claim-status criterion 33 (the existing light-mode warningSurface tints are visible enough), and the GCS-16 per-receipt cards. These rows are resolved as built.

**User decisions (2026-11-27/28), recorded by Task 12:** these are the user's own, not "build without blocking" choices, so they aren't open. Details in the spec (U1, U11, U12, A1.4) and [ADR 0025](learning/decisions/0025-tab-bar-and-activity.md).
- **"Tabs" is the first tab's label.** Chosen on 2026-11-27 over "Bills", "Splits" and "Home" (A1.4). The heading on the screen stays `SplitIt` (FEED-15).
- **Five tabs:** Tabs · Groups · (+) · Activity · Profile, with (+) as a raised button in the middle (U1).
- **No database changes for seen state** (U11). No `group_seen` or `activity_seen` tables and no other per-user tracking state in the database; "seen" lives on the device. A migration is still allowed when it's truly needed for correctness or privacy (FEED-27).
- **Light hues, colour only while unread** (U12, FEED-31). Feed markers are amber, green or light orange only until you've seen the line, then grey; a dispute is light orange, not red. Group rows get a small hue only for "claim" and "settled" (U10, FEED-20).
- **Status changes made with this note:** HOME-ADD-1 → Changed (superseded by the (+) tab button, FEED-19). GCS-7 → Changed (user, 2026-11-28, U10, FEED-20). HOME-ADD-2 stays Open.

## 2026-11-28 · Usernames groundwork (spec: [docs/specs/2026-11-28-usernames.md](specs/2026-11-28-usernames.md))
Decided by the product-manager. The user's rules (characters, length 3–20, case-insensitive uniqueness, required, editable) and the coordinator's (no backfill, `handle_new_user` unchanged, reads like `display_name`) aren't repeated here.

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UN-1 | How a username is written | Only through a `set_username` security-definer RPC; no client UPDATE grant on the column | Column UPDATE grant plus the check constraint | Gives separate "taken", "reserved" and "invalid" errors without parsing error text, and does nothing when the name hasn't changed | Open |
| UN-2 | Where the rule lives in SQL | Two named inline CHECK constraints (format, reserved); the RPC maps them by name. A Jest test checks they match `lib/username.ts` | A private validation function | One SQL definition, and no EXECUTE grants for a function called from a CHECK | Open |
| UN-3 | Lowercasing | Only the app lowercases, trims and strips `@`; the database rejects uppercase | The database lowercases too | Postgres `lower()` depends on the locale and could accept names the app rejects | Open |
| UN-4 | Dot and underscore rules | A dot can't come first, last or twice in a row; underscores anywhere; no letter required | Require a letter; no leading or trailing underscore | Common handle rules, and one regex works the same in JS and SQL | Open (needs your look: `123` and `___` are allowed) |
| UN-5 | Reserved words | Exact match on a 44-word starting list (spec contract V) | Substring or look-alike blocking | Small and predictable; a broader review is in before-production | Open (needs your look) |
| UN-7 | Clearing a username | Not possible once set | Allow clearing back to empty | You said it's required | Open |
| UN-8 | Reading your own username in the app | A new `getMyUsername`; `getMyProfile` unchanged | Add it to `getMyProfile` | Avoids touching the profile screen and its test, which the tab-bar session is reworking | Open |
| UN-9 | Save time limit | 15 s, then "Couldn't save" (starting value) | No limit | A hung save would trap people on the later required step; retrying the same name is harmless | Open |
| UN-10 | Activity for a rename | None | Log an event | Bills use the account id, and names are always read live | Open |
| UN-11 | Error copy | The spec's copy table ("That username is taken. Try another one." and so on) | Shorter or merged messages | One clear message per problem | Open (copy: needs your look) |
| UN-12 | A number suffix on an all-underscore base | `withNumberSuffix('___', 9999)` gives `9999`: valid, but the base is dropped (QA edge note) | Keep at least one base character; refuse bases with no letter (ties to Q2) | Only bases made of `_` hit it, and `suggestUsername` never produces one. Decide in the later gate's spec | Open |
| UN-13 | Username required | **Mandatory** (user, 2026-11-28). Every profile gets one at sign-up from the Google name (`yash_saraiya`, with a number if taken, `user…` if the name has no usable letters), existing profiles are backfilled, and the column is `not null`. People can change it later (editor still to build). Spec Amendment A1 | Optional until a pick-your-username step (the spec body, UN-6) | The user's call. Assigning it automatically keeps sign-up from ever failing or waiting on a screen | Decided by the user |
| UN-14 | What an auto-assigned username reveals | Built from the Google display name (`yash_saraiya`), visible to the same people who already see that name (co-participants, co-members) | A random handle (`user4821`) until the person picks one | Readable handles help tell two "Yash Saraiya"s apart and reveal nothing beyond the name already shown. If you'd prefer random, it's one line in `private.assign_username` | Open |
| UN-15 | Can a username change? | **Never** (user, 2026-11-28: "enforce you cant change username - unique id"). Assigned at sign-up, then fixed for every role; `set_username` dropped. Spec Amendment A2 | Editable with a rename policy (the earlier plan) | The user's call: the username is the account's stable id. Trade-off: people keep the auto-assigned handle (e.g. `user1635` for a name with no usable letters) | Decided by the user |
| UN-16 | A deleted account's username | Becomes free again when the account (and its profile) is deleted | Retire it forever (a `retired_usernames` list) | Not built yet; with permanent usernames, reuse could let a new account look like an old one to people who knew it | Open (A2.7) |
| UN-17 | Pick your username once? | **Pick once, then locked** (user, relayed by splitit-app-52: "they get to pick - but we verify its unique and add 3/4 random number after it, if not unique as suggested usernames"). Sign-up assigns a provisional name; the person picks once (the provisional one counts), then it never changes. Taken names get 3–4-digit suggestions checked as free. Spec Amendment A3 | The auto value is final | The user's call | Decided by the user |
| UN-18 | Can the pick step be skipped? | **No, it can't be skipped** (user, 2026-11-28). Required on first launch, with "Keep @<provisional>" as a one-tap choice | Skippable, name stays provisional until picked later | The user's call | Decided by the user |
| UN-19 | Username on Profile | Read-only row "Username @yash_saraiya"; while provisional it adds a "Choose" link to the pick step | Show only once locked | The user asked to see it on Profile (relayed by splitit-app-52); the link gives a way back to the pick step if it's skippable (UN-18) | Open (copy) |
| UN-20 | Lock timeout on the username-required backfill (review R8) | **None** on `20261128100019_username_required.sql` (user, 2026-11-28: "we dont need it"); the later lock migration keeps its 5 s limit | Add `set local lock_timeout = '5s'` | The user's call; push at a quiet time (before-production step 5) | Decided by the user |
| UN-21 | Backfill on the hosted project | Not needed (user, 2026-11-28: no production users besides the user; clearing data is fine). The migration keeps its backfill step, which does nothing on empty data, so the file stays as applied | Remove the backfill from the migration | Editing an applied migration buys nothing; on cleared data it's a no-op | Decided by the user |
| UN-22 | Buttons on "Choose your username" | One primary button that follows the field ("Keep @ana" while unchanged, "Use @ana_s" once edited, with a quiet "Keep @ana" link under it); Sign out as a ghost button; no Skip | Two fixed buttons (Keep and Save) | One clear action on screen; Keep stays one tap away | Open (copy: needs your look) |

## 2026-11-27 · QR popup (`components/QrSheet.tsx`; user request "show qr code should create a popup with the qr code - make it easier")
Made by the product-engineer. Supersedes IQ-1's fold-out and IQ-2's "Hide QR code" toggle, and IQ-6's fixed 200-pt size. IQ-3, IQ-5, IQ-7 (image label), IQ-8 (library), IQ-9 are unchanged.

| # | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| QS-1 | Presentation | iOS: system page sheet (swipe down closes). Android/web: bottom sheet over a scrim (tap outside, Android back, web Escape close). Same as `ItemSheet` | Centered dialog on Android/web | The user allowed either; matching ItemSheet keeps one sheet look and makes the later `components/ui/Sheet.tsx` (splitit-app-96) a straight move | Open |
| QS-2 | Trigger | "Show QR code" stays, always that label (no "Hide" state, no `expanded`); hint "Opens a QR code friends can scan to open the invite link" | Keep the toggle | It's a popup now; Done closes it | Open |
| QS-3 | Title | Bill: "Scan to join this bill". Group: "Scan to join <group name>", "Scan to join this group" if the name is missing. From `lib/inviteQr.ts` `qrSheetTitle`; the group screen passes `groupName` (new optional prop on `GroupInvite`). The share message still has no group name (GR-28) | No title | The title tells the friend scanning what they're joining; the group name is member-set data, not URL text | Open |
| QS-4 | QR size | As big as fits: `qrTileLayout(window)` in `lib/inviteQr.ts`, 160–300 pt, shrinking on short (landscape) windows so Done stays near. Quiet zone = 4/25 of the size (4 modules of the smallest 25-module code), so it's always ≥ 4 modules | Fixed 200 pt (IQ-6) | Bigger modules scan from further away and in dim light. About 270 pt on a 390-pt phone | Open |
| QS-5 | Copy link in the popup | Yes, with its own "Link copied" line (cleared when the popup closes or the link changes) | Only in the panel | The user listed it; someone showing the code may also want to paste it. It is the same action as the panel's button, in a second place, by request | Open |
| QS-6 | Primary action | "Done" (primary, full width) at the bottom; "Copy link" secondary above it | Done as a secondary/ghost button | The user asked for a clear Done; nothing else on the sheet is primary | Open |
| QS-7 | Focus | Title is a header and gets screen-reader focus on show (native). On close, focus returns to "Show QR code": iOS in `onDismiss` (after the slide-out), Android when it closes, web by React Native Web's Modal focus trap. Needed a `ref` prop on `TextLink` (React 19 ref-as-prop) | No focus handling | The user asked for it; without it VoiceOver lands at the top of the screen | Open |
| QS-8 | Screen brightness | Not raised | `expo-brightness` | Needs a native module; the user said skip | Closed |

## 2026-11-28 · Delete group (spec: [docs/specs/2026-11-28-delete-group.md](specs/2026-11-28-delete-group.md))
Decided by the product-manager. The user's binding rules aren't repeated here: any member may delete, only once everyone is settled up; it's deleted for everyone; the confirm is a popup. **Exception logged:** the group screen's "inline confirms, no modal" rule is broken on purpose for this one confirm, at the user's request (DG-1). It's a small in-app dialog, not `Alert.alert`, so it works on web too.

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DG-1 | Confirm style | Centred in-app dialog (`components/ConfirmDialog.tsx`) | Inline card like Leave; `Alert.alert` | The user asked for a popup; `Alert.alert` doesn't work on web | User's decision (exception to the inline-confirm rule) |
| DG-2 | Soft or hard delete | Soft: `groups.deleted_at` / `deleted_by`, hidden everywhere, writes refused, restorable by SQL for 30 days, no restore UI | Hard delete now | The DB can't verify "settled up" (balances are lib-only, ADR 0022 §4), so a mistaken or malicious delete must be reversible. Hard delete also needs an append-only exception, the FK order and a receipts decision now | **Open (needs your answer, Q1)** |
| DG-3 | 30-day purge | Not built; before-production item (`pg_cron` or manual SQL) | Build a `pg_cron` job now | Nothing is lost by waiting; the purge needs the receipts decision | **Open (Q2)** |
| DG-4 | What `delete_group` checks | Caller is a member (42501), not already deleted, the screen's money fingerprint still matches (`group_changed`), no waiting receipt (`receipts_waiting`). No balance check | A SQL copy of the balance math | A balance check would be a second copy of C-G2 that could drift; the fingerprint proves the screen judged this exact money state | Open |
| DG-5 | Waiting receipts block deletion | Yes: "Every receipt has to be fully claimed before you can delete this group." | Allow when nets are settled | A waiting receipt counts as $0, so someone could be owed for it; cheap for the DB to check with the same rule (contract W) | **Open (Q4)** |
| DG-6 | "Settled" tolerance | Every member within 1¢ (`isSettledUp`, GR-3) | Exactly $0 | Same rule as "You're settled up" everywhere else | **Open (Q3, money)** |
| DG-7 | Home notice lifetime | `"<name>" was deleted.` stays until Dismiss or Home loses focus; no timer | Auto-hide after ~4 s | Timed messages are easy to miss with a screen reader | Open (Q6) |
| DG-8 | Popup wording about undo | "You can't undo this in the app." | "This can't be undone."; mention a 30-day restore | Accurate under soft delete without promising a support path | Open (Q7, copy) | **Superseded by DG-17/A2.8:** "This can't be undone."
| DG-9 | People links and profile visibility after delete | Unchanged: people stay in "People you've split with" and keep seeing each other's Venmo username | Drop links made only through that group | Smallest change; they did share a group | Open (Q5) | **Changed by DG-17 (hard delete), noted 2026-11-28 by the docs task:** the member rows and the group's receipts are deleted, so links made only through that group are gone (`private.my_links()` reads those rows). Links from bills outside the group stay.
| DG-10 | A deleted group's invite link | Existing "This link doesn't work anymore. Ask for a new one." (`invalid_code`) | "This group was deleted" | No new state on the join screen; doesn't tell link holders anything about the group | Open |
| DG-11 | A deleted group's receipt opened by URL | The bill screen's existing no-access state; `lib/bills.ts` (another session's) untouched | New "its group was deleted" copy | Rare (only from an old link); avoids editing a shared file | Open |
| DG-12 | Own retry after a lost answer | Success, no change, when `deleted_by` is the caller | Always `group_deleted` | Makes a timed-out delete safe to retry (lesson 2026-11-27 "outcome unknown") | Open |
| DG-13 | Lock on the group row in `delete_group` | `FOR UPDATE`, then the group's bills `FOR UPDATE` ascending | `FOR NO KEY UPDATE` like leave_group | Waits for in-flight group writes so the fingerprint sees them; a `group_events` trigger refuses writes that land after | Open (engineering) |
| DG-14 | Event or feed line for a delete | None | A `group_deleted` group event | The user turned down a similar "you were removed" line; the deleted state on the screen says it | Open |
| DG-15 | Disabled Delete row | Shown, disabled, with the reason as visible text (waiting reason first) | Hide the row until deletable | The user's proposed design; tells people what's left to do | User's design (kept) |
| DG-16 | Popup counts | "It will disappear for all 4 members, with its 3 expenses, 2 receipts and payment history." (1 member: "for you"; 2: "both members"; empty parts left out) | Always all three counts | Avoids "0 expenses" and "all 1 members" | Open (copy) |
| DG-17 | Soft or hard delete | **Hard delete** (user, 2026-11-28: "no soft delete … just hard delete since we show confirm popup"). The group, its expenses, payments, events and its receipts are removed in one transaction; nothing to restore. Spec Amendment A2 | Soft delete with a 30-day restore (the earlier default) | The user's call: the confirm popup is the safeguard. Risk kept in before-production: the database can't verify "settled up" and a delete can't be undone | Decided by the user |
| DG-18 | A clash between two writes (deadlock / lock timeout) | New error `busy`: "That didn't go through. Please try again.", control released, no auto-retry (spec A3) | Keep reading it as `network` ("Check your connection…") | The user asked for a friendly retry message; the connection wasn't the problem | Decided by the user |
| DG-19 | "payment history" in the delete popup | Shown when the group has live payments or recent "Not received" (undone) payments the snapshot carries (review R3) | Count every payment row, which needs a new count in the snapshot | An older undone payment can still be deleted without the popup naming it; the popup is a summary, and the money is already settled. Revisit if it matters | Open |
| DG-T4-1 | Popup body wording (A2.8) for every count | `Its 3 expenses, 2 receipts and payment history will be removed for all 4 members. This can't be undone.` Empty parts are left out; with none: `It will be removed for you. This can't be undone.` Who: 1 → `you`, 2 → `both members`, 3+ → `all n members`. `deleteConfirmText(snapshot)` takes no viewer id (nothing in it depends on the viewer) | Always all three counts ("0 expenses") | A2.8 gave one example; this keeps DG-16's rules for 1 member, 2 members and empty parts. **Copy: needs your look** | Open |
| DG-T4-2 | When a group screen shows "This group was deleted" | `useGroupSnapshot` gives `{ status: 'deleted' }` when a refetch answers `not_allowed` after the group was shown (also after a failed refetch in between). It stays `deleted` after a later network / busy refetch. A first load that answers `not_allowed` stays `not_allowed` (the not-a-member copy) | Show it on a first load too | After a hard delete the database can't tell "deleted" from "not a member" (A2.9). Only a screen that already showed the group knows it was there. A member who left the group on another device also sees "deleted" here | Open |
| DG-T4-3 | `busy` on group reads | `busy` for every group function, reads included (`group_snapshot`, `my_groups`, the table reads), checked before every other mapping, and for a thrown error carrying the code | Writes only, as `lib/bills.ts` does | A3.1 says "reads included". On a read the refetch keeps what's on screen, as with `network` | Open |
| DG-T4-4 | What a refused delete does (`deleteRefusal` in lib/groupScreenText.ts, for T5) | `group_changed` / `receipts_waiting`: the dialog closes, C6 / C7 under the options card, refetch. `not_allowed`: the group is gone, so C8 (A2.11). `busy`: the dialog stays open with the busy copy. `network` and anything unexpected: it stays open with C5 | Let the screen decide | Screens build no strings. One table for T5's dialog | Open |
| DG-T4-5 | Group sub-screens before T5/T6 | `deleted` shows each screen's existing not-a-member state, which is what those screens showed for this answer before. T5/T6 replace it with C8 | Leave the type error to T5/T6 | Keeps the type-check green with no change in behaviour | Temporary (T5/T6) |
| DG-T5-1 | The confirm dialog's layout (`components/ConfirmDialog.tsx`) | A centred card, at most 360 pt wide, over the scrim: the title (22 pt bold), the body in full text colour, then two full-width buttons stacked: `Delete group` (tonal red) above `Cancel` (text button). C5 / the busy line goes under the buttons. Fades in; the card scrolls at large text sizes and 200% zoom, and keeps clear of the notch | Buttons side by side (Cancel left, Delete right, as iOS alerts) | Stacked buttons never squeeze or wrap at large text, and both are in thumb reach. **Look: needs your eye in light and dark (user check 58)** | Open |
| DG-T5-2 | Tapping outside the dialog | Cancels (not while deleting). The scrim is hidden from screen readers and isn't a Tab stop on web, so there is one `Cancel`; Escape and Android back also cancel | Make the scrim a second labelled "Cancel" button (as the QR sheet's "Close") | Two buttons with the same name in a two-button dialog is confusing for screen-reader users | Open |
| DG-T5-3 | C7 after the refetch | `Every receipt has to be fully claimed…` shows under the options card only while the Delete row doesn't already show the same words as its caption, so once the refetch shows the waiting receipt it appears once (as the caption) | Show both | Saying the same sentence twice in one card is clutter | Open |
| DG-T5-4 | Other writes on the group screen answered `not_allowed` | They now also refetch (before, only "I wasn't part of this" refetched on a refusal), so a group deleted by someone else switches to C8 after a Rename / Hide / Leave tap (criterion 47). A write whose call throws reads as a network failure instead of leaving the control stuck | Leave the other writes as they were | A2.9: `not_allowed` is now also how a deleted group answers. The refusal's words still show first | Open |
| DG-T6-1 | A1.1: a feed line of a deleted group | The group screen shows C8 for a first-load refusal when it was opened with `?from=feed` (allow-listed; never shown). **Needs splitit-app-52's one-line change** in `app/(app)/(tabs)/activity.tsx`: open group lines as `/groups/<id>?from=feed`. Until then such a tap shows the not-a-member words | (a) C8 for every first-load refusal; (b) one neutral sentence for both cases (a `lib/` copy change) | A feed line proves the viewer was a member when it loaded, so "deleted" is the honest reading there; (a) would tell someone who was never in a group that it was deleted. Known gap: someone who left on another device and taps a stale line also sees C8 | Open (waiting on 52) |
| DG-T6-2 | Home notice placement and look | Directly under the `SplitIt` wordmark, above the other notices: a green check line `"Tahoe trip" was deleted.` with a `Dismiss` link on the right. Taken each time Home comes into focus, not only on mount, because Home stays mounted under the group screen | A card | A one-line result reads as "done", not as something to act on; a card would compete with the bill notices | Open |
| DG-T6-3 | The top bar in the deleted state | Each sub-screen keeps its own bar (Back). Back from Settle, Who paid what or an expense goes to the group, which shows C8 too | Hide Back so only `Go home` is left | Keeps the bar the same in every state of the screen; nothing breaks either way | Open |

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GR-T17-16 | When the bill screen knows the bill's group | `lib/useBillGroup` reads `fetchBillGroup` on every focus (the mount included) and on Try again, not on the bill's live refetches. The bill screen, Settle and Add people show their spinner until the first answer | Read it on every bill refetch; add `group_id` to `fetchBill` | The group changes only on the host's "Add to a group" / "Take out" screen, so returning is when it changes. Reading on every refetch adds a `bills` query per live hint; changing `BillData` touches every bill fixture in the repo | Open |
| GR-T17-17 | While the bill's group is unknown | **Resolved by the main session (money safety, option 1):** until a group read succeeds, no screen shows Pay / I paid / Request / payer marks / Leave / the invite card. A failed read shows "Couldn't check this bill's group. Try again." (alert; "Checking this bill's group…" while it reads) in their place on the bill footer, Settle up and Add people; claiming stays. A write refused with `in_group` (`useVenmoSend`'s new `onRefused`, Settle's not-received and pay-back writes, Leave) forgets the last answer (`useBillGroup.recheck`) and reads again; once known it says "This bill is now in Tahoe trip. Settle up in the group." (after Venmo opened: "Venmo opened, but this bill is now in Tahoe trip. Don't send it. Settle up in the group."), with "Settled in Tahoe trip" / "Open Tahoe trip" as the link. Bill test fixtures carry `group_id: null` | Show the bill as a bill in no group (the first build) | On web Pay opens Venmo before the database refuses the mark | Resolved |
| GR-T17-18 | "Invite people to Tahoe trip" on a group bill | A quiet link (user-plus icon) in the invite card's place that opens the group with its invite panel (`/groups/<id>?invite=1`), for every member | Render `GroupInvite` inline on the bill (needs the group snapshot for the join code) | One place for the group's invite (rule: every action has one place); no snapshot read on the bill screen | Open |
| GR-T17-19 | The footer and Settle on a group bill | Footer: the shares summary stays, then one link line "Settled in Tahoe trip" (hint "Opens the group to see balances and settle up") in place of Pay / I paid / Settle up; the "Couldn't refresh" line still shows after a failed refetch. Settle up: the title and one card "Settled in Tahoe trip", "This bill counts in the group's balances. Payments for it are made in the group.", primary "Open Tahoe trip". **Copy: needs your look** | A notice above the normal Settle content | Nothing on either screen can start a bill-level payment (criterion 80) | Open |
| GR-T17-20 | Add people reached by a link on a group bill | "Everyone in Tahoe trip is on this bill. To add someone, invite them to the group." with primary "Open Tahoe trip" and "Back to the bill"; no Add, no Remove. **Copy: needs your look** | Leave the screen as is (the database refuses with `in_group`) | Criterion 111 ("Remove absent"); the bill screen has no Add people on a group bill, but a link can still reach this route | Open |
| GR-T17-21 | `in_group` mapping | Mapped from `join_bill`, `add_participant`, `remove_participant`, `leave_bill`, `mark_paid`, `settle_difference` and `mark_not_received` (the T5 triggers and `add_participant`). `set_claim`, `unmark_paid` and `set_payer` never raise it, so it stays `network` there. Settle's `actionError` / `afterOpenRefusal` keep their generic copy for it ("Couldn't save…", "Venmo opened, but this wasn't saved. Don't send it.") | The join copy everywhere | "Ask for the group's link" only makes sense on the invite link | Open |
| GR-T17-22 | A refused Try again on the failed attach | The line under the title shows the refusal's fixed copy (`attachErrorText`, e.g. "Everyone on the bill has to be in the group first."); a network failure keeps "Couldn't add it to Tahoe trip. Try again.". Try again is busy until the group re-read after a successful attach ends; if that re-read fails it is enabled again (a second attach to the same group is a no-op success) | The same line for every failure | Says what to fix when retrying can't help | Open |

| GR-T18-13 | Who the chips list | The bill's participants. Settled rows still draw no status (A1.S3). **Section toggles** are quieter: the Show / Hide word and chevron in muted grey at the regular weight (`DisclosureRow quiet`), so teal on Home means "your next step" | Keep the pills; summary tiles; cap Owed to you with "See all"; merge the two waiting sections into "Needs you" | The user's call (above); words without capsules remove a coloured shape per row and keep status said in words, not colour alone | Open |

Home only (`HOME_STATUS_COPY` in lib/homeSections.ts); `billMarkerView` and the other screens are unchanged. ### 2026-11-28 · Appearance: Auto / Light / Dark (user request in chat)
User's words: "when a new user signs in and ask to enter their venmo can we also have them choose between light and dark mode / auto and keep that in profile as well as a setting".

| ID | Decision | Picked | Alternatives | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| APPEAR-1 | Where the choice is stored | **On this device only**: one lib/storage key, `splitit.appearance` (SecureStore on phones, localStorage on web). No database column, no migration. Kept across sign-out, since it belongs to the device, not the person. A new phone or browser starts on Auto | A `profiles` column that follows the person to every device | The user doesn't want tracking fields in the DB; a look setting is per-screen anyway | Open |
| APPEAR-2 | The default | **Auto** (follow the phone's or browser's setting), labelled `Auto` / `Light` / `Dark` | Light by default | Matches what people already chose for their device; nothing changes for anyone who never touches it | Open |
| APPEAR-3 | Where it sits on Profile | Its own card titled `Appearance`, right under the Venmo card (the two settings together), above "People you've split with". A 3-way radio chooser filling the card, with `Auto follows your phone's setting.` under it. Applies at once: no Save button and no "Saved" line. Shown even while the profile is loading or failed to load (it doesn't depend on it) | Below People, next to Sign out; a row that opens a picker | Settings together, near the top; one tap, no extra screen | Open |
| APPEAR-4 | On the "Add your Venmo" card | A quiet second row: `Appearance` on the left, a compact `Auto / Light / Dark` chooser on the right. It applies and saves at once and is **not** an answer to the prompt: the card still goes away only when Venmo is saved or on "Not now", and once it's gone the choice lives on Profile only | No chooser on the card (Profile only); a separate one-time card | The user asked for it at the Venmo step; keeping it off the card's show/hide rule means it can't keep the card alive | Open |
| APPEAR-5 | Native first frames | On phones the stored choice is read from SecureStore at launch, so the first frames (the loading spinner) can show in the phone's scheme for a moment before switching. Web has no flash: an inline script in `public/index.html` applies the choice before the bundle loads | Hold the splash screen until it's read | Small and brief; the splash-screen hold touches the startup path (not in this task) | Open: check on a device |
